package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.animations;

import java.util.Optional;

public record AnimationSequenceData(
    String sequenceId,
    Optional<String> preAnim,
    Optional<String> loopAnim,
    Optional<String> postAnim,
    boolean waitForCompletion
) {}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/animations/AnimationSequenceReloadListener.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.animations;

import com.google.gson.*;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.ResourceManager;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener;
import net.minecraft.util.GsonHelper;
import net.minecraft.util.profiling.ProfilerFiller;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RegisterClientReloadListenersEvent;

import java.util.*;

public final class AnimationSequenceReloadListener extends SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener {
  private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
  private static final Map<String, Map<String, AnimationSequenceData>> CACHE = new HashMap<>();
  private static final AnimationSequenceReloadListener INSTANCE = new AnimationSequenceReloadListener();

  private AnimationSequenceReloadListener() {
    super(GSON, "animation_sequences");
  }

  /**
   * Registers the asset reload listener safely onto the client distribution mod bus.
   */
  public static void onRegisterClientReloadListeners(RegisterClientReloadListenersEvent event) {
    event.registerReloadListener(INSTANCE);
  }

  /**
   * Resolves loaded animation sequence parameters matching targeted type structures.
   */
  public static AnimationSequenceData getSequence(String entityType, String sequenceId) {
    Map<String, AnimationSequenceData> sequences = CACHE.get(entityType);
    return sequences != null ? sequences.get(sequenceId) : null;
  }

  @Override
  protected void apply(Map<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> objects, ResourceManager manager, ProfilerFiller profiler) {
    CACHE.clear();

    for (Map.Entry<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> entry : objects.entrySet()) {
      ResourceLocation fileLocation = entry.getKey();
      JsonElement element = entry.getValue();

      JsonObject root = GsonHelper.convertToJsonObject(element, "animation_sequence");
      String entityType = GsonHelper.getAsString(root, "affected_entity");

      JsonObject seqsObj = GsonHelper.getAsJsonObject(root, "sequences");
      Map<String, AnimationSequenceData> sequenceMap = new HashMap<>();

      for (Map.Entry<String, JsonElement> seqEntry : seqsObj.entrySet()) {
        String seqId = seqEntry.getKey();
        JsonObject body = GsonHelper.convertToJsonObject(seqEntry.getValue(), seqId);

        Optional<String> pre = body.has("pre") ? Optional.of(GsonHelper.getAsString(body, "pre")) : Optional.empty();
        Optional<String> loop = body.has("loop") ? Optional.of(GsonHelper.getAsString(body, "loop")) : Optional.empty();
        Optional<String> post = body.has("post") ? Optional.of(GsonHelper.getAsString(body, "post")) : Optional.empty();
        boolean wait = GsonHelper.getAsBoolean(body, "wait_for_completion", false);

        sequenceMap.put(seqId, new AnimationSequenceData(seqId, pre, loop, post, wait));
      }

      // Index globally across multiple string mutations to ensure lookup hits
      CACHE.put(entityType, sequenceMap);
      CACHE.put(fileLocation.toString(), sequenceMap);
      CACHE.put(fileLocation.getPath(), sequenceMap);
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/animations/EntityAnimationStateMachine.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.animations;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.components.IEntityComponent;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.components.IProceduralAnimConfig;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animatable.GeoEntity;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.AnimationState;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.RawAnimation;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.PlayState;

import java.util.function.Supplier;

public final class EntityAnimationStateMachine implements IEntityComponent {
  private enum Phase { PRE, LOOP, POST }

  private final Supplier<Boolean> dialogueCondition;
  private Entity entity;
  
  private String currentSequenceId = "idle";
  private Phase currentPhase = Phase.LOOP;
  private String lastPlayedAnimName = "";
  private String entityTypeKey = null;
  private String networkSequenceOverride = null;

  // Universal procedural interval properties
  private int randomActionTimer = 0;
  private int nextRandomActionThreshold = -1;

  public EntityAnimationStateMachine(Supplier<Boolean> dialogueCondition) {
    this.dialogueCondition = dialogueCondition;
  }

  @Override
  public void onInit(Entity entity) {
    this.entity = entity;
  }

  @Override
  public void onTick() {
    // Modular tick updates live here independently
  }

  private String getEntityTypeKey() {
    if (this.entityTypeKey == null && this.entity != null) {
      this.entityTypeKey = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(this.entity.getType()).toString();
    }
    return this.entityTypeKey != null ? this.entityTypeKey : "hexacreatecore:cursed_blacksmith";
  }

  public <T extends GeoEntity> PlayState handleAnimationState(AnimationState<T> event) {
    boolean hasDialogue = this.dialogueCondition.get();
    
    // 1. Pure ECS Check: Universal Intercept of explicit server-driven network animation overrides!
    if (event.getController().getCurrentRawAnimation() != null) {
      String activeTrigger = event.getController().getCurrentRawAnimation().getAnimationStages().toString();
      
      if (activeTrigger.startsWith("override:")) {
        this.networkSequenceOverride = activeTrigger.substring(9);
      }
    }

    // Determine baseline target sequences based on runtime interactions
    String targetSequence = hasDialogue ? "talking" : "idle";
    
    if (hasDialogue && this.networkSequenceOverride != null) {
      targetSequence = this.networkSequenceOverride;
    }

    if (!hasDialogue) {
      this.networkSequenceOverride = null;
      targetSequence = "idle";
    }

    // 2. Structural boundary checking: handling transitions out of active sequences
    if (!this.currentSequenceId.equals(targetSequence)) {
      AnimationSequenceData currentData = AnimationSequenceReloadListener.getSequence(getEntityTypeKey(), this.currentSequenceId);
      
      boolean canTransition = true;
      if (currentData != null && currentData.waitForCompletion()) {
        if (this.currentPhase == Phase.PRE || this.currentPhase == Phase.POST) {
          canTransition = event.getController().hasAnimationFinished();
        }
      }

      if (canTransition) {
        if (currentData != null && currentData.postAnim().isPresent() && this.currentPhase != Phase.POST) {
          this.currentPhase = Phase.POST;
        } else {
          this.currentSequenceId = targetSequence;
          AnimationSequenceData nextData = AnimationSequenceReloadListener.getSequence(getEntityTypeKey(), this.currentSequenceId);
          this.currentPhase = (nextData != null && nextData.preAnim().isPresent()) ? Phase.PRE : Phase.LOOP;
          this.lastPlayedAnimName = ""; 
          event.getController().forceAnimationReset();
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Process output sequence phases to drive execution parameters
    AnimationSequenceData activeData = AnimationSequenceReloadListener.getSequence(getEntityTypeKey(), this.currentSequenceId);
    
    // Action bar debug text channel
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player != null) {
      String debugMsg = String.format("§b[Anim Debug] Seq: §e%s §7| Phase: §e%s §7| Data found: %s", 
          this.currentSequenceId, this.currentPhase, (activeData != null ? "§aYES" : "§cNO"));
      mc.player.displayClientMessage(Component.literal(debugMsg), true);
    }

    if (activeData == null) {
      return event.setAndContinue(RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop("idle"));
    }

    switch (this.currentPhase) {
      case PRE -> {
        String anim = activeData.preAnim().orElse("");
        boolean isCompleted = event.getController().hasAnimationFinished() || event.getAnimationTick() >= 20.0d;
        
        if (isCompleted && anim.equals(this.lastPlayedAnimName)) {
          this.currentPhase = Phase.LOOP;
          event.getController().forceAnimationReset();
          return PlayState.CONTINUE;
        }
        this.lastPlayedAnimName = anim;
        return event.setAndContinue(RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay(anim));
      }
      case LOOP -> {
        String anim = activeData.loopAnim().orElse("idle");
        this.lastPlayedAnimName = anim;
        return event.setAndContinue(RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop(anim));
      }
      case POST -> {
        String anim = activeData.postAnim().orElse("");
        boolean isCompleted = event.getController().hasAnimationFinished() || event.getAnimationTick() >= 20.0d;
        
        if (isCompleted && anim.equals(this.lastPlayedAnimName)) {
          this.currentPhase = Phase.LOOP;
          event.getController().forceAnimationReset();
          return PlayState.CONTINUE;
        }
        this.lastPlayedAnimName = anim;
        return event.setAndContinue(RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay(anim));
      }
    }

    return PlayState.CONTINUE;
  }

  /**
   * Processes layered multi-controller animations (Generic Actions & Continuous Ambience) 
   * completely independent of the base locomotion/dialogue sequences layer.
   */
  public <T extends GeoEntity> PlayState handleOverlayAnimationState(AnimationState<T> event) {
    // 1. Resolve configuration parameters using the abstract data layers
    IProceduralAnimConfig config = (this.entity instanceof IProceduralAnimConfig c) ? c : new IProceduralAnimConfig() {
      @Override public int getMinRandomInterval() { return 60; }
      @Override public int getMaxRandomInterval() { return 160; }
    };

    String ambientAnim = config.getContinuousAmbientAnim();
    String actionAnim = config.getRandomActionAnim();

    // 2. Instantiate threshold calculations using native entity seed random generation
    if (this.nextRandomActionThreshold == -1 && this.entity != null) {
      this.nextRandomActionThreshold = config.getNextRandomTicks(this.entity.getRandom());
    }

    // 3. Assemble base continuous ambient layer loop path
    RawAnimation overlayAnimStack = RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop(ambientAnim);

    this.randomActionTimer++;
    if (this.randomActionTimer >= this.nextRandomActionThreshold) {
      // Inject secondary high-priority random action clip on top of baseline loop stack parameters
      overlayAnimStack = RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay(actionAnim).thenLoop(ambientAnim);
      
      // Flush trackers and schedule subsequent dynamic threshold windows
      this.randomActionTimer = 0;
      if (this.entity != null) {
        this.nextRandomActionThreshold = config.getNextRandomTicks(this.entity.getRandom());
      }
    }

    return event.setAndContinue(overlayAnimStack);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/BossRushManager.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushData.CustomEffectScript;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushData.PhaseConfig;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushData.SequenceConfig;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.network.BossRushSyncPayload;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.DialogueAction;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.DifficultyManager;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.DifficultySavedData;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleOptions;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LightningBolt;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MobSpawnType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.boss.enderdragon.EnderDragon;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.boss.enderdragon.phases.EnderDragonPhase;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingDeathEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class BossRushManager {

  private static final class RushSession {
    final UUID playerUuid;
    final ResourceLocation dimension;
    final Vec3 originPos;
    final SequenceConfig sequence;
    int phaseIndex = 0;
    int bossIndexInPhase = 0;
    UUID currentBossUuid = null;
    final Set<UUID> allSpawnedEntities = new HashSet<>();

    RushSession(ServerPlayer player, SequenceConfig sequence) {
      this.playerUuid = player.getUUID();
      this.dimension = player.serverLevel().dimension().location();
      this.originPos = player.position();
      this.sequence = sequence;
    }
  }

  private static final Map<UUID, RushSession> ACTIVE_SESSIONS = new HashMap<>();

  private BossRushManager() {}

  public static boolean isPlayerInRush(UUID playerUuid) {
    return ACTIVE_SESSIONS.containsKey(playerUuid);
  }

  @DialogueAction(value = "hexacreatecore:start_boss_rush", selfTargeted = true)
  public static void startBossRushAction(ServerPlayer player, Entity proxy) {
    startBossRush(player);
  }

  public static boolean startBossRush(ServerPlayer player) {
    if (ACTIVE_SESSIONS.containsKey(player.getUUID())) {
      player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("§cA Boss Rush is already active!"));
      return false;
    }

    SequenceConfig seq = BossRushReloadListener.getSequence();
    if (seq == null || seq.phases().isEmpty()) {
      player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("§cNo Boss Rush sequence.json loaded!"));
      return false;
    }

    RushSession session = new RushSession(player, seq);
    ACTIVE_SESSIONS.put(player.getUUID(), session);

    beginPhase(player, session, 0, seq.onTriggerItem(), seq.onTriggerSound());
    return true;
  }


  private static void beginPhase(ServerPlayer player, RushSession session, int index, String totemItemId, String totemSoundId) {
    session.phaseIndex = index;
    session.bossIndexInPhase = 0;
    PhaseConfig phase = session.sequence.phases().get(index);

    playOriginEffect(player.serverLevel(), session.originPos, phase.effects().onStart());

    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new BossRushSyncPayload(
      true,
      phase.name(),
      phase.description(),
      phase.music(),
      phase.musicTitle(),
      totemItemId,
      totemSoundId,
      "",
      -1,
      BossRushReloadListener.getRawHealthBarsJson()
    ));

    spawnNextBossInPhase(player, session);
  }

  private static void spawnNextBossInPhase(ServerPlayer player, RushSession session) {
    PhaseConfig phase = session.sequence.phases().get(session.phaseIndex);

    if (session.bossIndexInPhase >= phase.bosses().size()) {
      playOriginEffect(player.serverLevel(), session.originPos, phase.effects().onEnd());

      int nextPhase = session.phaseIndex + 1;
      if (session.sequence.phases().size() > nextPhase) {
        beginPhase(player, session, nextPhase, phase.onCompletionItem(), phase.onCompletionSound());
      } else {
        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(player.getUUID());
        DifficultyTier activeTier = DifficultySavedData.get(player.serverLevel()).getActiveTier();
        if (activeTier == DifficultyTier.ABSOLUTION) {
          DifficultyManager.unlockGenesisForServer(player.serverLevel());
        }

        String completionMsg = switch (activeTier) {
          case ETHEREAL -> "This mortal realm have seen enough... Aren't you satisfied ?";
          case ABSOLUTION -> "Breaking through the world as they would, your path is one of peril...";
          case GENESIS -> "You've Done it... Now, what shall it be ?";
          default -> "Boss Rush Completed!";
        };

        PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new BossRushSyncPayload(
          false,
          "",
          "",
          session.sequence.endingMusic(),
          session.sequence.endingMusicTitle(),
          phase.onCompletionItem(),
          phase.onCompletionSound(),
          completionMsg,
          -1,
          "{}"
        ));
      }
      return;
    }

    String rawEntityId = phase.bosses().get(session.bossIndexInPhase);
    ResourceLocation entityLoc = ResourceLocation.tryParse(rawEntityId);
    if (entityLoc == null || !BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.containsKey(entityLoc)) {
      HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.warn("Boss Rush skipped unknown entity ID: {}", rawEntityId);
      session.bossIndexInPhase++;
      spawnNextBossInPhase(player, session);
      return;
    }

    var entityType = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.get(entityLoc);
    ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();

    Vec3 look = player.getLookAngle();
    double spawnX = session.originPos.x + (look.x * 6.0D);
    double spawnZ = session.originPos.z + (look.z * 6.0D);
    double spawnY = session.originPos.y + 1.0D;

    BlockPos spawnPos = BlockPos.containing(spawnX, spawnY, spawnZ);
    Entity spawned = entityType.spawn(level, spawnPos, MobSpawnType.EVENT);

    if (spawned != null) {
      if (spawned instanceof EnderDragon dragon) {
        dragon.setPos(spawnX, spawnY + 8.0D, spawnZ);
        dragon.getPhaseManager().setPhase(EnderDragonPhase.HOLDING_PATTERN);
      }
      session.currentBossUuid = spawned.getUUID();
      session.allSpawnedEntities.add(spawned.getUUID());

      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new BossRushSyncPayload(
        true,
        "",
        "",
        "",
        "",
        "",
        "",
        "",
        spawned.getId(),
        BossRushReloadListener.getRawHealthBarsJson()
      ));
    }
  }

  public static void failAndCleanupSession(ServerPlayer player, RushSession session, String reasonBanner) {
    ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(session.playerUuid);
    ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();

    for (UUID entityUuid : session.allSpawnedEntities) {
      Entity e = level.getEntity(entityUuid);
      if (e != null && e.isAlive()) {
        e.discard();
      }
    }
    session.allSpawnedEntities.clear();

      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new BossRushSyncPayload(
      false, "", "", "", "", "", "", reasonBanner, -1, "{}"
    ));
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
    if (ACTIVE_SESSIONS.isEmpty()) return;

    for (RushSession session : new HashSet<>(ACTIVE_SESSIONS.values())) {
      ServerPlayer player = event.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(session.playerUuid);
      if (player == null || !player.isAlive()) {
        if (player != null) {
          failAndCleanupSession(player, session, "§cBoss Rush Failed");
        } else {
          ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(session.playerUuid);
        }
        continue;
      }

      ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();
      ResourceLocation currentDim = level.dimension().location();
      double maxDist = session.sequence.areaOfEffect();
      if (!currentDim.equals(session.dimension) || player.position().distanceToSqr(session.originPos) > (maxDist * maxDist)) {
        failAndCleanupSession(player, session, "§cLeft Boss Rush Arena");
        continue;
      }

      // Fallback check for bosses like EnderDragon or modded bosses that discard/die without firing LivingDeathEvent
      if (session.currentBossUuid != null) {
        Entity boss = level.getEntity(session.currentBossUuid);
        boolean bossFinished = (boss == null || !boss.isAlive() || boss.isRemoved());
        if (!bossFinished && boss instanceof EnderDragon dragon && dragon.dragonDeathTime >= 180) {
          dragon.discard();
          bossFinished = true;
        }
        if (bossFinished) {
          session.currentBossUuid = null;
          session.bossIndexInPhase++;
          spawnNextBossInPhase(player, session);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onBossDeath(LivingDeathEvent event) {
    if (ACTIVE_SESSIONS.isEmpty() || event.getEntity().level().isClientSide()) return;
    UUID deadUuid = event.getEntity().getUUID();

    for (RushSession session : new HashSet<>(ACTIVE_SESSIONS.values())) {
      if (deadUuid.equals(session.currentBossUuid)) {
        session.currentBossUuid = null;
        ServerPlayer player = event.getEntity().getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(session.playerUuid);
        if (player != null && player.isAlive()) {
          session.bossIndexInPhase++;
          spawnNextBossInPhase(player, session);
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerLogout(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedOutEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) {
      RushSession session = ACTIVE_SESSIONS.get(sp.getUUID());
      if (session != null) {
        failAndCleanupSession(sp, session, "");
      }
    }
  }

  private static void playOriginEffect(ServerLevel level, Vec3 origin, String effectId) {
    if (effectId == null || effectId.isEmpty()) return;

    CustomEffectScript script = BossRushReloadListener.getCustomEffect(effectId);
    if (script != null) {
      if (script.lightning()) {
        LightningBolt bolt = EntityType.LIGHTNING_BOLT.create(level);
        if (bolt != null) {
          bolt.moveTo(origin.x, origin.y, origin.z);
          bolt.setVisualOnly(true);
          level.addFreshEntity(bolt);
        }
      }
      if (!script.sound().isEmpty()) {
        ResourceLocation soundLoc = ResourceLocation.tryParse(script.sound());
        if (soundLoc != null && BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.containsKey(soundLoc)) {
          SoundEvent se = BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.get(soundLoc);
          level.playSound(null, BlockPos.containing(origin), se, SoundSource.AMBIENT, 1.5f, 1.0f);
        }
      }
      spawnParticleRing(level, origin, script.particle(), script.count(), script.radius());
    } else {
      spawnParticleRing(level, origin, effectId, 48, 3.0D);
    }
  }

  private static void spawnParticleRing(ServerLevel level, Vec3 origin, String particleId, int count, double radius) {
    ParticleOptions options = ParticleTypes.END_ROD;
    ResourceLocation loc = ResourceLocation.tryParse(particleId);
    if (loc != null && BuiltInRegistries.PARTICLE_TYPE.containsKey(loc)) {
      if (BuiltInRegistries.PARTICLE_TYPE.get(loc) instanceof ParticleOptions directOptions) {
        options = directOptions;
      }
    }

    for (int i = 0; count > i; i++) {
      double angle = (2.0D * Math.PI * i) / count;
      double px = origin.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
      double pz = origin.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
      level.sendParticles(options, px, origin.y + 0.5D, pz, 2, 0.1D, 0.4D, 0.1D, 0.02D);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerLogin(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedInEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) {
      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(sp, new BossRushSyncPayload(
        false, "", "", "", "", "", "", "", -1, BossRushReloadListener.getRawHealthBarsJson()
      ));
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onDatapackSync(net.neoforged.neoforge.event.OnDatapackSyncEvent event) {
    String json = BossRushReloadListener.getRawHealthBarsJson();
    if (event.getPlayer() != null) {
      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(event.getPlayer(), new BossRushSyncPayload(
        isPlayerInRush(event.getPlayer().getUUID()), "", "", "", "", "", "", "", -1, json
      ));
    } else {
      for (ServerPlayer sp : event.getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
        PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(sp, new BossRushSyncPayload(
          isPlayerInRush(sp.getUUID()), "", "", "", "", "", "", "", -1, json
        ));
      }
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/client/BossHealthBarRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;

import java.util.List;

public final class BossHealthBarRenderer {
  private static float smoothedHealthRatio = 1.0f;
  private static int trackedEntityId = -1;

  private BossHealthBarRenderer() {}

  public static void renderCustomBossBar(
    GuiGraphics guiGraphics,
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource,
    Font font,
    int screenWidth,
    LivingEntity boss,
    HealthBarStyle style,
    float lagOffsetX,
    float lagOffsetY
  ) {
    try{
      if (boss == null || !boss.isAlive()) {
        trackedEntityId = -1;
        return;
      }

      float maxHp = Math.max(1.0f, boss.getMaxHealth());
      float currentHp = Mth.clamp(boss.getHealth(), 0.0f, maxHp);

      float hpScale = 1.0f;
      var maxHpAttr = boss.getAttribute(net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes.MAX_HEALTH);
      if (maxHpAttr != null) {
        var mod = maxHpAttr.getModifier(net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore.MODID, "difficulty_hp_scale"
        ));
        if (mod != null) {
          hpScale = (float) Math.max(0.1D, 1.0D + mod.amount());
        }
      }

      style = style.resolveForHp(currentHp, hpScale);

      float targetRatio = currentHp / maxHp;

      if (trackedEntityId != boss.getId()) {
        trackedEntityId = boss.getId();
        smoothedHealthRatio = targetRatio;
      } else {
        smoothedHealthRatio = Mth.lerp(0.12f, smoothedHealthRatio, targetRatio);
      }

      float screenCenterX = (screenWidth * 0.5f) + lagOffsetX;

      // 1. Top Box: Entity Nameplate with gradient-colored characters
      String rawName = (style.displayName() != null && !style.displayName().isEmpty())
      ? style.displayName()
      : boss.getDisplayName().getString();
      MutableComponent gradientName = buildGradientText(rawName, style);
      int nameTextWidth = font.width(gradientName);
      float nameBoxWidth = Math.max(96.0f, nameTextWidth + 22.0f);
      float nameBoxHeight = font.lineHeight + 8.0f;
      float nameCenterY = 6.0f + (nameBoxHeight * 0.5f) + lagOffsetY;

      List<FormattedCharSequence> nameLines = List.of(gradientName.getVisualOrderText());
      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        guiGraphics.pose(), bufferSource, font,
        screenCenterX, nameCenterY, 0.0f,
        nameBoxWidth, nameBoxHeight, 2.5f, 2.5f,
        style.backgroundColor(), style.borderColor(), nameLines, true
      );

      // 2. Second Box: Wide Health Bar Container directly beneath the Nameplate
      float barBoxWidth = Math.min(240.0f, screenWidth - 40.0f);
      float barBoxHeight = 14.0f;
      float barCenterY = (6.0f + nameBoxHeight + 3.0f) + (barBoxHeight * 0.5f) + lagOffsetY;

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        guiGraphics.pose(), bufferSource, font,
        screenCenterX, barCenterY, 0.0f,
        barBoxWidth, barBoxHeight, 2.5f, 2.5f,
        style.backgroundColor(), style.borderColor(), List.of(), false
      );

      // Flush ThreeDified geometry before drawing the quantized gradient cells on top
      bufferSource.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
      bufferSource.endBatch();

      // Inner fill bounds inside the border
      float pad = 3.0f;
      float innerLeft = screenCenterX - (barBoxWidth * 0.5f) + pad;
      float innerTop = barCenterY - (barBoxHeight * 0.5f) + pad;
      float innerWidth = barBoxWidth - (pad * 2.0f);
      float innerHeight = barBoxHeight - (pad * 2.0f);

      // Trailing damage drain bar
      int trailWidth = Math.round(innerWidth * smoothedHealthRatio);
      if (trailWidth > 0) {
        guiGraphics.fill(
          Math.round(innerLeft),
          Math.round(innerTop),
          Math.round(innerLeft) + trailWidth,
          Math.round(innerTop + innerHeight),
          0x66FF4455
        );
      }

      // Quantized pixel grid controlled by pixel_density (1.0 = 1px, 0.05 = 8px)
      int fillWidth = Math.round(innerWidth * targetRatio);
      if (fillWidth > 0) {
        float cellSize = Mth.lerp(style.pixelDensity(), 8.0f, 1.0f);
        int cols = Math.max(1, Math.round(innerWidth / cellSize));
        int rows = Math.max(1, Math.round(innerHeight / cellSize));

        double rad = Math.toRadians(style.colorAngle());
        double dirX = Math.cos(rad);
        double dirY = Math.sin(rad);
        double maxProj = Math.abs(dirX) + Math.abs(dirY);
        if (0.0001D > maxProj) maxProj = 1.0D;

        int clipRight = Math.round(innerLeft) + fillWidth;

        for (int c = 0; cols > c; c++) {
          float x0 = innerLeft + (c * (innerWidth / cols));
          float x1 = innerLeft + ((c + 1) * (innerWidth / cols));
          int ix0 = Math.round(x0);
          int ix1 = Math.min(clipRight, Math.round(x1));
          if (ix0 >= ix1) break;

          float u = (c + 0.5f) / (float) cols;

          for (int r = 0; rows > r; r++) {
            float y0 = innerTop + (r * (innerHeight / rows));
            float y1 = innerTop + ((r + 1) * (innerHeight / rows));
            int iy0 = Math.round(y0);
            int iy1 = Math.round(y1);
            if (iy0 >= iy1) continue;

            float v = (r + 0.5f) / (float) rows;

            // Directional linear gradient projection (-90 to +90 degrees)
            double proj = ((u - 0.5D) * dirX + (v - 0.5D) * dirY) / maxProj + 0.5D;
            int color = style.sampleGradient((float) proj);

            // Subtle top bevel highlight & bottom shadow for crisp pixel depth
            if (r == 0 && rows > 2) {
              color = HealthBarStyle.lerpArgb(color, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0.22f);
            } else if (r == rows - 1 && rows > 2) {
              color = HealthBarStyle.lerpArgb(color, 0xFF000000, 0.28f);
            }

            guiGraphics.fill(ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1, color);
          }
        }
      }

      // Centered HP readout inside the bar
      String hpText = Math.round(currentHp) + " / " + Math.round(maxHp);
      int hpTextW = font.width(hpText);
      PoseStack pose = guiGraphics.pose();
      pose.pushPose();
      pose.translate(screenCenterX - (hpTextW * 0.25f), innerTop + (innerHeight * 0.5f) - (font.lineHeight * 0.25f), 5.0f);
      pose.scale(0.5f, 0.5f, 1.0f);
      guiGraphics.drawString(font, hpText, 0, 0, 0xFFFFFFFF, true);
      pose.popPose();
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("BossHealthBarRenderer error", t);
    }
  }

  private static MutableComponent buildGradientText(String text, HealthBarStyle style) {
    MutableComponent result = Component.empty();
    int len = text.length();
    for (int i = 0; len > i; i++) {
      float t = (len > 1) ? ((float) i / (float) (len - 1)) : 0.5f;
      int rgb = style.sampleGradient(t) & 0x00FFFFFF;
      result.append(Component.literal(String.valueOf(text.charAt(i)))
        .withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withColor(rgb).withBold(true)));
    }
    return result;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/client/BossRushClientHud.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.threedishapes_hooks.ThreeDiShapeHooks;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class BossRushClientHud {
  private static boolean bossRushActive = false;
  private static int activeBossEntityId = -1;
  private static String currentMusicId = "";
  private static final Map<String, HealthBarStyle> CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS = new HashMap<>();

  private static String phaseName = "";
  private static String fullDescription = "";
  private static int revealedChars = 0;
  private static long bannerStartMs = 0L;
  private static final long BANNER_DURATION_MS = 7000L;
  private static final long BANNER_SLIDE_MS = 750L;

  private static String musicTitle = "";
  private static long musicToastStartMs = 0L;
  private static final long MUSIC_TOAST_DURATION_MS = 5000L;
  private static final long MUSIC_TOAST_SLIDE_MS = 750L;
  private static SoundInstance activeMusicInstance = null;

  private static String statusBanner = "";
  private static int statusBannerTicks = 0;

  private static boolean hasCameraHistory = false;
  private static float lastCamYaw = 0.0f;
  private static float lastCamPitch = 0.0f;
  private static float lagOffsetX = 0.0f;
  private static float lagOffsetY = 0.0f;

  private static final TextEffectStyle PHASE_TITLE_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.spectrum();
  private static final TextEffectStyle PHASE_DESC_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.glitch();
  private static final TextEffectStyle MUSIC_TOAST_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.prismaticOutline();

  public static boolean isBossRushActive() {
    return bossRushActive;
  }

  private static LivingEntity resolveDisplayedBoss(Minecraft mc) {
    if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null || CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.isEmpty()) return null;

    if (bossRushActive && activeBossEntityId != -1) {
      Entity target = mc.level.getEntity(activeBossEntityId);
      if (target instanceof LivingEntity boss && !boss.isRemoved() && boss.isAlive()) {
        ResourceLocation typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(boss.getType());
        if (CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.containsKey(typeId.toString())) {
          return boss;
        }
      }
    }

    LivingEntity nearestGlobalBoss = null;
    double bestDistSq = 48.0D * 48.0D;

    for (Entity entity : mc.level.entitiesForRendering()) {
      if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity living) || living == mc.player || !living.isAlive()) continue;
      ResourceLocation typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(living.getType());
      HealthBarStyle style = CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.get(typeId.toString());
      if (style != null && !style.bossRushOnly()) {
        double distSq = mc.player.distanceToSqr(living);
        if (bestDistSq > distSq) {
          bestDistSq = distSq;
          nearestGlobalBoss = living;
        }
      }
    }

    return nearestGlobalBoss;
  }

  public static boolean shouldBlockExternalSound(SoundInstance sound) {
    if (!bossRushActive || sound == null || sound == activeMusicInstance) return false;

    ResourceLocation loc = sound.getLocation();
    if (HexaCreateCore.MODID.equals(loc.getNamespace())) return false;

    SoundSource source = sound.getSource();
    String path = loc.getPath().toLowerCase();

    if (source == SoundSource.MUSIC || source == SoundSource.RECORDS) return true;
    if (path.contains("music") || path.contains("theme") || path.contains("bgm") || path.contains("ost")) return true;

    return false;
  }


  public static void handleSyncPacket(BossRushSyncPayload payload) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();

    if (payload.healthBarsJson() != null && !payload.healthBarsJson().isEmpty() && !"{}".equals(payload.healthBarsJson())) {
      try {
        JsonObject parsed = JsonParser.parseString(payload.healthBarsJson()).getAsJsonObject();
        CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.clear();
        CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.putAll(BossRushReloadListener.parseHealthBarsMap(parsed));
      } catch (Exception e) {
        HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("Failed parsing synced health_bars.json on client", e);
      }
    }

    if (!payload.totemItemId().isEmpty() && mc.gameRenderer != null) {
      ResourceLocation itemLoc = ResourceLocation.tryParse(payload.totemItemId());
      if (itemLoc != null && BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.containsKey(itemLoc)) {
        Item item = BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.get(itemLoc);
        mc.gameRenderer.displayItemActivation(new ItemStack(item));
        if (mc.player != null && mc.particleEngine != null) {
          mc.particleEngine.createTrackingEmitter(mc.player, ParticleTypes.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING, 30);
        }
      }
    }

    if (!payload.totemSoundId().isEmpty() && mc.player != null) {
      ResourceLocation soundLoc = payload.totemSoundId().contains(":")
        ? ResourceLocation.tryParse(payload.totemSoundId())
        : ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, payload.totemSoundId());
      if (soundLoc != null) {
        SoundEvent se = BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.containsKey(soundLoc)
          ? BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.get(soundLoc)
          : SoundEvent.createVariableRangeEvent(soundLoc);
        mc.player.playSound(se, 1.0f, 1.0f);
      }
    }

    if (!payload.statusBanner().isEmpty()) {
      statusBanner = payload.statusBanner();
      statusBannerTicks = 80;
    }

    bossRushActive = payload.active();

    if (!payload.active()) {
      activeBossEntityId = -1;
      currentMusicId = "";
      phaseName = "";
      fullDescription = "";
      bannerStartMs = 0L;

      if (!payload.musicId().isEmpty()) {
        playBossRushMusic(payload.musicId(), false);
        musicTitle = payload.musicTitle().isEmpty() ? payload.musicId() : payload.musicTitle();
        musicToastStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
      } else {
        stopActiveMusic();
        musicToastStartMs = 0L;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (payload.activeBossEntityId() != -1) {
      activeBossEntityId = payload.activeBossEntityId();
    }

    if (!payload.phaseName().isEmpty()) {
      phaseName = payload.phaseName();
      fullDescription = payload.phaseDescription();
      revealedChars = 0;
      bannerStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    if (!payload.musicId().isEmpty()) {
      playBossRushMusic(payload.musicId(), true);
      musicTitle = payload.musicTitle().isEmpty() ? payload.musicId() : payload.musicTitle();
      musicToastStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }
  }


  private static void playBossRushMusic(String musicName) {
    playBossRushMusic(musicName, true);
  }

  private static void playBossRushMusic(String musicName, boolean loop) {
    stopActiveMusic();
    currentMusicId = loop ? musicName : "";
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();

    mc.getSoundManager().stop(null, SoundSource.MUSIC);
    mc.getSoundManager().stop(null, SoundSource.RECORDS);

    ResourceLocation soundLoc = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "boss_rush.music." + musicName);
    SoundEvent event = SoundEvent.createVariableRangeEvent(soundLoc);

    activeMusicInstance = new SimpleSoundInstance(
      event.getLocation(), SoundSource.MASTER, 1.0f, 1.0f,
      SoundInstance.createUnseededRandom(), loop, 0,
      SoundInstance.Attenuation.NONE, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0.0D, true
    );
    mc.getSoundManager().play(activeMusicInstance);
  }

  public static void stopActiveMusic() {
    if (activeMusicInstance != null) {
      Minecraft.getInstance().getSoundManager().stop(activeMusicInstance);
      activeMusicInstance = null;
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onRenderVanillaBossBar(CustomizeGuiOverlayEvent.BossEventProgress event) {
    if (hasActiveCustomBossBar()) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();

    if (bannerStartMs > 0L && (BANNER_DURATION_MS > (System.currentTimeMillis() - bannerStartMs))) {
      if (fullDescription.length() > revealedChars) {
        revealedChars = Math.min(fullDescription.length(), revealedChars + 2);
        if (mc.player != null && revealedChars % 4 == 0) {
          mc.player.playSound(SoundEvents.UI_BUTTON_CLICK.value(), 0.15f, 1.8f);
        }
      }
    }

    if (statusBannerTicks > 0) statusBannerTicks--;

    if (bossRushActive && mc.player != null) {
      if (!currentMusicId.isEmpty() && mc.player.tickCount % 20 == 0) {
        if (activeMusicInstance == null || !mc.getSoundManager().isActive(activeMusicInstance)) {
          playBossRushMusic(currentMusicId);
        }
      }

      if (mc.player.tickCount % 10 == 0) {
        try {
          Object soundEngine = null;
          for (Field f : mc.getSoundManager().getClass().getDeclaredFields()) {
            if (f.getType().getSimpleName().equals("SoundEngine")) {
              f.setAccessible(true);
              soundEngine = f.get(mc.getSoundManager());
              break;
            }
          }
          if (soundEngine != null) {
            for (Field f : soundEngine.getClass().getDeclaredFields()) {
              if (Map.class.isAssignableFrom(f.getType())) {
                f.setAccessible(true);
                Object mapObj = f.get(soundEngine);
                if (mapObj instanceof Map<?, ?> map) {
                  for (Object key : new ArrayList<>(map.keySet())) {
                    if (key instanceof SoundInstance si && si != activeMusicInstance && shouldBlockExternalSound(si)) {
                      mc.getSoundManager().stop(si);
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
      }
    }
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiLayerEvent.Post event) {
    if (!event.getName().equals(VanillaGuiLayers.EXPERIENCE_BAR)) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;

    long nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
    long bannerElapsedMs = (bannerStartMs > 0L) ? (nowMs - bannerStartMs) : Long.MAX_VALUE;
    boolean isBannerVisible = (bannerStartMs > 0L) && (BANNER_DURATION_MS > bannerElapsedMs);

    long musicElapsedMs = (musicToastStartMs > 0L) ? (nowMs - musicToastStartMs) : Long.MAX_VALUE;
    boolean isMusicToastVisible = (musicToastStartMs > 0L) && (MUSIC_TOAST_DURATION_MS > musicElapsedMs) && !musicTitle.isEmpty();

    LivingEntity displayedBoss = resolveDisplayedBoss(mc);
    boolean anyVisible = (displayedBoss != null) || isBannerVisible || isMusicToastVisible || (statusBannerTicks > 0);
    if (!anyVisible) {
      hasCameraHistory = false;
      lagOffsetX = 0.0f;
      lagOffsetY = 0.0f;
      return;
    }

    float currentYaw = mc.player.getYRot();
    float currentPitch = mc.player.getXRot();
    if (!hasCameraHistory) {
      lastCamYaw = currentYaw;
      lastCamPitch = currentPitch;
      hasCameraHistory = true;
    } else {
      float deltaYaw = Mth.wrapDegrees(currentYaw - lastCamYaw);
      float deltaPitch = currentPitch - lastCamPitch;
      lastCamYaw = currentYaw;
      lastCamPitch = currentPitch;

      lagOffsetX = Mth.clamp(lagOffsetX - (deltaYaw * 0.45f), -24.0f, 24.0f);
      lagOffsetY = Mth.clamp(lagOffsetY - (deltaPitch * 0.45f), -16.0f, 16.0f);

      float dt = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
      double linearAlpha = Math.min(1.0D, dt * 3.0D);
      float sineEase = (float) Math.sin(linearAlpha * (Math.PI / 2.0D));

      lagOffsetX = Mth.lerp(sineEase, lagOffsetX, 0.0f);
      lagOffsetY = Mth.lerp(sineEase, lagOffsetY, 0.0f);
    }

    Font font = mc.font;
    int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
    int screenHeight = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();
    float screenCenterX = screenWidth * 0.5f;

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getGuiGraphics().pose();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();

    if (displayedBoss != null) {
      ResourceLocation typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(displayedBoss.getType());
      HealthBarStyle barStyle = CLIENT_HEALTH_BARS.get(typeId.toString());
      if (barStyle != null) {
        BossHealthBarRenderer.renderCustomBossBar(
          event.getGuiGraphics(), bufferSource, font, screenWidth, displayedBoss, barStyle, lagOffsetX, lagOffsetY
        );
      }
    }

    boolean drawCustomTitle = false;
    float savedTitleCenterX = 0.0f;
    float savedTitleCenterY = 0.0f;
    List<String> savedTitleLines = List.of();

    if (isBannerVisible && !phaseName.isEmpty()) {
      String cleanPhaseName = ChatFormatting.stripFormatting(phaseName);
      if (cleanPhaseName == null) cleanPhaseName = phaseName;
      String styledName = "§6§l" + cleanPhaseName;
      int maxTitleWidth = Math.min(260, screenWidth - 40);
      List<FormattedCharSequence> lines = font.split(Component.literal(styledName), maxTitleWidth - 20);

      int widestTitle = 0;
      for (FormattedCharSequence seq : lines) {
        widestTitle = Math.max(widestTitle, font.width(seq));
      }

      float boxWidth = widestTitle + 24.0f;
      float boxHeight = (lines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;

      long bannerRemainingMs = BANNER_DURATION_MS - bannerElapsedMs;
      float slideProgress = 1.0f;
      if (BANNER_SLIDE_MS > bannerElapsedMs) {
        float t = (float) bannerElapsedMs / (float) BANNER_SLIDE_MS;
        slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(t * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      } else if (BANNER_SLIDE_MS > bannerRemainingMs) {
        float t = (float) bannerRemainingMs / (float) BANNER_SLIDE_MS;
        slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(t * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      }

      float targetCenterY = 48.0f + (boxHeight * 0.5f);
      float offscreenCenterY = -(boxHeight * 0.5f) - 10.0f;
      float centerX = screenCenterX + lagOffsetX;
      float centerY = Mth.lerp(slideProgress, offscreenCenterY, targetCenterY) + lagOffsetY;

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        centerX, centerY, 0.0f,
        boxWidth, boxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0xCC110E1A, 0xFFFFAA00, List.of(), true
      );
      ThreeDiShapeHooks.renderAttachedShapes(
        poseStack, bufferSource, UiShapeTarget.BOSS_RUSH_TITLE, "",
        centerX, centerY, 0.0f, boxWidth, boxHeight
      );

      drawCustomTitle = true;
      savedTitleCenterX = centerX;
      savedTitleCenterY = centerY;
      savedTitleLines = toPlainLines(lines);
    }

    boolean drawCustomMusic = false;
    float savedMusicCenterX = 0.0f;
    float savedMusicCenterY = 0.0f;
    List<String> savedMusicLines = List.of();

    if (isMusicToastVisible) {
      String cleanMusic = ChatFormatting.stripFormatting("♪ " + musicTitle);
      if (cleanMusic == null) cleanMusic = "♪ " + musicTitle;
      List<FormattedCharSequence> lines = font.split(Component.literal(cleanMusic), 220);

      int widestMusic = 0;
      for (FormattedCharSequence seq : lines) {
        widestMusic = Math.max(widestMusic, font.width(seq));
      }

      float boxWidth = widestMusic + 22.0f;
      float boxHeight = (lines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;

      long remainingMs = MUSIC_TOAST_DURATION_MS - musicElapsedMs;
      float slideProgress = 1.0f;
      if (MUSIC_TOAST_SLIDE_MS > musicElapsedMs) {
        float t = (float) musicElapsedMs / (float) MUSIC_TOAST_SLIDE_MS;
        slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(t * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      } else if (MUSIC_TOAST_SLIDE_MS > remainingMs) {
        float t = (float) remainingMs / (float) MUSIC_TOAST_SLIDE_MS;
        slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(t * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      }

      float targetCenterX = screenWidth - (boxWidth * 0.5f) - 10.0f;
      float offscreenCenterX = screenWidth + (boxWidth * 0.5f) + 10.0f;
      float centerX = Mth.lerp(slideProgress, offscreenCenterX, targetCenterX) + (lagOffsetX * 0.5f);
      float centerY = 58.0f + (boxHeight * 0.5f) + (lagOffsetY * 0.5f);

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        centerX, centerY, 0.0f,
        boxWidth, boxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0xCC110E1A, 0xFF21B7D1, List.of(), true
      );

      drawCustomMusic = true;
      savedMusicCenterX = centerX;
      savedMusicCenterY = centerY;
      savedMusicLines = toPlainLines(lines);
    }


    boolean drawCustomStatus = false;
    float savedStatusCenterX = 0.0f;
    float savedStatusCenterY = 0.0f;
    List<String> savedStatusLines = List.of();
    int statusBorderColor = com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client.DifficultyClientState.activeTier.accentColor;

    if (statusBannerTicks > 0 && !statusBanner.isEmpty()) {
      String cleanStatus = ChatFormatting.stripFormatting(statusBanner);
      if (cleanStatus == null) cleanStatus = statusBanner;
      var boldStatus = Component.literal(cleanStatus).withStyle(net.minecraft.network.chat.Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
      List<FormattedCharSequence> lines = font.split(boldStatus, 260);
      int widestStatus = 0;
      for (FormattedCharSequence seq : lines) {
        widestStatus = Math.max(widestStatus, font.width(seq));
      }

      float boxWidth = widestStatus + 26.0f;
      float boxHeight = (lines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 14.0f;
      float centerX = screenCenterX + lagOffsetX;
      float centerY = 48.0f + (boxHeight * 0.5f) + lagOffsetY;

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        centerX, centerY, 0.0f,
        boxWidth, boxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0xCC110E1A, statusBorderColor, List.of(), true
      );

      drawCustomStatus = true;
      savedStatusCenterX = centerX;
      savedStatusCenterY = centerY;
      savedStatusLines = toPlainLines(lines);
    }
    boolean drawCustomDesc = false;
    float savedDescCenterX = 0.0f;
    float savedDescCenterY = 0.0f;
    List<String> savedDescLines = List.of();

    if (isBannerVisible && revealedChars > 0) {
      String rawVisible = fullDescription.substring(0, revealedChars);
      String cleanVisible = ChatFormatting.stripFormatting(rawVisible);
      if (cleanVisible == null) cleanVisible = rawVisible;
      int maxBoxWidth = Math.min(260, screenWidth - 40);
      List<FormattedCharSequence> descLines = font.split(Component.literal(cleanVisible), maxBoxWidth - 20);

      int widestLine = 0;
      for (FormattedCharSequence seq : descLines) {
        widestLine = Math.max(widestLine, font.width(seq));
      }

      float descBoxWidth = widestLine + 24.0f;
      float descBoxHeight = (descLines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;
      float descCenterX = screenCenterX + lagOffsetX;
      float descCenterY = (screenHeight - 50.0f - (descBoxHeight * 0.5f)) + lagOffsetY;

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        descCenterX, descCenterY, 0.0f,
        descBoxWidth, descBoxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0xCC110E1A, 0xFF8A4FFF, List.of(), true
      );

      drawCustomDesc = true;
      savedDescCenterX = descCenterX;
      savedDescCenterY = descCenterY;
      savedDescLines = toPlainLines(descLines);
    }

    bufferSource.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    bufferSource.endBatch();
    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();

    if (drawCustomStatus) {
      TextEffectStyle statusStyle = getCompletionBannerStyle();
      float startY = savedStatusCenterY - (savedStatusLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);
      for (int i = 0; savedStatusLines.size() > i; i++) {
        String line = savedStatusLines.get(i);
        var boldComp = Component.literal(line).withStyle(net.minecraft.network.chat.Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
        float lx = savedStatusCenterX - (font.width(boldComp) * 0.5f);
        ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2DBold(
          event.getGuiGraphics(), font, line, lx, startY + (i * font.lineHeight), statusStyle
        );
      }
    }

    if (drawCustomMusic) {
      float startY = savedMusicCenterY - (savedMusicLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);
      for (int i = 0; savedMusicLines.size() > i; i++) {
        String line = savedMusicLines.get(i);
        float lx = savedMusicCenterX - (font.width(line) * 0.5f);
        ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2D(
          event.getGuiGraphics(), font, line, lx, startY + (i * font.lineHeight), MUSIC_TOAST_STYLE
        );
      }
    }

    if (drawCustomTitle) {
      float startY = savedTitleCenterY - (savedTitleLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);
      for (int i = 0; savedTitleLines.size() > i; i++) {
        String line = savedTitleLines.get(i);
        var boldComp = Component.literal(line).withStyle(net.minecraft.network.chat.Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
        float lx = savedTitleCenterX - (font.width(boldComp) * 0.5f);
        ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2DBold(
          event.getGuiGraphics(), font, line, lx, startY + (i * font.lineHeight), PHASE_TITLE_STYLE
        );
      }
    }

    if (drawCustomDesc) {
      float startY = savedDescCenterY - (savedDescLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);
      for (int i = 0; savedDescLines.size() > i; i++) {
        String line = savedDescLines.get(i);
        float lx = savedDescCenterX - (font.width(line) * 0.5f);
        ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2D(
          event.getGuiGraphics(), font, line, lx, startY + (i * font.lineHeight), PHASE_DESC_STYLE
        );
      }
    }
  }

  private static List<String> toPlainLines(List<FormattedCharSequence> seqs) {
    List<String> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>(seqs.size());
    for (FormattedCharSequence seq : seqs) {
      StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
      seq.accept((idx, style, codePoint) -> {
        sb.appendCodePoint(codePoint);
        return true;
      });
      out.add(sb.toString());
    }
    return out;
  }


  @SubscribeEvent(priority = net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onPreRenderGuiLayer(RenderGuiLayerEvent.Pre event) {
    if (!hasActiveCustomBossBar()) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player != null && mc.player.tickCount % 60 == 0) {
      HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("[BossBarLayerProbe] Active GuiLayer: {}", event.getName());
    }

    ResourceLocation layerName = event.getName();
    String ns = layerName.getNamespace().toLowerCase();
    String path = layerName.getPath().toLowerCase();

    if (HexaCreateCore.MODID.equals(ns) || "minecraft".equals(ns) || "jade".equals(ns)) {
      if (VanillaGuiLayers.BOSS_OVERLAY.equals(layerName)) {
        event.setCanceled(true);
      }
      return;
    }

    // Cancel any external mod layer related to boss bars / custom boss HUDs
    if (ns.contains("legendary") || ns.contains("bosses") || ns.contains("wither")
    || path.contains("boss") || path.contains("health_bar") || path.contains("bossbar")) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }

  private static TextEffectStyle getCompletionBannerStyle() {
    var tier = com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client.DifficultyClientState.activeTier;
    return switch (tier) {
      case ETHEREAL -> new TextEffectStyle(
        List.of(0xFF38BDF8, 0xFFBAE6FD, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFF38BDF8),
        List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
        1.6f, 0.45f, 0.28f,
        TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM, 0x7738BDF8, 0x44E0F2FE, true, true
      );
      case ABSOLUTION -> new TextEffectStyle(
        List.of(0xFF1A082B, 0xFF6B21A8, 0xFFA855F7, 0xFFE9D5FF),
        List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
        0.0f, 0.40f, 0.25f,
        TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.NONE, 0xFF9333EA, 0xFF1A082B, false, true
      );
      case GENESIS -> new TextEffectStyle(
        List.of(0xFFFF1E56, 0xFF8B0000, 0xFFDC2626, 0xFFFF1E56),
        List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
        2.4f, 0.85f, 0.40f,
        TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH, 0x99FF1E56, 0x99660011, true, true
      );
      default -> PHASE_TITLE_STYLE;
    };
  }

  public static boolean hasActiveCustomBossBar() {
    return resolveDisplayedBoss(Minecraft.getInstance()) != null;
  }

}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/data/BossHealthBarData.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data;

import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import java.util.List;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.Overload;

public final class BossHealthBarData {

  
  public record HpPhaseOverride(
    float belowHp,
    String displayName,
    int borderColor,
    int backgroundColor,
    List<Integer> barColors,
    List<Float> barColorPositions,
    float pixelDensity,
    float colorAngle,
    String moveDirection,
    float moveSpeed
  ) {}


  public record HealthBarStyle(
    String displayName,
    int borderColor,
    int backgroundColor,
    List<Integer> barColors,
    List<Float> barColorPositions,
    float pixelDensity,
    float colorAngle,
    String moveDirection,
    float moveSpeed,
    boolean bossRushOnly,
    List<HpPhaseOverride> hpPhases
  ) {

    public int sampleGradient(float t) {
      if (barColors.isEmpty()) return 0xFFFFFFFF;
      if (barColors.size() == 1) return barColors.get(0);

      float shiftedT = t;
      boolean moving = moveSpeed > 0.0f && ("left".equalsIgnoreCase(moveDirection) || "right".equalsIgnoreCase(moveDirection));
      if (moving) {
        float timeSec = (System.currentTimeMillis() % 600000L) / 1000.0f;
        float offset = timeSec * (moveSpeed * 0.15f);
        shiftedT = "left".equalsIgnoreCase(moveDirection) ? (t + offset) : (t - offset);
        shiftedT = ((shiftedT % 1.0f) + 1.0f) % 1.0f;
      }

      float clamped = Mth.clamp(shiftedT, 0.0f, 1.0f);
      int count = barColors.size();
      float firstPos = getStopPos(0, count);
      float lastPos = getStopPos(count - 1, count);

      if (moving && (firstPos > clamped || clamped > lastPos)) {
        float wrapSpan = (1.0f - lastPos) + firstPos;
        if (wrapSpan > 0.0001f) {
          float distFromLast = (clamped >= lastPos) ? (clamped - lastPos) : ((1.0f - lastPos) + clamped);
          return lerpArgb(barColors.get(count - 1), barColors.get(0), distFromLast / wrapSpan);
        }
      }

      if (firstPos >= clamped) return barColors.get(0);
      if (clamped >= lastPos) return barColors.get(count - 1);

      for (int i = 0; (count - 1) > i; i++) {
        float p0 = getStopPos(i, count);
        float p1 = getStopPos(i + 1, count);
        if (clamped >= p0 && p1 >= clamped) {
          float span = p1 - p0;
          float localAlpha = (span > 0.0001f) ? ((clamped - p0) / span) : 0.0f;
          return lerpArgb(barColors.get(i), barColors.get(i + 1), localAlpha);
        }
      }
      return barColors.get(count - 1);
    }

    private float getStopPos(int index, int total) {
      if (barColorPositions != null && barColorPositions.size() > index) {
        return Mth.clamp(barColorPositions.get(index), 0.0f, 1.0f);
      }
      return (total > 1) ? ((float) index / (float) (total - 1)) : 0.0f;
    }

    public static int lerpArgb(int c0, int c1, float t) {
      int a0 = (c0 >>> 24) & 0xFF, r0 = (c0 >>> 16) & 0xFF, g0 = (c0 >>> 8) & 0xFF, b0 = c0 & 0xFF;
      int a1 = (c1 >>> 24) & 0xFF, r1 = (c1 >>> 16) & 0xFF, g1 = (c1 >>> 8) & 0xFF, b1 = c1 & 0xFF;
      int a = (int) Mth.lerp(t, a0, a1);
      int r = (int) Mth.lerp(t, r0, r1);
      int g = (int) Mth.lerp(t, g0, g1);
      int b = (int) Mth.lerp(t, b0, b1);
      return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }

    public static int parseHexColor(String hex, int fallback) {
      if (hex == null || hex.isEmpty()) return fallback;
      String clean = hex.trim();
      if (clean.startsWith("#")) clean = clean.substring(1);
      if (clean.startsWith("0x") || clean.startsWith("0X")) clean = clean.substring(2);
      try {
        long val = Long.parseLong(clean, 16);
        return (clean.length() == 6) ? (int) (0xFF000000L | val) : (int) val;
      } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
        return fallback;
      }
    }

    public HealthBarStyle resolveForHp(float currentHp) {
      return resolveForHp(currentHp, 1.0f);
    }

    @Overload
    public HealthBarStyle resolveForHp(float currentHp, float hpScale) {
      if (hpPhases == null || hpPhases.isEmpty()) return this;
      float scale = Math.max(0.01f, hpScale);
      HpPhaseOverride best = null;
      for (HpPhaseOverride phase : hpPhases) {
        float scaledThreshold = phase.belowHp() * scale;
        if (scaledThreshold >= currentHp) {
          if (best == null || (best.belowHp() * scale) > scaledThreshold) {
            best = phase;
          }
        }
      }
      if (best == null) return this;
      String activeName = (best.displayName() != null && !best.displayName().isEmpty()) ? best.displayName() : displayName;
      return new HealthBarStyle(
        activeName,
        best.borderColor(),
        best.backgroundColor(),
        best.barColors(),
        best.barColorPositions(),
        best.pixelDensity(),
        best.colorAngle(),
        best.moveDirection(),
        best.moveSpeed(),
        bossRushOnly,
        List.of()
      );
    }
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data;

import java.util.List;

public final class BossRushData {
  public record SequenceConfig(
    String onTriggerItem,
    String onTriggerSound,
    int areaOfEffect,
    String endingMusic,
    String endingMusicTitle,
    List<PhaseConfig> phases
  ) {}

  public record PhaseConfig(
    String id,
    String name,
    String description,
    String music,
    String musicTitle,
    String onCompletionItem,
    String onCompletionSound,
    List<String> bosses,
    PhaseEffects effects
  ) {}

  public record PhaseEffects(
    String onStart,
    String onEnd
  ) {}

  public record CustomEffectScript(
    String particle,
    String sound,
    int count,
    double radius,
    boolean lightning
  ) {}
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/data/BossRushReloadListener.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushData.*;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.ResourceManager;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener;
import net.minecraft.util.profiling.ProfilerFiller;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.AddReloadListenerEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class BossRushReloadListener extends SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener {
  private static final Gson GSON = new Gson();
  private static SequenceConfig activeSequence = null;
  private static final Map<String, CustomEffectScript> CUSTOM_EFFECTS = new HashMap<>();
  private static final Map<String, BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle> HEALTH_BAR_STYLES = new HashMap<>();
  private static String rawHealthBarsJson = "{}";


  public BossRushReloadListener() {
    super(GSON, "boss_rush");
  }

  public static SequenceConfig getSequence() {
    return activeSequence;
  }

  public static CustomEffectScript getCustomEffect(String id) {
    return CUSTOM_EFFECTS.get(id);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onAddReloadListeners(AddReloadListenerEvent event) {
    event.addListener(new BossRushReloadListener());
  }

  @Override
  protected void apply(Map<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> objects, ResourceManager manager, ProfilerFiller profiler) {
    HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("[HealthBarDebug] Reload keys found: {}", objects.keySet());

    activeSequence = null;
    CUSTOM_EFFECTS.clear();
    HEALTH_BAR_STYLES.clear();
    rawHealthBarsJson = "{}";

    for (Map.Entry<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> entry : objects.entrySet()) {
      ResourceLocation loc = entry.getKey();
      if (!HexaCreateCore.MODID.equals(loc.getNamespace()) || !entry.getValue().isJsonObject()) continue;

      JsonObject root = entry.getValue().getAsJsonObject();
      String path = loc.getPath();

      if ("sequence".equals(path)) {
        activeSequence = parseSequence(root);
        HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("Loaded Boss Rush sequence with {} phases (AoE: {} blocks).",
          activeSequence.phases().size(), activeSequence.areaOfEffect());
      } else if (path.startsWith("effects/")) {
        String effectId = path.substring("effects/".length());
        CUSTOM_EFFECTS.put(effectId, parseEffectScript(root));
      } else if ("health_bars".equals(path)) {
        rawHealthBarsJson = root.toString();
        HEALTH_BAR_STYLES.putAll(parseHealthBarsMap(root));
        HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("Loaded {} custom Boss Rush health bar styles.", HEALTH_BAR_STYLES.size());
      }
    }
  }

  public static Map<String, BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle> parseHealthBarsMap(JsonObject root) {
    Map<String, BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle> map = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<String, JsonElement> entry : root.entrySet()) {
      if (!entry.getValue().isJsonObject()) continue;
      JsonObject obj = entry.getValue().getAsJsonObject();

      String displayName = obj.has("display_name") ? obj.get("display_name").getAsString() : "";
      int border = BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(
        obj.has("border_color") ? obj.get("border_color").getAsString() : "#FFAA00", 0xFFFFAA00
      );
      int bg = BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(
        obj.has("background_color") ? obj.get("background_color").getAsString() : "#CC110E1A", 0xCC110E1A
      );

      List<Integer> colors = new ArrayList<>();
      if (obj.has("bar_color") && obj.get("bar_color").isJsonArray()) {
        for (JsonElement c : obj.getAsJsonArray("bar_color")) {
          colors.add(BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(c.getAsString(), 0xFFFFFFFF));
        }
      }
      if (colors.isEmpty()) colors.add(0xFFFF3355);

      List<Float> positions = new ArrayList<>();
      if (obj.has("bar_color_pos") && obj.get("bar_color_pos").isJsonArray()) {
        for (JsonElement p : obj.getAsJsonArray("bar_color_pos")) {
          positions.add(p.getAsFloat());
        }
      }

      float pixelDensity = obj.has("pixel_density") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(obj.get("pixel_density").getAsFloat(), 0.05f, 1.0f) : 0.5f;
      float colorAngle = obj.has("color_angle") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(obj.get("color_angle").getAsFloat(), -90.0f, 90.0f) : 0.0f;
      String moveDir = obj.has("move_direction") ? obj.get("move_direction").getAsString() : "none";
      float moveSpeed = obj.has("move_speed") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(obj.get("move_speed").getAsFloat(), 0.0f, 20.0f) : 0.0f;
      boolean bossRushOnly = !obj.has("boss_rush_only") || obj.get("boss_rush_only").getAsBoolean();

      List<BossHealthBarData.HpPhaseOverride> hpPhases = new ArrayList<>();
      if (obj.has("hp_phases") && obj.get("hp_phases").isJsonArray()) {
        for (JsonElement phaseElem : obj.getAsJsonArray("hp_phases")) {
          if (!phaseElem.isJsonObject()) continue;
          JsonObject pObj = phaseElem.getAsJsonObject();
          float belowHp = pObj.has("below_hp") ? pObj.get("below_hp").getAsFloat() : 100.0f;
          String pName = pObj.has("display_name") ? pObj.get("display_name").getAsString() : displayName;
          int pBorder = BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(
            pObj.has("border_color") ? pObj.get("border_color").getAsString() : "#FF1E56", border
          );
          int pBg = BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(
            pObj.has("background_color") ? pObj.get("background_color").getAsString() : "#17060C", bg
          );

          List<Integer> pColors = new ArrayList<>();
          if (pObj.has("bar_color") && pObj.get("bar_color").isJsonArray()) {
            for (JsonElement c : pObj.getAsJsonArray("bar_color")) {
              pColors.add(BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle.parseHexColor(c.getAsString(), 0xFFFFFFFF));
            }
          }
          if (pColors.isEmpty()) pColors.addAll(colors);

          List<Float> pPositions = new ArrayList<>();
          if (pObj.has("bar_color_pos") && pObj.get("bar_color_pos").isJsonArray()) {
            for (JsonElement p : pObj.getAsJsonArray("bar_color_pos")) {
              pPositions.add(p.getAsFloat());
            }
          }

          float pDensity = pObj.has("pixel_density") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(pObj.get("pixel_density").getAsFloat(), 0.05f, 1.0f) : pixelDensity;
          float pAngle = pObj.has("color_angle") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(pObj.get("color_angle").getAsFloat(), -90.0f, 90.0f) : colorAngle;
          String pDir = pObj.has("move_direction") ? pObj.get("move_direction").getAsString() : moveDir;
          float pSpeed = pObj.has("move_speed") ? net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(pObj.get("move_speed").getAsFloat(), 0.0f, 20.0f) : moveSpeed;

          hpPhases.add(new BossHealthBarData.HpPhaseOverride(belowHp, pName, pBorder, pBg, pColors, pPositions, pDensity, pAngle, pDir, pSpeed));
        }
      }

      map.put(entry.getKey(), new BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle(
        displayName, border, bg, colors, positions, pixelDensity, colorAngle, moveDir, moveSpeed, bossRushOnly, hpPhases
      ));
    }
    return map;
  }


  private static SequenceConfig parseSequence(JsonObject root) {
    String onTrigger = root.has("on_trigger") ? root.get("on_trigger").getAsString() : "";
    String onTriggerSound = root.has("on_trigger_sound") ? root.get("on_trigger_sound").getAsString() : "minecraft:item.totem.use";
    int areaOfEffect = root.has("area_of_effect") ? root.get("area_of_effect").getAsInt() : 64;
    String endingMusic = root.has("ending_music") ? root.get("ending_music").getAsString() : "";
    String endingMusicTitle = root.has("ending_music_title") ? root.get("ending_music_title").getAsString() : "";

    List<PhaseConfig> phases = new ArrayList<>();
    if (root.has("phases") && root.get("phases").isJsonArray()) {
      for (JsonElement elem : root.getAsJsonArray("phases")) {
        if (!elem.isJsonObject()) continue;
        JsonObject p = elem.getAsJsonObject();

        String id = p.has("id") ? p.get("id").getAsString() : "phase_" + phases.size();
        String name = p.has("name") ? p.get("name").getAsString() : id;
        String desc = p.has("description") ? p.get("description").getAsString() : "";
        String music = p.has("music") ? p.get("music").getAsString() : "";
        String musicTitle = p.has("music_title") ? p.get("music_title").getAsString() : music;
        String onCompletion = p.has("on_completion") ? p.get("on_completion").getAsString() : "";
        String onCompletionSound = p.has("on_completion_sound") ? p.get("on_completion_sound").getAsString() : "minecraft:item.totem.use";

        List<String> bosses = new ArrayList<>();
        if (p.has("bosses") && p.get("bosses").isJsonArray()) {
          JsonArray bossArr = p.getAsJsonArray("bosses");
          for (JsonElement b : bossArr) {
            bosses.add(b.getAsString());
          }
        }

        String onStart = "";
        String onEnd = "";
        if (p.has("effects") && p.get("effects").isJsonObject()) {
          JsonObject eff = p.getAsJsonObject("effects");
          if (eff.has("on_start")) onStart = eff.get("on_start").getAsString();
          if (eff.has("on_end")) onEnd = eff.get("on_end").getAsString();
        }

        phases.add(new PhaseConfig(id, name, desc, music, musicTitle, onCompletion, onCompletionSound, bosses, new PhaseEffects(onStart, onEnd)));
      }
    }

    return new BossRushData.SequenceConfig(onTrigger, onTriggerSound, areaOfEffect, endingMusic, endingMusicTitle, phases);
  }

  private static CustomEffectScript parseEffectScript(JsonObject obj) {
    String particle = obj.has("particle") ? obj.get("particle").getAsString() : "minecraft:enchant";
    String sound = obj.has("sound") ? obj.get("sound").getAsString() : "";
    int count = obj.has("count") ? obj.get("count").getAsInt() : 60;
    double radius = obj.has("radius") ? obj.get("radius").getAsDouble() : 3.5D;
    boolean lightning = obj.has("lightning") && obj.get("lightning").getAsBoolean();
    return new CustomEffectScript(particle, sound, count, radius, lightning);
  }
  
  public static Map<String, BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle> getHealthBarStyles() {
    return HEALTH_BAR_STYLES;
  }

  public static String getRawHealthBarsJson() {
    return rawHealthBarsJson;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/bossrush/network/BossRushSyncPayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.network;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;

public record BossRushSyncPayload(
  boolean active,
  String phaseName,
  String phaseDescription,
  String musicId,
  String musicTitle,
  String totemItemId,
  String totemSoundId,
  String statusBanner,
  int activeBossEntityId,
  String healthBarsJson
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public static final Type<BossRushSyncPayload> TYPE =
    new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "boss_rush_sync"));

  public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, BossRushSyncPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
    (buf, p) -> {
      buf.writeBoolean(p.active());
      buf.writeUtf(p.phaseName() != null ? p.phaseName() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.phaseDescription() != null ? p.phaseDescription() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.musicId() != null ? p.musicId() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.musicTitle() != null ? p.musicTitle() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.totemItemId() != null ? p.totemItemId() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.totemSoundId() != null ? p.totemSoundId() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeUtf(p.statusBanner() != null ? p.statusBanner() : "", 32767);
      buf.writeInt(p.activeBossEntityId());
      buf.writeUtf(p.healthBarsJson() != null ? p.healthBarsJson() : "{}", 262144);
    },
    buf -> new BossRushSyncPayload(
      buf.readBoolean(),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readUtf(32767),
      buf.readInt(),
      buf.readUtf(262144)
    )
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
    return TYPE;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/compat/WitherReincarnatedCompat.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.compat;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.config.ModConfigEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLPaths;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.ModConfigSpec;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class WitherReincarnatedCompat {

  private static boolean enforcing = false;

  private WitherReincarnatedCompat() {}

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onModConfigLoad(ModConfigEvent.Loading event) {
    if ("witherreincarnated".equals(event.getConfig().getModId())) {
      enforceApiBoundMusicConfig();
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onModConfigReload(ModConfigEvent.Reloading event) {
    if ("witherreincarnated".equals(event.getConfig().getModId())) {
      enforceApiBoundMusicConfig();
    }
  }

  @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  public static void enforceApiBoundMusicConfig() {
    if (enforcing || !ModList.get().isLoaded("witherreincarnated")) return;
    enforcing = true;
    try {
      Class<?> cfgClass = Class.forName("com.alexander.witherreincarnated.config.WRClientConfig");
      ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<Double> musicVol = (ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<Double>) cfgClass.getField("music_volume").get(null);
      ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<Boolean> oneTrack = (ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<Boolean>) cfgClass.getField("only_play_one_music_track").get(null);
      ModConfigSpec spec = (ModConfigSpec) cfgClass.getField("SPEC").get(null);

      boolean changed = false;
      if (musicVol.get() != 0.0D) {
        musicVol.set(0.0D);
        changed = true;
      }
      if (oneTrack.get()) {
        oneTrack.set(false);
        changed = true;
      }
      if (changed) {
        spec.save();
      }

      annotateTomlFile();
      HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("[HexaCreateCore] Bound Wither: Reincarnated client music_volume = 0.0 (API bound).");
    } catch (Exception e) {
      HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.warn("[HexaCreateCore] Failed to bind Wither: Reincarnated client config", e);
    } finally {
      enforcing = false;
    }
  }

  private static void annotateTomlFile() {
    try {
      Path tomlPath = FMLPaths.CONFIGDIR.get().resolve("witherreincarnated-client.toml");
      if (!Files.exists(tomlPath)) return;

      String content = Files.readString(tomlPath, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      String tag = "# [API bound by HexaCreateCore: Managed by Boss Rush Audio Engine]";
      if (!content.contains(tag)) {
        content = content.replace("music_volume =", tag + "\nmusic_volume =");
        content = content.replace("only_play_one_music_track =", tag + "\nonly_play_one_music_track =");
        Files.writeString(tomlPath, content, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      }
    } catch (Exception ignored) {
    }
  }

  @EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
  public static final class ClientGuard {
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onScreenClose(net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent.Closing event) {
      enforceApiBoundMusicConfig();
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientTick(net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
      if ((++tickCounter % 20) == 0) {
        enforceApiBoundMusicConfig();
      }
    }
  }

}
