==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/components/IProceduralAnimConfig.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.components;

import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;

public interface IProceduralAnimConfig {
  /**
   * The minimum tick interval before triggering the random overlay animation.
   */
  int getMinRandomInterval();

  /**
   * The maximum tick interval before triggering the random overlay animation.
   */
  int getMaxRandomInterval();

  /**
   * The name of the continuous baseline looping animation (e.g., "breathing", "hovering").
   */
  default String getContinuousAmbientAnim() {
    return "breathing";
  }

  /**
   * The name of the rare, one-shot action animation to play at intervals (e.g., "scratching", "blink").
   */
  default String getRandomActionAnim() {
    return "blink";
  }

  /**
   * Computes the next randomized threshold block window dynamically.
   */
  default int getNextRandomTicks(RandomSource random) {
    int min = getMinRandomInterval();
    int max = getMaxRandomInterval();
    if (min >= max) return min;
    return min + random.nextInt(max - min + 1);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/AeronauticsAnchorHandler.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class AeronauticsAnchorHandler {

  public static final double ANCHOR_RANGE = 24.0D;
  private static final long EPOCH_NANOS = System.nanoTime();
  private static boolean loggedSubMethods = false;
  private static boolean loggedPadlockPos = false;

  private AeronauticsAnchorHandler() {}

  public static boolean tryAnchorSubLevelClient(Level level, Player player) {
    if (!level.isClientSide) return false;
    HitResult hit = player.pick(ANCHOR_RANGE, 1.0f, false);
    if (hit == null || hit.getType() == HitResult.Type.MISS) return false;

    Vec3 hitLocation = hit.getLocation();
    Object subLevel = getContainingClientSubLevel(level, hitLocation);
    if (subLevel == null) return false;

    return sendAnchorPacket(level, subLevel, hitLocation);
  }

  private static boolean sendAnchorPacket(Level level, Object subLevel, Vec3 hitLocation) {
    try {
      UUID subLevelId = (UUID) subLevel.getClass().getMethod("getUniqueId").invoke(subLevel);
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new WonderumAnchorPayload(subLevelId));

      for (int i = 0; 10 > i; i++) {
        level.addParticle(
          ParticleTypes.END_ROD,
          hitLocation.x, hitLocation.y, hitLocation.z,
          (level.random.nextDouble() - 0.5D) * 0.18D,
          (level.random.nextDouble() - 0.5D) * 0.18D,
          (level.random.nextDouble() - 0.5D) * 0.18D
        );
      }
      return true;
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      t.printStackTrace();
      return false;
    }
  }

  public static Object getContainingClientSubLevel(Level level, Vec3 hitLocation) {
    try {
      Class<?> sableClass = Class.forName("dev.ryanhcode.sable.Sable");
      Object helper = sableClass.getField("HELPER").get(null);
      for (Method m : helper.getClass().getMethods()) {
        if (m.getName().contains("Containing")) {
          List<Class<?>> params = Arrays.asList(m.getParameterTypes());
          if (params.size() == 1 && params.get(0).isAssignableFrom(Vec3.class)) {
            Object res = m.invoke(helper, hitLocation);
            if (res != null) return res;
          }
          if (params.size() == 2 && params.get(0).isAssignableFrom(Level.class) && params.get(1).isAssignableFrom(Vec3.class)) {
            Object res = m.invoke(helper, level, hitLocation);
            if (res != null) return res;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    return null;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientUseKey(InputEvent.InteractionKeyMappingTriggered event) {
    if (!event.isUseItem()) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;
    if (!mc.player.getItemInHand(event.getHand()).is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;

    if (tryAnchorSubLevelClient(mc.level, mc.player)) {
      event.setSwingHand(true);
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderWorld(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;
    if (!mc.player.getMainHandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get()) && !mc.player.getOffhandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;

    try {
      Class<?> simClientClass = Class.forName("dev.simulated_team.simulated.SimulatedClient");
      Object staffHandler = simClientClass.getField("PHYSICS_STAFF_CLIENT_HANDLER").get(null);
      Method getLocks = staffHandler.getClass().getDeclaredMethod("getLocks", Level.class);
      getLocks.setAccessible(true);
      @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
      List<UUID> locks = (List<UUID>) getLocks.invoke(staffHandler, mc.level);
      if (locks == null || locks.isEmpty()) return;

      Class<?> containerClass = Class.forName("dev.ryanhcode.sable.api.sublevel.SubLevelContainer");
      Object container = containerClass.getMethod("getContainer", Level.class).invoke(null, mc.level);
      Method getSubLevel = containerClass.getMethod("getSubLevel", UUID.class);

      Vec3 cam = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera.getPosition();
      PoseStack ps = event.getPoseStack();
      MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buf = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
      float timeSec = (float) ((System.nanoTime() - EPOCH_NANOS) * 1.0e-9D);

      for (UUID lockId : locks) {
        Object sub = getSubLevel.invoke(container, lockId);
        if (sub == null) continue;

        Vec3 worldPos = findOpenAirWorldAboveContraption(sub);
        if (!loggedPadlockPos) {
          loggedPadlockPos = true;
          mc.player.displayClientMessage(
            net.minecraft.network.chat.Component.literal("§b[PadlockPos] §f" + String.format("(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", worldPos.x, worldPos.y, worldPos.z)), false
          );
        }
        if (cam.distanceToSqr(worldPos) > 128.0D * 128.0D) continue;

        float bobY = (float) Math.sin(timeSec * 2.6f) * 0.08f;
        float spinDeg = (timeSec * 75.0f) % 360.0f;

        ps.pushPose();
        ps.translate(worldPos.x - cam.x, worldPos.y - cam.y + 0.45D + bobY, worldPos.z - cam.z);
        ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(spinDeg));

        draw3DPadlock(ps, buf.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID), 0.28f);
        ps.popPose();
      }
      buf.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      if (!loggedPadlockPos) {
        loggedPadlockPos = true;
        Throwable cause = (t.getCause() != null) ? t.getCause() : t;
        mc.player.displayClientMessage(
          net.minecraft.network.chat.Component.literal("§c[PadlockErr] §f" + cause.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + cause.getMessage()), false
        );
        cause.printStackTrace();
      }
    }
  }

  private static Vec3 findOpenAirWorldAboveContraption(Object sub) throws Exception {
    Class<?> clientSubIface = Class.forName("dev.ryanhcode.sable.sublevel.ClientSubLevel");
    Class<?> poseIface = Class.forName("dev.ryanhcode.sable.companion.math.Pose3dc");
    Class<?> vec3dcIface = Class.forName("org.joml.Vector3dc");

    Object pose = clientSubIface.getMethod("renderPose").invoke(sub);

    // 1. Read SubLevel-local pivot from Pose3dc#rotationPoint()
    Object rotPt = poseIface.getMethod("rotationPoint").invoke(pose);
    double lx = (double) vec3dcIface.getMethod("x").invoke(rotPt);
    double ly = (double) vec3dcIface.getMethod("y").invoke(rotPt);
    double lz = (double) vec3dcIface.getMethod("z").invoke(rotPt);

    // 2. Scan all non-air blocks around the SubLevel-local pivot to find exact (lenX/2, lenY/2, lenZ/2) center & top air block
    net.minecraft.world.level.BlockGetter subBlocks =
      (net.minecraft.world.level.BlockGetter) clientSubIface.getMethod("getLevel").invoke(sub);
    BlockPos seed = BlockPos.containing(lx, ly, lz);

    int minX = Integer.MAX_VALUE, minY = Integer.MAX_VALUE, minZ = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    int maxX = Integer.MIN_VALUE, maxY = Integer.MIN_VALUE, maxZ = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    boolean foundAny = false;

    for (int dx = -16; 16 >= dx; dx++) {
      for (int dy = -16; 16 >= dy; dy++) {
        for (int dz = -16; 16 >= dz; dz++) {
          BlockPos p = seed.offset(dx, dy, dz);
          if (!subBlocks.getBlockState(p).isAir()) {
            foundAny = true;
            if (minX > p.getX()) minX = p.getX();
            if (p.getX() > maxX) maxX = p.getX();
            if (minY > p.getY()) minY = p.getY();
            if (p.getY() > maxY) maxY = p.getY();
            if (minZ > p.getZ()) minZ = p.getZ();
            if (p.getZ() > maxZ) maxZ = p.getZ();
          }
        }
      }
    }

    double centerX = foundAny ? (minX + maxX + 1.0D) * 0.5D : lx;
    double midY = foundAny ? (minY + maxY + 1.0D) * 0.5D : ly;
    double centerZ = foundAny ? (minZ + maxZ + 1.0D) * 0.5D : lz;

    BlockPos check = BlockPos.containing(centerX, midY, centerZ);
    for (int step = 0; 64 > step; step++) {
      if (subBlocks.getBlockState(check).isAir()) {
        Vec3 localAirPos = new Vec3(centerX, check.getY() + 0.35D, centerZ);
        return (Vec3) poseIface.getMethod("transformPosition", Vec3.class).invoke(pose, localAirPos);
      }
      check = check.above();
    }

    Vec3 fallbackLocal = new Vec3(centerX, maxY + 1.35D, centerZ);
    return (Vec3) poseIface.getMethod("transformPosition", Vec3.class).invoke(pose, fallbackLocal);
  }

  private static void draw3DPadlock(PoseStack ps, VertexConsumer vc, float s) {
    // 1. 3D Padlock Body (Silver-Platinum block)
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(0.0f, -s * 0.325f, 0.0f);
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), s * 1.30f, s * 0.85f, s * 0.48f, 0xFFF8FAFC);
    ps.popPose();

    // 2. Dark Void Keyhole Recess passing through Front & Back faces
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(0.0f, -s * 0.33f, 0.0f);
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), s * 0.24f, s * 0.32f, s * 0.51f, 0xFF0F172A);
    ps.popPose();

    // 3. 3D Extruded Cyan U-Shackle (Left Pillar, Right Pillar, Top Arch)
    int shackleColor = 0xFF38BDF8;

    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(-s * 0.34f, s * 0.41f, 0.0f);
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), s * 0.20f, s * 0.62f, s * 0.24f, shackleColor);
    ps.popPose();

    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(s * 0.34f, s * 0.41f, 0.0f);
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), s * 0.20f, s * 0.62f, s * 0.24f, shackleColor);
    ps.popPose();

    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(0.0f, s * 0.65f, 0.0f);
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), s * 0.88f, s * 0.22f, s * 0.24f, shackleColor);
    ps.popPose();
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/blueprint/WonderumBlueprintDrafter.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.blueprint;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.hologram.BlueprintReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.hologram.MultiblockBlueprintData;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.hologram.MultiblockHologramRenderer;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.HologramProjectionPayload;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.SoundType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class WonderumBlueprintDrafter {

  private static int selectedIndex = 0;
  private static String selectedStructureId = null;

  private WonderumBlueprintDrafter() {}

  public record GhostTarget(BlockPos worldPos, BlockState expectedState) {}

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.NORMAL)
  public static void onSneakRightClickAir(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickItem event) {
    if (!event.getLevel().isClientSide) return;
    if (event.getHand() != InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND) return;
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    if (!event.getEntity().isShiftKeyDown()) return;

    List<String> wonderumIds = BlueprintReloadListener.getWonderumBlueprintIds();
    if (wonderumIds.isEmpty()) {
      event.getEntity().displayClientMessage(
        Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §7No blueprints found in §fblueprints/structures/wonderum/"), true
      );
      return;
    }

    selectedIndex = (selectedIndex + 1) % wonderumIds.size();
    selectedStructureId = wonderumIds.get(selectedIndex);

    MultiblockHologramRenderer.setProjection(new HologramProjectionPayload(selectedStructureId, BlockPos.ZERO, -1));
    event.getEntity().playNotifySound(SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.85f, 1.35f);
    event.getEntity().displayClientMessage(
      Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fBlueprint Selected: §e" + selectedStructureId + " §8(Right-click block to anchor)"), true
    );
    event.setCanceled(true);
    event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
  public static void onRightClickBlock(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickBlock event) {
    if (!event.getLevel().isClientSide) return;
    if (event.getHand() != InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND) return;
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;

    // 1. Anchoring a pending Wonderum blueprint hologram
    if (MultiblockHologramRenderer.isPendingPlacement() && selectedStructureId != null) {
      BlockPos anchor = event.getPos().relative(event.getFace() != null ? event.getFace() : net.minecraft.core.Direction.UP);
      MultiblockHologramRenderer.setProjection(new HologramProjectionPayload(selectedStructureId, anchor, 72000));
      event.getEntity().playNotifySound(SoundEvents.BEACON_ACTIVATE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.75f, 1.4f);
      event.getEntity().displayClientMessage(
        Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fHologram Anchored: §a" + selectedStructureId + " §8(Right-click ghost blocks to draft)"), true
      );
      event.setCanceled(true);
      event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
      return;
    }

    // 2. Pseudo-creative drafting when clicking directly on/through a projected ghost block
    if (!event.getEntity().isShiftKeyDown() && MultiblockHologramRenderer.getActiveStructureId() != null) {
      if (tryDraftGhostBlock(event.getLevel(), event.getEntity())) {
        event.setCanceled(true);
        event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
      }
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
  public static void onRightClickAirDraft(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickItem event) {
    if (!event.getLevel().isClientSide) return;
    if (event.getHand() != InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND) return;
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    if (event.getEntity().isShiftKeyDown()) return;
    if (MultiblockHologramRenderer.getActiveStructureId() == null) return;

    if (tryDraftGhostBlock(event.getLevel(), event.getEntity())) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
      event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;
    if (!mc.player.getMainHandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    if (MultiblockHologramRenderer.getActiveStructureId() == null) return;

    GhostTarget target = raycastGhostBlock(mc.level, mc.player, 10.0D);
    if (target != null) {
      Component blockName = target.expectedState().getBlock().getName();
      mc.player.displayClientMessage(
        Component.literal("§b[Draft] §fTarget: §e" + blockName.getString() + " §8(Right-click to place)"), true
      );
    }
  }

  private static boolean tryDraftGhostBlock(Level level, Player player) {
    GhostTarget target = raycastGhostBlock(level, player, 10.0D);
    if (target == null) return false;

    BlockState expected = target.expectedState();
    Item neededItem = expected.getBlock().asItem();
    if (!player.getAbilities().instabuild && !hasItemInInventory(player, neededItem)) {
      player.displayClientMessage(
        Component.literal("§c[Draft] §fMissing: §e" + expected.getBlock().getName().getString()), true
      );
      return true;
    }

    int blockStateId = net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block.getId(expected);
    PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new WonderumDraftBlockPayload(target.worldPos(), blockStateId));

    SoundType sound = expected.getSoundType(level, target.worldPos(), player);
    player.playNotifySound(sound.getPlaceSound(), SoundSource.BLOCKS, (sound.getVolume() + 1.0f) * 0.5f, sound.getPitch() * 0.9f);
    return true;
  }

  private static boolean hasItemInInventory(Player player, Item item) {
    for (int i = 0; player.getInventory().getContainerSize() > i; i++) {
      ItemStack stack = player.getInventory().getItem(i);
      if (!stack.isEmpty() && stack.is(item)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  public static GhostTarget raycastGhostBlock(Level level, Player player, double reach) {
    String structureId = MultiblockHologramRenderer.getActiveStructureId();
    BlockPos anchor = MultiblockHologramRenderer.getAnchorPos();
    if (structureId == null || anchor == null) return null;

    MultiblockBlueprintData bp = BlueprintReloadListener.getBlueprint(structureId);
    if (bp == null) return null;

    Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition(1.0f);
    Vec3 look = player.getViewVector(1.0f);
    int total = bp.blockCount();
    int[] offX = bp.offsetX();
    int[] offY = bp.offsetY();
    int[] offZ = bp.offsetZ();
    BlockState[] states = bp.states();

    GhostTarget best = null;
    double bestDistSq = reach * reach;

    for (int i = 0; total > i; i++) {
      BlockPos wp = anchor.offset(offX[i], offY[i], offZ[i]);
      BlockState worldState = level.getBlockState(wp);
      if (worldState.is(states[i].getBlock())) continue;

      net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB box = new net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB(wp);
      if (box.clip(eye, eye.add(look.scale(reach))).isPresent()) {
        double distSq = eye.distanceToSqr(Vec3.atCenterOf(wp));
        if (bestDistSq > distSq) {
          bestDistSq = distSq;
          best = new GhostTarget(wp, states[i]);
        }
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/blueprint/WonderumDraftBlockPayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.blueprint;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record WonderumDraftBlockPayload(BlockPos targetPos, int blockStateId) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public static final Type<WonderumDraftBlockPayload> TYPE = new Type<>(
    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "wonderum_draft_block")
  );

  public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, WonderumDraftBlockPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
    BlockPos.STREAM_CODEC, WonderumDraftBlockPayload::targetPos,
    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, WonderumDraftBlockPayload::blockStateId,
    WonderumDraftBlockPayload::new
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() { return TYPE; }

  public static void handleServer(WonderumDraftBlockPayload payload, IPayloadContext ctx) {
    ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
      if (!(ctx.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;
      if (!sp.getMainHandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;

      ServerLevel level = sp.serverLevel();
      BlockPos pos = payload.targetPos();
      if (!level.isLoaded(pos) || sp.distanceToSqr(pos.getX() + 0.5D, pos.getY() + 0.5D, pos.getZ() + 0.5D) > 144.0D) return;

      BlockState desired = Block.stateById(payload.blockStateId());
      if (desired.isAir()) return;

      BlockState existing = level.getBlockState(pos);
      if (!existing.isAir() && !existing.canBeReplaced()) return;

      if (!sp.getAbilities().instabuild) {
        Item needed = desired.getBlock().asItem();
        if (!consumeOne(sp, needed)) return;
      }

      level.setBlock(pos, desired, 3);
      level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.END_ROD, pos.getX() + 0.5D, pos.getY() + 0.5D, pos.getZ() + 0.5D, 8, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.02D);
    });
  }

  private static boolean consumeOne(ServerPlayer sp, Item item) {
    for (int i = 0; sp.getInventory().getContainerSize() > i; i++) {
      ItemStack stack = sp.getInventory().getItem(i);
      if (!stack.isEmpty() && stack.is(item)) {
        stack.shrink(1);
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.core.UUIDUtil;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.UUID;

public record WonderumAnchorPayload(UUID subLevelId) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public static final Type<WonderumAnchorPayload> TYPE = new Type<>(
    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "wonderum_anchor")
  );

  public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, WonderumAnchorPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
    UUIDUtil.STREAM_CODEC, WonderumAnchorPayload::subLevelId,
    WonderumAnchorPayload::new
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
    return TYPE;
  }

  public static void handleServer(WonderumAnchorPayload payload, IPayloadContext ctx) {
    ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
      if (!(ctx.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;
      if (!sp.getMainHandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get()) && !sp.getOffhandItem().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;

      ServerLevel level = sp.serverLevel();
      try {
        Class<?> handlerClass = Class.forName("dev.simulated_team.simulated.content.physics_staff.PhysicsStaffServerHandler");
        Object serverHandler = handlerClass.getMethod("get", ServerLevel.class).invoke(null, level);
        serverHandler.getClass().getMethod("toggleLock", UUID.class).invoke(serverHandler, payload.subLevelId());

        String statusMsg = "§b[Wonderum] §fSub-Level Spatial Anchor §aTOGGLED §7(" + payload.subLevelId().toString().substring(0, 8) + ")";
        try {
          Method isLockedMethod = serverHandler.getClass().getMethod("isLocked", UUID.class);
          boolean locked = (boolean) isLockedMethod.invoke(serverHandler, payload.subLevelId());
          statusMsg = locked ? "§b[Wonderum] §fSub-Level Spatial Anchor §aLOCKED" : "§b[Wonderum] §fSub-Level Spatial Anchor §eRELEASED";
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

        level.playSound(null, sp.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.BEACON_ACTIVATE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.75f, 1.35f);
        level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.END_ROD, sp.getX(), sp.getEyeY(), sp.getZ(), 12, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.04D);
        sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal(statusMsg), false);
      } catch (Throwable t) {
        Throwable cause = (t.getCause() != null) ? t.getCause() : t;
        sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§c[AnchorServerErr] §f" + cause.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + cause.getMessage()), false);
        cause.printStackTrace();
      }
    });
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/WonderumDepotHandler.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ItemParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.Recipe;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeType;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.SingleRecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.IItemHandler;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.ItemHandlerHelper;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class WonderumDepotHandler {

  private WonderumDepotHandler() {}

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onLeftClickDepot(PlayerInteractEvent.LeftClickBlock event) {
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    Level level = event.getLevel();
    BlockPos pos = event.getPos();
    ResourceLocation blockId = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(level.getBlockState(pos).getBlock());
    if (blockId == null || !blockId.getNamespace().equals("create") || !blockId.getPath().contains("depot")) return;

    // Cancel block-breaking & Create's normal left-click item extraction
    event.setCanceled(true);

    if (!level.isClientSide && level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
      if (pressDepotOnce(serverLevel, pos, event.getEntity())) {
        serverLevel.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.ANVIL_LAND, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.55f, 1.35f);
      }
    }
  }

  @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
  public static boolean pressDepotOnce(ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos, Player player) {
    BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
    if (be == null) return false;
    try {
      Class<?> behaviourTypeClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.foundation.blockEntity.behaviour.BehaviourType");
      Class<?> depotBehaviourClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.logistics.depot.DepotBehaviour");
      Object depotType = depotBehaviourClass.getField("TYPE").get(null);
      Object depotBehaviour = be.getClass().getMethod("getBehaviour", behaviourTypeClass).invoke(be, depotType);
      if (depotBehaviour == null) return false;

      ItemStack held = (ItemStack) depotBehaviourClass.getMethod("getHeldItemStack").invoke(depotBehaviour);
      if (held == null || held.isEmpty()) return false;

      ItemStack singleInput = held.copyWithCount(1);
      Class<?> allRecipeTypesClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.AllRecipeTypes");
      Object pressingEnum = Enum.valueOf((Class<Enum>) allRecipeTypesClass, "PRESSING");
      Method findMethod = allRecipeTypesClass.getMethod("find", RecipeInput.class, Level.class);

      Optional<RecipeHolder<?>> recipeOpt = (Optional<RecipeHolder<?>>) findMethod.invoke(pressingEnum, new SingleRecipeInput(singleInput), level);
      if (recipeOpt.isEmpty()) {
        Class<?> seqClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.processing.sequenced.SequencedAssemblyRecipe");
        Class<?> pressingRecipeClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.press.PressingRecipe");
        RecipeType<?> rType = (RecipeType<?>) allRecipeTypesClass.getMethod("getType").invoke(pressingEnum);
        recipeOpt = (Optional<RecipeHolder<?>>) seqClass.getMethod("getRecipe", Level.class, ItemStack.class, RecipeType.class, Class.class)
          .invoke(null, level, singleInput, rType, pressingRecipeClass);
      }
      if (recipeOpt.isEmpty()) return false;

      Class<?> recipeApplierClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.foundation.recipe.RecipeApplier");
      List<ItemStack> outputs = (List<ItemStack>) recipeApplierClass
        .getMethod("applyRecipeOn", Level.class, ItemStack.class, Recipe.class, boolean.class)
        .invoke(null, level, singleInput, recipeOpt.get().value(), true);
      if (outputs.isEmpty()) return false;

      // Spawn forge impact particles
      level.sendParticles(
        new ItemParticleOption(ParticleTypes.ITEM, singleInput),
        pos.getX() + 0.5D, pos.getY() + 0.92D, pos.getZ() + 0.5D,
        12, 0.14D, 0.08D, 0.14D, 0.06D
      );

      // Consume 1 input item
      held.shrink(1);
      boolean heldNowEmpty = held.isEmpty();
      if (heldNowEmpty) {
        depotBehaviourClass.getMethod("removeHeldItem").invoke(depotBehaviour);
      }

      // Access package-private processingOutputBuffer & TransportedItemStack safely
      var bufferField = depotBehaviourClass.getDeclaredField("processingOutputBuffer");
      bufferField.setAccessible(true);
      IItemHandler outputBuffer = (IItemHandler) bufferField.get(depotBehaviour);

      Class<?> transportedClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.belt.transport.TransportedItemStack");
      Method setCenteredMethod = depotBehaviourClass.getMethod("setCenteredHeldItem", transportedClass);

      for (ItemStack out : outputs) {
        if (out.isEmpty()) continue;
        if (heldNowEmpty) {
          Object transportedOut = transportedClass.getConstructor(ItemStack.class).newInstance(out);
          setCenteredMethod.invoke(depotBehaviour, transportedOut);
          heldNowEmpty = false;
        } else {
          ItemStack remainder = ItemHandlerHelper.insertItemStacked(outputBuffer, out, false);
          if (!remainder.isEmpty() && (player == null || !player.getInventory().add(remainder))) {
            Block.popResource(level, pos.above(), remainder);
          }
        }
      }

      be.getClass().getMethod("notifyUpdate").invoke(be);
      return true;
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      t.printStackTrace();
      return false;
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/WonderumItemRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.BlackHoleDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks.PortalShaderHook;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.BlockEntityWithoutLevelRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.ItemRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.resources.model.BakedModel;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemDisplayContext;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;

public final class WonderumItemRenderer extends BlockEntityWithoutLevelRenderer {

  public static final WonderumItemRenderer INSTANCE = new WonderumItemRenderer();
  private static final long EPOCH_NANOS = System.nanoTime();

  private WonderumItemRenderer() {
    super(Minecraft.getInstance().getBlockEntityRenderDispatcher(), Minecraft.getInstance().getEntityModels());
  }

  @Override
  public void renderByItem(ItemStack stack, ItemDisplayContext displayContext, PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, int packedLight, int packedOverlay) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    ItemRenderer itemRenderer = mc.getItemRenderer();
    BakedModel baseModel = mc.getModelManager().getModel(WonderumClientEvents.WONDERUM_BASE_MODEL);
    boolean leftHand = displayContext == ItemDisplayContext.FIRST_PERSON_LEFT_HAND || displayContext == ItemDisplayContext.THIRD_PERSON_LEFT_HAND;

    // 1. Render static Wonderum hammer model
    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
    itemRenderer.render(stack, displayContext, leftHand, poseStack, bufferSource, packedLight, packedOverlay, baseModel);
    poseStack.popPose();

    // 2. Align with the item display transform and translate to the hollow chamber [1.0, 14.5, 1.0] / 16.0
    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
    baseModel.applyTransform(displayContext, poseStack, leftHand);
    poseStack.translate(-0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f);
    poseStack.translate(1.0f / 16.0f, 14.5f / 16.0f, 1.0f / 16.0f);

    // 3. Billboard the silver-and-black singularity shader toward the camera
    if (displayContext != ItemDisplayContext.GUI) {
      Quaternionf inversePoseRot = new Quaternionf();
      poseStack.last().pose().getNormalizedRotation(inversePoseRot).conjugate();
      poseStack.mulPose(inversePoseRot);
      poseStack.mulPose(mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().cameraOrientation());
    }

    float timeSec = (float) ((System.nanoTime() - EPOCH_NANOS) * 1.0e-9D);
    PortalShaderHook.bind(BlackHoleDictionary.SILVER_SINGULARITY)
      .size(0.115f, 0.115f)
      .resolution(40, 8)
      .render(poseStack, bufferSource, timeSec);

    poseStack.popPose();
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.BlackHoleDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks.PortalShaderHook;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.volumetrics.dictionaries.BlackHoleVolumetricDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.volumetrics.dictionaries.SiftRiftVolumetricDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.volumetrics.hooks.VolumetricHook;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class WormholeClientRenderer {

  private static final long EPOCH_NANOS = System.nanoTime();
  private static List<WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode> activeNodes = new ArrayList<>();
  private static WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode pendingNode = null;

  private WormholeClientRenderer() {}

  public static void updateSyncState(List<WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode> nodes, WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode pending) {
    activeNodes = nodes;
    pendingNode = pending;
  }

  public static boolean hasPendingAnchor() {
    return pendingNode != null;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderWorld(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;
    if (activeNodes.isEmpty() && pendingNode == null) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;

    Vec3 cam = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera.getPosition();
    PoseStack ps = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buf = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    float timeSec = (float) ((System.nanoTime() - EPOCH_NANOS) * 1.0e-9D);

    for (WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode node : activeNodes) {
      renderFaceWormhole(ps, buf, cam, node.pos(), node.face(), 1.05f, 0.36f, timeSec);
    }

    if (pendingNode != null) {
      renderFaceWormhole(ps, buf, cam, pendingNode.pos(), pendingNode.face(), 0.72f, 0.22f, timeSec);
    }
  }

  private static void renderFaceWormhole(
    PoseStack ps, MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buf, Vec3 cam,
    BlockPos pos, Direction face, float discSize, float crownDepth, float timeSec
  ) {
    double fx = pos.getX() + 0.5D + face.getStepX() * 0.506D;
    double fy = pos.getY() + 0.5D + face.getStepY() * 0.506D;
    double fz = pos.getZ() + 0.5D + face.getStepZ() * 0.506D;
    if (cam.distanceToSqr(fx, fy, fz) > 96.0D * 96.0D) return;

    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(fx - cam.x, fy - cam.y, fz - cam.z);
    orientToFace(ps, face);


    // 1. Face-aligned square relativistic accretion halo at z = 0.0f
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.05f);
    PortalShaderHook.bind(BlackHoleDictionary.SILVER_SINGULARITY)
      .size(discSize * 0.88f, discSize * 0.88f)
      .resolution(36, 8)
      .flushBatch()
      .render(ps, buf, timeSec);
    ps.popPose();

    float coreHalf = discSize * 0.59f;
    // 2. 3D Pitch-Black Superellipsoid Core + Rim-Lit Silver Photon Horizon protruding cleanly at z = 0.04f
    VolumetricHook.bind(BlackHoleVolumetricDictionary.SILVER_SINGULARITY)
      .at(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.04f)
      .coreSize(coreHalf, coreHalf, 0.11f)
      .eventHorizon(0.095f, 5)
      .render(ps, buf, timeSec);

    // 3. Sift-Rift translucent white wobbling crown hugging the 3D core's perimeter
    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    RenderSystem.disableCull();
    RenderSystem.depthMask(false);

    //VertexConsumer vc = buf.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    //float crownHalf = coreHalf + 0.075f;
    // 3. True 3D Volumetric Sift-Rift Crown wrapping the black-hole perimeter (inner wall, outer wall, and front 3D cap)
    VolumetricHook.bind(SiftRiftVolumetricDictionary.WHITE_RIFT)
      .at(0.0f, 0.0f, -0.005f)
      .coreSize(coreHalf * 0.915f, coreHalf * 0.915f, 0.0f)
      .renderCrown(ps, buf, 0.095f, -crownDepth, 6, timeSec);
    buf.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);

    RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
    RenderSystem.enableCull();
    ps.popPose();
  }

  private static void orientToFace(PoseStack ps, Direction face) {
    switch (face) {
      case SOUTH -> {}
      case NORTH -> ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(180.0f));
      case EAST  -> ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(90.0f));
      case WEST  -> ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(-90.0f));
      case UP    -> ps.mulPose(Axis.XP.rotationDegrees(-90.0f));
      case DOWN  -> ps.mulPose(Axis.XP.rotationDegrees(90.0f));
    }
  }

  private static void drawSiftRiftCrown(
    VertexConsumer vc, Matrix4f m,
    float halfSize, float maxDepth, float zStart,
    float timeSec, int peakOpacity, int endOpacity
  ) {
    final int segments = 40;

    for (int i = 0; segments > i; i++) {
      float t0 = (float) (i * Math.PI * 2.0D / segments);
      float t1 = (float) ((i + 1) * Math.PI * 2.0D / segments);

      float c0 = (float) Math.cos(t0);
      float s0 = (float) Math.sin(t0);
      float sqX0 = (float) (Math.signum(c0) * Math.pow(Math.abs(c0), 0.32D));
      float sqY0 = (float) (Math.signum(s0) * Math.pow(Math.abs(s0), 0.32D));

      float c1 = (float) Math.cos(t1);
      float s1 = (float) Math.sin(t1);
      float sqX1 = (float) (Math.signum(c1) * Math.pow(Math.abs(c1), 0.32D));
      float sqY1 = (float) (Math.signum(s1) * Math.pow(Math.abs(s1), 0.32D));

      float wave0 = (float) (Math.sin(timeSec * 4.4D + t0 * 4.0D) * 0.55D + Math.cos(timeSec * 3.1D - t0 * 6.0D) * 0.45D);
      float wave1 = (float) (Math.sin(timeSec * 4.4D + t1 * 4.0D) * 0.55D + Math.cos(timeSec * 3.1D - t1 * 6.0D) * 0.45D);

      float rIn0 = halfSize + wave0 * 0.016f;
      float rIn1 = halfSize + wave1 * 0.016f;

      float rMid0 = halfSize * 1.03f + wave0 * 0.022f;
      float rMid1 = halfSize * 1.03f + wave1 * 0.022f;

      float rOut0 = halfSize * 1.12f + wave0 * 0.036f;
      float rOut1 = halfSize * 1.12f + wave1 * 0.036f;

      float zMid0 = zStart - 0.032f;
      float zMid1 = zStart - 0.032f;

      float zOut0 = zStart - maxDepth * (0.82f + 0.25f * wave0);
      float zOut1 = zStart - maxDepth * (0.82f + 0.25f * wave1);

      // Band 1: Smooth 0 -> peakOpacity ramp right out of the event horizon
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX0 * rIn0,  sqY0 * rIn0,  zStart, 255, 255, 255, 0);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX1 * rIn1,  sqY1 * rIn1,  zStart, 255, 255, 255, 0);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX1 * rMid1, sqY1 * rMid1, zMid1,  255, 255, 255, peakOpacity);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX0 * rMid0, sqY0 * rMid0, zMid0,  255, 255, 255, peakOpacity);

      // Band 2: Outer Sift-rift extrusion (peakOpacity -> endOpacity)
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX0 * rMid0, sqY0 * rMid0, zMid0,  255, 255, 255, peakOpacity);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX1 * rMid1, sqY1 * rMid1, zMid1,  255, 255, 255, peakOpacity);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX1 * rOut1, sqY1 * rOut1, zOut1,  255, 255, 255, endOpacity);
      RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, sqX0 * rOut0, sqY0 * rOut0, zOut0,  255, 255, 255, endOpacity);
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/wormhole/WormholeKineticBridge.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class WormholeKineticBridge {

  public record DrivenState(float speed, float perRpmCapacity) {}

  private static final Map<ResourceKey<Level>, Map<BlockPos, DrivenState>> DRIVEN_NODES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

  private WormholeKineticBridge() {}

  public static void setDriven(ResourceKey<Level> dim, BlockPos pos, float speed, float perRpmCapacity) {
    DRIVEN_NODES.computeIfAbsent(dim, k -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>())
      .put(pos.immutable(), new DrivenState(speed, perRpmCapacity));
  }

  public static void clearDriven(ResourceKey<Level> dim, BlockPos pos) {
    Map<BlockPos, DrivenState> map = DRIVEN_NODES.get(dim);
    if (map != null) {
      map.remove(pos);
    }
  }

  public static DrivenState getDriven(Level level, BlockPos pos) {
    if (level == null || level.isClientSide) return null;
    Map<BlockPos, DrivenState> map = DRIVEN_NODES.get(level.dimension());
    return (map != null) ? map.get(pos) : null;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/wormhole/WormholeManager.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class WormholeManager {

  public static final double MIN_SAFE_DISTANCE = 10.0D;

  private WormholeManager() {}

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
  public static void onSneakRightClickBlock(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickBlock event) {
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    if (!event.getEntity().isShiftKeyDown()) return;

    Level level = event.getLevel();
    BlockPos pos = event.getPos();
    Direction face = event.getFace() != null ? event.getFace() : Direction.UP;

    boolean isKinetic = isCreateKineticBlockEntity(level.getBlockEntity(pos));
    if (level.isClientSide) {
      if (isKinetic || WormholeClientRenderer.hasPendingAnchor()) {
        event.setCanceled(true);
        event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;
    ServerLevel serverLevel = sp.serverLevel();
    WormholeSavedData data = WormholeSavedData.get(serverLevel.getServer());
    WormholeSavedData.Endpoint pending = data.getPending(sp.getUUID());

    if (!isKinetic && pending == null) return;

    event.setCanceled(true);
    event.setCancellationResult(InteractionResult.CONSUME);

    ResourceKey<Level> dim = serverLevel.dimension();
    WormholeSavedData.Endpoint clicked = new WormholeSavedData.Endpoint(dim, pos.immutable(), face);

    if (pending == null) {
      data.setPending(sp.getUUID(), clicked);
      serverLevel.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.END_PORTAL_FRAME_FILL, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.85f, 1.35f);
      serverLevel.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.END_ROD, pos.getX() + 0.5D, pos.getY() + 0.5D, pos.getZ() + 0.5D, 16, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.25D, 0.04D);
      sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fWormhole Anchor §dA §fbound on §7" + face.getName().toUpperCase() + " §8— Sneak+Right-Click target node (>10 blocks away)"), true);
      syncAllPlayers(serverLevel.getServer());
      return;
    }

    if (pending.dimension().equals(dim) && pending.pos().equals(pos)) {
      data.clearPending(sp.getUUID());
      serverLevel.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.BEACON_DEACTIVATE, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.7f, 0.9f);
      sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fPending Wormhole Anchor §eCLEARED"), true);
      syncAllPlayers(serverLevel.getServer());
      return;
    }

    if (pending.dimension().equals(dim)) {
      double distSq = pending.pos().distSqr(pos);
      if (MIN_SAFE_DISTANCE * MIN_SAFE_DISTANCE >= distSq) {
        data.clearPending(sp.getUUID());
        triggerSpatialImplosion(serverLevel, pending.pos(), pos);
        sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§c[Wonderum] §4SPATIAL IMPLOSION! §fWormhole anchors cannot coexist within §e10 blocks§f!"), true);
        syncAllPlayers(serverLevel.getServer());
        return;
      }
    }

    ServerLevel pendingLevel = serverLevel.getServer().getLevel(pending.dimension());
    if (pendingLevel != null && WormholeKineticBridge.getDriven(pendingLevel, pending.pos()) != null) {
      releaseDrivenNode(pendingLevel, pending.pos());
    }
    if (WormholeKineticBridge.getDriven(serverLevel, pos) != null) {
      releaseDrivenNode(serverLevel, pos);
    }

    //releaseDrivenNode(serverLevel, pos);

    data.clearPending(sp.getUUID());
    data.addPair(pending, clicked);
    serverLevel.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.END_PORTAL_SPAWN, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.7f, 1.45f);
    serverLevel.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.REVERSE_PORTAL, pos.getX() + 0.5D, pos.getY() + 0.5D, pos.getZ() + 0.5D, 24, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.08D);
    sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fGravitational Wormhole §aLINKED §8(" + pending.dimension().location().getPath() + " ↔ " + dim.location().getPath() + ")"), true);
    syncAllPlayers(serverLevel.getServer());
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
  public static void onSneakLeftClickBlock(PlayerInteractEvent.LeftClickBlock event) {
    if (!event.getItemStack().is(ModItems.WONDERUM.get())) return;
    if (!event.getEntity().isShiftKeyDown()) return;
    if (event.getLevel().isClientSide || !(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;

    ServerLevel serverLevel = sp.serverLevel();
    MinecraftServer server = serverLevel.getServer();
    WormholeSavedData data = WormholeSavedData.get(server);
    ResourceKey<Level> dim = serverLevel.dimension();
    BlockPos pos = event.getPos();

    WormholeSavedData.WormholePair targetPair = null;
    for (WormholeSavedData.WormholePair pair : data.getPairs()) {
      if (pair.matches(dim, pos)) {
        targetPair = pair;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (targetPair != null && data.removeAt(dim, pos)) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
      ServerLevel levelA = server.getLevel(targetPair.a().dimension());
      ServerLevel levelB = server.getLevel(targetPair.b().dimension());
      if (levelA != null) releaseDrivenNode(levelA, targetPair.a().pos());
      if (levelB != null) releaseDrivenNode(levelB, targetPair.b().pos());

      serverLevel.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.BEACON_DEACTIVATE, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.8f, 0.8f);
      sp.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§b[Wonderum] §fGravitational Wormhole §eCOLLAPSED"), true);
      syncAllPlayers(server);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
    MinecraftServer server = event.getServer();
    WormholeSavedData data = WormholeSavedData.get(server);
    if (data.getPairs().isEmpty()) return;

    boolean changed = false;
    Iterator<WormholeSavedData.WormholePair> it = data.getPairs().iterator();
    while (it.hasNext()) {
      WormholeSavedData.WormholePair pair = it.next();
      ServerLevel levelA = server.getLevel(pair.a().dimension());
      ServerLevel levelB = server.getLevel(pair.b().dimension());
      if (levelA == null || levelB == null) continue;

      if (pair.a().dimension().equals(pair.b().dimension()) && MIN_SAFE_DISTANCE * MIN_SAFE_DISTANCE >= pair.a().pos().distSqr(pair.b().pos())) {
        it.remove();
        data.setDirty();
        changed = true;
        releaseDrivenNode(levelA, pair.a().pos());
        releaseDrivenNode(levelB, pair.b().pos());
        triggerSpatialImplosion(levelA, pair.a().pos(), pair.b().pos());
        continue;
      }

      if (!levelA.isLoaded(pair.a().pos()) || !levelB.isLoaded(pair.b().pos())) continue;

      BlockEntity beA = levelA.getBlockEntity(pair.a().pos());
      BlockEntity beB = levelB.getBlockEntity(pair.b().pos());
      if (beA == null || beB == null) {
        it.remove();
        data.setDirty();
        changed = true;
        releaseDrivenNode(levelA, pair.a().pos());
        releaseDrivenNode(levelB, pair.b().pos());
        continue;
      }

      bridgeKineticRotation(beA, beB);
    }

    if (changed) syncAllPlayers(server);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerLoginOrChangeDim(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedInEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) syncPlayer(sp);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerChangedDim(PlayerEvent.PlayerChangedDimensionEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) syncPlayer(sp);
  }

  public static void triggerSpatialImplosion(ServerLevel level, BlockPos posA, BlockPos posB) {
    Vec3 center = Vec3.atCenterOf(posA);
    level.playSound(null, posA, SoundEvents.GENERIC_EXPLODE.value(), SoundSource.BLOCKS, 1.4f, 0.55f);
    level.playSound(null, posA, SoundEvents.RESPAWN_ANCHOR_DEPLETE.value(), SoundSource.BLOCKS, 1.2f, 0.5f);
    level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.EXPLOSION_EMITTER, center.x, center.y, center.z, 2, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.0D);
    level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.REVERSE_PORTAL, center.x, center.y, center.z, 60, 1.5D, 1.5D, 1.5D, 0.15D);

    AABB pullBox = new AABB(posA).inflate(7.0D);
    for (LivingEntity entity : level.getEntitiesOfClass(LivingEntity.class, pullBox)) {
      Vec3 pullVec = center.subtract(entity.position()).normalize().scale(1.15D);
      entity.setDeltaMovement(entity.getDeltaMovement().add(pullVec.x, 0.45D, pullVec.z));
      entity.hurtMarked = true;
      entity.hurt(level.damageSources().magic(), 8.0f);
    }
  }

  private static boolean isCreateKineticBlockEntity(BlockEntity be) {
    if (be == null) return false;
    for (Class<?> c = be.getClass(); c != null; c = c.getSuperclass()) {
      if (c.getName().equals("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.base.KineticBlockEntity")) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private static void releaseDrivenNode(ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos) {
    WormholeKineticBridge.clearDriven(level.dimension(), pos);
    BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
    if (be == null) return;
    try {
      Class<?> kbeClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.base.KineticBlockEntity");
      if (!kbeClass.isInstance(be)) return;
      kbeClass.getMethod("detachKinetics").invoke(be);
      kbeClass.getMethod("setSpeed", float.class).invoke(be, 0.0f);
      kbeClass.getMethod("setNetwork", Long.class).invoke(be, (Long) null);
      kbeClass.getMethod("sendData").invoke(be);
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
  }

  private static void bridgeKineticRotation(BlockEntity beA, BlockEntity beB) {
    try {
      Class<?> kbeClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.base.KineticBlockEntity");
      Class<?> netClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.KineticNetwork");
      if (!kbeClass.isInstance(beA) || !kbeClass.isInstance(beB)) return;

      Level levelA = beA.getLevel();
      Level levelB = beB.getLevel();
      if (levelA == null || levelB == null) return;

      boolean aDriven = WormholeKineticBridge.getDriven(levelA, beA.getBlockPos()) != null;
      boolean bDriven = WormholeKineticBridge.getDriven(levelB, beB.getBlockPos()) != null;

      Method getSpeed = kbeClass.getMethod("getSpeed");
      float speedA = (float) getSpeed.invoke(beA);
      float speedB = (float) getSpeed.invoke(beB);

      BlockEntity driver = beA;
      BlockEntity receiver = beB;
      float driverSpeed = speedA;

      if (aDriven && !bDriven) {
        driver = beB;
        receiver = beA;
        driverSpeed = speedB;
      } else if (!aDriven && !bDriven && Math.abs(speedB) > Math.abs(speedA)) {
        driver = beB;
        receiver = beA;
        driverSpeed = speedB;
      }

      Level recvLevel = receiver.getLevel();
      BlockPos recvPos = receiver.getBlockPos();
      if (recvLevel == null) return;

      Object driverNet = kbeClass.getMethod("getOrCreateNetwork").invoke(driver);
      float driverCapacity = driverNet != null ? (float) netClass.getMethod("calculateCapacity").invoke(driverNet) : 0.0f;
      float perRpmCap = Math.abs(driverSpeed) > 0.01f ? (driverCapacity / Math.abs(driverSpeed)) : 0.0f;

      WormholeKineticBridge.DrivenState prev = WormholeKineticBridge.getDriven(recvLevel, recvPos);
      boolean changed = prev == null
      || Math.abs(prev.speed() - driverSpeed) > 0.01f
      || Math.abs(prev.perRpmCapacity() - perRpmCap) > 0.05f;

      if (Math.abs(driverSpeed) > 0.01f) {
        WormholeKineticBridge.setDriven(recvLevel.dimension(), recvPos, driverSpeed, perRpmCap);
      } else {
        WormholeKineticBridge.clearDriven(recvLevel.dimension(), recvPos);
      }

      if (changed) {
        Method detachKinetics = kbeClass.getMethod("detachKinetics");
        Method attachKinetics = kbeClass.getMethod("attachKinetics");
        Method setSpeed = kbeClass.getMethod("setSpeed", float.class);
        Method setNetwork = kbeClass.getMethod("setNetwork", Long.class);
        Method getOrCreateNetwork = kbeClass.getMethod("getOrCreateNetwork");
        Method sendData = kbeClass.getMethod("sendData");

        detachKinetics.invoke(receiver);
        setSpeed.invoke(receiver, driverSpeed);

        if (Math.abs(driverSpeed) > 0.01f) {
          setNetwork.invoke(receiver, recvPos.asLong());
          attachKinetics.invoke(receiver);

          Class<?> propClass = Class.forName("com.simibubi.create.content.kinetics.RotationPropagator");
          propClass.getMethod("handleAdded", Level.class, BlockPos.class, kbeClass)
            .invoke(null, recvLevel, recvPos, receiver);

          Object recvNet = getOrCreateNetwork.invoke(receiver);
          if (recvNet != null) {
            netClass.getMethod("updateCapacityFor", kbeClass, float.class)
              .invoke(recvNet, receiver, perRpmCap);
            netClass.getMethod("updateStress").invoke(recvNet);
          }
        } else {
          setNetwork.invoke(receiver, (Long) null);
        }

        sendData.invoke(receiver);
        var bs = recvLevel.getBlockState(recvPos);
        recvLevel.sendBlockUpdated(recvPos, bs, bs, 3);
        recvLevel.updateNeighborsAt(recvPos, bs.getBlock());
      }
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
  }

  public static void syncAllPlayers(MinecraftServer server) {
    for (ServerPlayer sp : server.getPlayerList().getPlayers()) syncPlayer(sp);
  }

  public static void syncPlayer(ServerPlayer sp) {
    WormholeSavedData data = WormholeSavedData.get(sp.serverLevel().getServer());
    ResourceKey<Level> dim = sp.serverLevel().dimension();
    List<WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode> nodes = new ArrayList<>();
    for (WormholeSavedData.WormholePair pair : data.getPairs()) {
      if (pair.a().dimension().equals(dim)) nodes.add(new WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode(pair.a().pos(), pair.a().face()));
      if (pair.b().dimension().equals(dim)) nodes.add(new WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode(pair.b().pos(), pair.b().face()));
    }
    WormholeSavedData.Endpoint pending = data.getPending(sp.getUUID());
    boolean hasPendingInDim = pending != null && pending.dimension().equals(dim);
    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(sp, new WormholeSyncPayload(
      nodes,
      hasPendingInDim,
      hasPendingInDim ? new WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode(pending.pos(), pending.face()) : new WormholeSyncPayload.SyncNode(BlockPos.ZERO, Direction.UP)
    ));
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/wormhole/WormholeSavedData.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.SavedData;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class WormholeSavedData extends SavedData {

  public record Endpoint(ResourceKey<Level> dimension, BlockPos pos, Direction face) {
    public CompoundTag save() {
      CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
      tag.putString("Dim", dimension.location().toString());
      tag.putLong("Pos", pos.asLong());
      tag.putString("Face", face.getName());
      return tag;
    }

    public static Endpoint load(CompoundTag tag) {
      ResourceKey<Level> dim = ResourceKey.create(Registries.DIMENSION, ResourceLocation.parse(tag.getString("Dim")));
      BlockPos pos = BlockPos.of(tag.getLong("Pos"));
      Direction face = Direction.byName(tag.getString("Face"));
      return new Endpoint(dim, pos, face != null ? face : Direction.UP);
    }
  }

  public record WormholePair(UUID id, Endpoint a, Endpoint b) {
    public boolean matches(ResourceKey<Level> dim, BlockPos pos) {
      return (a.dimension().equals(dim) && a.pos().equals(pos))
          || (b.dimension().equals(dim) && b.pos().equals(pos));
    }
  }

  private static final String DATA_NAME = "hexacreatecore_wonderum_wormholes";
  private final List<WormholePair> pairs = new ArrayList<>();
  private final Map<UUID, Endpoint> pendingAnchors = new HashMap<>();

  public WormholeSavedData() {}

  public static WormholeSavedData get(MinecraftServer server) {
    ServerLevel overworld = server.overworld();
    return overworld.getDataStorage().computeIfAbsent(
      new Factory<>(WormholeSavedData::new, WormholeSavedData::load, null),
      DATA_NAME
    );
  }

  public List<WormholePair> getPairs() { return pairs; }
  public Endpoint getPending(UUID playerId) { return pendingAnchors.get(playerId); }

  public void setPending(UUID playerId, Endpoint endpoint) {
    pendingAnchors.put(playerId, endpoint);
    setDirty();
  }

  public void clearPending(UUID playerId) {
    if (pendingAnchors.remove(playerId) != null) setDirty();
  }

  public void addPair(Endpoint a, Endpoint b) {
    removeAt(a.dimension(), a.pos());
    removeAt(b.dimension(), b.pos());
    pairs.add(new WormholePair(UUID.randomUUID(), a, b));
    setDirty();
  }

  public boolean removeAt(ResourceKey<Level> dim, BlockPos pos) {
    boolean removed = pairs.removeIf(p -> p.matches(dim, pos));
    if (removed) setDirty();
    return removed;
  }

  public static WormholeSavedData load(CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.Provider registries) {
    WormholeSavedData data = new WormholeSavedData();
    ListTag pairList = tag.getList("Pairs", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
    for (int i = 0; pairList.size() > i; i++) {
      CompoundTag entry = pairList.getCompound(i);
      UUID id = entry.hasUUID("Id") ? entry.getUUID("Id") : UUID.randomUUID();
      data.pairs.add(new WormholePair(id, Endpoint.load(entry.getCompound("A")), Endpoint.load(entry.getCompound("B"))));
    }
    ListTag pendingList = tag.getList("Pending", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
    for (int i = 0; pendingList.size() > i; i++) {
      CompoundTag entry = pendingList.getCompound(i);
      if (entry.hasUUID("Player")) {
        data.pendingAnchors.put(entry.getUUID("Player"), Endpoint.load(entry.getCompound("Endpoint")));
      }
    }
    return data;
  }

  @Override
  public CompoundTag save(CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.Provider registries) {
    ListTag pairList = new ListTag();
    for (WormholePair p : pairs) {
      CompoundTag entry = new CompoundTag();
      entry.putUUID("Id", p.id());
      entry.put("A", p.a().save());
      entry.put("B", p.b().save());
      pairList.add(entry);
    }
    tag.put("Pairs", pairList);

    ListTag pendingList = new ListTag();
    for (Map.Entry<UUID, Endpoint> e : pendingAnchors.entrySet()) {
      CompoundTag entry = new CompoundTag();
      entry.putUUID("Player", e.getKey());
      entry.put("Endpoint", e.getValue().save());
      pendingList.add(entry);
    }
    tag.put("Pending", pendingList);
    return tag;
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/create/wonderum/wormhole/WormholeSyncPayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public record WormholeSyncPayload(
  List<SyncNode> activeNodesInDim,
  boolean hasPending,
  SyncNode pendingNode
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public record SyncNode(BlockPos pos, Direction face) {
    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, SyncNode> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
      BlockPos.STREAM_CODEC, SyncNode::pos,
      Direction.STREAM_CODEC, SyncNode::face,
      SyncNode::new
    );
  }

  public static final Type<WormholeSyncPayload> TYPE = new Type<>(
    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "wonderum_wormhole_sync")
  );

  public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, WormholeSyncPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
    SyncNode.STREAM_CODEC.apply(ByteBufCodecs.list()), WormholeSyncPayload::activeNodesInDim,
    ByteBufCodecs.BOOL, WormholeSyncPayload::hasPending,
    SyncNode.STREAM_CODEC, WormholeSyncPayload::pendingNode,
    WormholeSyncPayload::new
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() { return TYPE; }

  public static void handleClient(WormholeSyncPayload payload, IPayloadContext ctx) {
    ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
      WormholeClientRenderer.updateSyncState(
        new ArrayList<>(payload.activeNodesInDim()),
        payload.hasPending() ? payload.pendingNode() : null
      );
    });
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.METHOD)
public @interface DialogueAction {
  /** The full action ID, e.g. "hexacreatecore:start_forging" */
  String value();

  /** Set to true for player-only actions that skip the 6-block NPC reach check */
  boolean selfTargeted() default false;
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.threedishapes_hooks.ThreeDiShapeHooks;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.threedishapes_hooks.UiShapeTarget;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.LightTexture;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class Difficulty3DRingRenderer {
  private static Vec3 smoothedRingCenter = null;

  private static final TextEffectStyle ETHEREAL_TITLE_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF38BDF8, 0xFFBAE6FD, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFF38BDF8),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    3.2f, 0.45f, 0.32f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM,
    0x7738BDF8, 0x44E0F2FE, false, false, false
  );

  private static final TextEffectStyle ABSOLUTION_TITLE_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF140724, 0xFF581C87, 0xFF9333EA, 0xFF140724),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    2.0f, 0.70f, 0.35f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM,
    0x889333EA, 0x55E9D5FF, false, false, true
  );

  private static final TextEffectStyle GENESIS_TITLE_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFFFF1E56, 0xFF8B0000, 0xFFDC2626, 0xFFFF1E56),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    2.4f, 0.95f, 0.40f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH,
    0x99FF1E56, 0x99660011, false, true, false
  );

  private static final TextEffectStyle ETHEREAL_DESC_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF21B7D1, 0xFFBAE6FD, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFF21B7D1),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    1.6f, 0.0f, 0.25f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.NONE,
    0, 0, false, false, false
  );

  // Absolution Description: Same Purple/Dark-Violet gradient as title, zero wobble
  private static final TextEffectStyle ABSOLUTION_DESC_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF3B0764, 0xFF7E22CE, 0xFFD8B4FE, 0xFF7E22CE, 0xFF3B0764),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.25f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f),
    1.8f, 0.0f, 0.25f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.NONE,
    0, 0, false, false, false
  );

  // Genesis Description: Crimson & Blood-Red Chromatic Glitch (4th image style) with tiny readable wobble (0.25f)
  private static final TextEffectStyle GENESIS_DESC_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFFFF1E56, 0xFF8B0000, 0xFFDC2626, 0xFFFF1E56),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    2.2f, 0.25f, 0.35f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH,
    0x88FF1E56, 0x88660011, true, false, false
  );


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderWorld(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;
    if (!DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen) {
      smoothedRingCenter = null;
      return;
    }

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) {
      smoothedRingCenter = null;
      return;
    }

    List<DifficultyTier> tiers = new ArrayList<>();
    tiers.add(DifficultyTier.ETHEREAL);
    tiers.add(DifficultyTier.ABSOLUTION);
    if (DifficultyClientState.genesisUnlocked) {
      tiers.add(DifficultyTier.GENESIS);
    }

    int count = tiers.size();
    double stepAngleRad = (Math.PI * 2.0D) / count;
    double baseRad = Math.toRadians(DifficultyClientState.selectorBaseYaw);
    double radius = 2.8D;

    Vec3 camPos = event.getCamera().getPosition();
    Vec3 playerEye = mc.player.getEyePosition(event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(true));
    Vec3 lookVec = mc.player.getViewVector(1.0f).normalize();

    float dt = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
    double linearAlpha = Math.min(1.0D, dt * 4.5D);
    double sineEase = Math.sin(linearAlpha * (Math.PI / 2.0D));

    if (smoothedRingCenter == null || smoothedRingCenter.distanceToSqr(playerEye) > 100.0D) {
      smoothedRingCenter = playerEye;
    } else {
      smoothedRingCenter = smoothedRingCenter.lerp(playerEye, sineEase);
    }

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    Font font = mc.font;

    String bestHover = "";
    double bestDot = 0.90D;

    for (int i = 0; count > i; i++) {
      DifficultyTier tier = tiers.get(i);
      double angle = baseRad + (i * stepAngleRad);
      double dx = -Math.sin(angle) * radius;
      double dz = Math.cos(angle) * radius;

      Vec3 nodeWorldPos = new Vec3(smoothedRingCenter.x + dx, smoothedRingCenter.y + 0.1D, smoothedRingCenter.z + dz);
      Vec3 toNode = nodeWorldPos.subtract(playerEye).normalize();
      double dot = lookVec.dot(toNode);
      if (dot > bestDot) {
        bestDot = dot;
        bestHover = tier.id;
      }

      boolean isHovered = tier.id.equals(DifficultyClientState.hoveredTierId);
      boolean isSelected = tier == DifficultyClientState.activeTier;

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(nodeWorldPos.x - camPos.x, nodeWorldPos.y - camPos.y, nodeWorldPos.z - camPos.z);

      float faceYaw = (float) Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(smoothedRingCenter.x - nodeWorldPos.x, smoothedRingCenter.z - nodeWorldPos.z)) + 180.0f;
      poseStack.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(faceYaw));

      float scale = isHovered ? 0.0145f : 0.0125f;
      poseStack.scale(-scale, -scale, -scale);

      int borderColor = isHovered ? 0xFFFFFFFF : (isSelected ? 0xFFFFD700 : tier.accentColor);
      int bgColor = isHovered ? 0xEE1A1429 : 0xCC110E1A;

      // 1. Difficulty Name Box at eye level
      String title = (isSelected ? "✦ " : "") + tier.displayName + (isHovered ? " [Right-Click]" : "");
      FormattedCharSequence titleSeq = Component.literal("§l" + title).getVisualOrderText();
      float titleW = Math.max(110.0f, font.width(titleSeq) + 26.0f);
      float titleH = font.lineHeight + 12.0f;

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
        titleW, titleH, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        bgColor, borderColor, List.of(), false
      );

      ThreeDiShapeHooks.renderAttachedShapes(
        poseStack, bufferSource, UiShapeTarget.DIFFICULTY_CARD, tier.id,
        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, titleW, titleH
      );

      // 2. List of Changes floating directly above the Difficulty Name Box
      List<String> changes = DifficultyClientState.TIER_DESCRIPTIONS.getOrDefault(tier.id, List.of("No description configured."));
      List<FormattedCharSequence> descLines = new ArrayList<>();
      int maxDescW = 140;
      for (String line : changes) {
        for (FormattedCharSequence seq : font.split(Component.literal("§7• §f" + line), 210)) {
          descLines.add(seq);
          maxDescW = Math.max(maxDescW, font.width(seq) + 22);
        }
      }

      float boxGap = (tier == DifficultyTier.GENESIS) ? 42.0f : 8.0f;
      float descH = (descLines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;
      float descCenterY = -(titleH * 0.5f) - boxGap - (descH * 0.5f);

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        0.0f, descCenterY, 0.0f,
        (float) maxDescW, descH, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0xCC0D0B14, tier.accentColor, List.of(), false
      );

      ThreeDiShapeHooks.renderAttachedShapes(
        poseStack, bufferSource, UiShapeTarget.DIFFICULTY_DESCRIPTION, tier.id,
        0.0f, descCenterY, 0.0f, (float) maxDescW, descH
      );

      // Draw crisp front-facing text 3.5 units in front of the extruded box face (depth = 3.0)
      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 3.5f);

      String prefix = isSelected ? "✦ " : "";
      String tierTitle = prefix + tier.displayName;
      String suffix = isHovered ? " [Right-Click]" : "";

      TextEffectStyle titleStyle = switch (tier) {
        case ETHEREAL -> ETHEREAL_TITLE_STYLE;
        case ABSOLUTION -> ABSOLUTION_TITLE_STYLE;
        case GENESIS -> GENESIS_TITLE_STYLE;
        default -> ETHEREAL_TITLE_STYLE;
      };

      var boldTierComp = Component.literal(tierTitle).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
      var boldSuffixComp = Component.literal(suffix).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
      float tierW = font.width(boldTierComp);
      float suffixW = font.width(boldSuffixComp);
      float titleX = -(tierW + suffixW) * 0.5f;
      float titleY = -font.lineHeight * 0.5f;

      ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3DBold(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font, tierTitle, titleX, titleY, titleStyle
      );
      if (!suffix.isEmpty()) {
        font.drawInBatch(
          boldSuffixComp, titleX + tierW, titleY, 0xFFFFFFFF, false,
          poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT
        );
      }

      TextEffectStyle descStyle = switch (tier) {
        case ETHEREAL -> ETHEREAL_DESC_STYLE;
        case ABSOLUTION -> ABSOLUTION_DESC_STYLE;
        case GENESIS -> GENESIS_DESC_STYLE;
        default -> ETHEREAL_DESC_STYLE;
      };

      float startY = descCenterY - (descLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);
      for (int l = 0; descLines.size() > l; l++) {
        FormattedCharSequence lineSeq = descLines.get(l);
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        lineSeq.accept((idx, s, cp) -> {
          sb.appendCodePoint(cp);
          return true;
        });
        String plainLine = sb.toString();
        float lineX = -font.width(plainLine) * 0.5f;
        ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
          poseStack, bufferSource, font, plainLine, lineX, startY + (l * font.lineHeight), descStyle
        );
      }

      poseStack.popPose();
      poseStack.popPose();
    }
    DifficultyClientState.hoveredTierId = bestHover;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/DifficultyAtmosphereController.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class DifficultyAtmosphereController {

  private static DifficultyTier activeEffectTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
  private static long effectStartMs = 0L;

  private DifficultyAtmosphereController() {}

  public static void trigger(DifficultyTier tier) {
    if (tier == null || tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) {
      activeEffectTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
      effectStartMs = 0L;
