      return;
    }
    activeEffectTier = tier;
    effectStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
  }

  private record AtmosphereFrame(
    float activeAlpha,
    int skyRgb,
    int fogRgb,
    float fogDistanceScale, // 1.0f = normal, 0.15f = 15% of normal/DistantHorizons distance
    float veinVignetteAlpha
  ) {}

  private static AtmosphereFrame sampleFrame() {
    if (activeEffectTier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL || effectStartMs <= 0L) {
      return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    float sec = (System.currentTimeMillis() - effectStartMs) / 1000.0f;
    if (sec < 0.0f) return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);

    if (activeEffectTier == DifficultyTier.ETHEREAL) {
      // 2s fade in -> 2s hold -> 1s fade out (5.0s total)
      if (sec >= 5.0f) {
        activeEffectTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
        return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);
      }
      float a = (2.0f > sec) ? (sec / 2.0f) : ((4.0f > sec) ? 1.0f : (1.0f - ((sec - 4.0f) / 1.0f)));
      a = smoothStep(Mth.clamp(a, 0.0f, 1.0f));
      return new AtmosphereFrame(a, 0x38BDF8, 0x00E5FF, Mth.lerp(a, 1.0f, 0.38f), 0.0f);
    }

    if (activeEffectTier == DifficultyTier.ABSOLUTION) {
      // 3s fade in -> 2s hold -> 1s pale out (6.0s total)
      if (sec >= 6.0f) {
        activeEffectTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
        return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);
      }
      float a = (3.0f > sec) ? (sec / 3.0f) : ((5.0f > sec) ? 1.0f : (1.0f - ((sec - 5.0f) / 1.0f)));
      a = smoothStep(Mth.clamp(a, 0.0f, 1.0f));
      return new AtmosphereFrame(a, 0x2A0845, 0x09080C, Mth.lerp(a, 1.0f, 0.28f), 0.0f);
    }

    if (activeEffectTier == DifficultyTier.GENESIS) {
      // 0..10s: normal -> blood red sky + bright Genesis red close fog
      // 10..20s: blood red sky -> pitch black + bright red fog -> blood red
      // 20..25s: sky/fog recover + Heart Attack vein vignette (1s fade-in, 3s heartbeat pulse, 1s fade-out)
      if (sec >= 25.0f) {
        activeEffectTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
        return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);
      }

      if (10.0f > sec) {
        float t = smoothStep(sec / 10.0f);
        return new AtmosphereFrame(t, 0x7F0019, 0xFF1E56, Mth.lerp(t, 1.0f, 0.18f), 0.0f);
      } else if (20.0f > sec) {
        float t = smoothStep((sec - 10.0f) / 10.0f);
        int sky = lerpRgb(0x7F0019, 0x030002, t);
        int fog = lerpRgb(0xFF1E56, 0x660011, t);
        return new AtmosphereFrame(1.0f, sky, fog, Mth.lerp(t, 0.18f, 0.14f), 0.0f);
      } else {
        float sub = sec - 20.0f; // 0.0 -> 5.0s
        float envAlpha = 1.0f - smoothStep(sub / 5.0f);

        float veinBase;
        if (1.0f > sub) {
          veinBase = sub; // 1s fade in
        } else if (4.0f > sub) {
          // 3s violent double-heartbeat pulse ("lub-dub" every 1.0s)
          float beatPhase = (sub - 1.0f) % 1.0f;
          float lub = (float) Math.exp(-Math.pow((beatPhase - 0.12f) / 0.08f, 2.0D));
          float dub = (float) Math.exp(-Math.pow((beatPhase - 0.32f) / 0.08f, 2.0D)) * 0.85f;
          veinBase = Mth.clamp(0.62f + 0.38f * Math.max(lub, dub), 0.0f, 1.0f);
        } else {
          veinBase = 1.0f - (sub - 4.0f); // 1s fade out
        }
        return new AtmosphereFrame(envAlpha, 0x030002, 0x660011, Mth.lerp(envAlpha, 1.0f, 0.14f), Mth.clamp(veinBase, 0.0f, 1.0f));
      }
    }

    return new AtmosphereFrame(0.0f, 0, 0, 1.0f, 0.0f);
  }

  /** Called by ClientLevelMixin to tint the sky dome color smoothly */
  public static Vec3 applySkyColorTint(Vec3 baseSky) {
    AtmosphereFrame f = sampleFrame();
    if (0.001f >= f.activeAlpha) return baseSky;
    float tr = ((f.skyRgb >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    float tg = ((f.skyRgb >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    float tb = (f.skyRgb & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    return new Vec3(
      Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, baseSky.x, tr),
      Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, baseSky.y, tg),
      Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, baseSky.z, tb)
    );
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onFogColor(ViewportEvent.ComputeFogColor event) {
    AtmosphereFrame f = sampleFrame();
    if (0.001f >= f.activeAlpha) return;
    float tr = ((f.fogRgb >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    float tg = ((f.fogRgb >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    float tb = (f.fogRgb & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
    event.setRed(Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, event.getRed(), tr));
    event.setGreen(Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, event.getGreen(), tg));
    event.setBlue(Mth.lerp(f.activeAlpha, event.getBlue(), tb));
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderFog(ViewportEvent.RenderFog event) {
    AtmosphereFrame f = sampleFrame();
    if (0.001f >= f.activeAlpha || f.fogDistanceScale >= 0.999f) return;

    // Percentage-based fog compression (scales proportionally with any render distance / Distant Horizons)
    float near = event.getNearPlaneDistance() * f.fogDistanceScale;
    float far = Math.max(6.0f, event.getFarPlaneDistance() * f.fogDistanceScale);
    event.setNearPlaneDistance(near);
    event.setFarPlaneDistance(far);
    event.setCanceled(true);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderHeartAttackVignette(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
    AtmosphereFrame f = sampleFrame();
    if (0.005f >= f.veinVignetteAlpha) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    int w = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
    int h = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();
    float alpha = f.veinVignetteAlpha;

    GuiGraphics gui = event.getGuiGraphics();
    PoseStack ps = gui.pose();
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 350.0f);

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();
    RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);

    Tesselator tess = Tesselator.getInstance();
    BufferBuilder buf = tess.begin(VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);
    Matrix4f mat = ps.last().pose();

    int edgeAlpha = Math.round(215 * alpha);
    float borderX = w * 0.24f;
    float borderY = h * 0.24f;

    // 1. Deep Crimson Screen-Edge Vignette Frame (Top, Bottom, Left, Right)
    addGradientQuad(buf, mat, 0, 0, w, 0, w, borderY, 0, borderY, 0x66000D, edgeAlpha, 0x330005, 0);
    addGradientQuad(buf, mat, 0, h - borderY, w, h - borderY, w, h, 0, h, 0x330005, 0, 0x66000D, edgeAlpha);
    addGradientQuad(buf, mat, 0, 0, borderX, 0, borderX, h, 0, h, 0x66000D, edgeAlpha, 0x330005, 0);
    addGradientQuad(buf, mat, w - borderX, 0, w, 0, w, h, w - borderX, h, 0x330005, 0, 0x66000D, edgeAlpha);

    // 2. Branching Blood-Red Capillary Tendrils creeping inward from screen corners & edges
    int tendrilCount = 36;
    for (int i = 0; tendrilCount > i; i++) {
      double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0D * i) / tendrilCount;
      float cos = (float) Math.cos(angle);
      float sin = (float) Math.sin(angle);

      // Anchor on screen border
      float sx = (w * 0.5f) + cos * (w * 0.52f);
      float sy = (h * 0.5f) + sin * (h * 0.52f);
      float reach = (0.22f + 0.14f * (float) Math.sin(i * 2.7D)) * alpha;

      float curX = sx;
      float curY = sy;
      for (int seg = 0; 5 > seg; seg++) {
        float jitter = (float) Math.sin((i * 1.9D) + (seg * 2.3D)) * 12.0f;
        float nextX = curX - (cos * w * (reach / 5.0f)) - (sin * jitter);
        float nextY = curY - (sin * h * (reach / 5.0f)) + (cos * jitter);
        float thick = (5.5f - seg * 0.9f);
        float px = -sin * thick * 0.5f;
        float py = cos * thick * 0.5f;

        int a0 = Math.max(0, Math.round((230 - seg * 40) * alpha));
        int a1 = Math.max(0, Math.round((190 - seg * 42) * alpha));
        addGradientQuad(buf, mat, curX - px, curY - py, curX + px, curY + py, nextX + px * 0.5f, nextY + py * 0.5f, nextX - px * 0.5f, nextY - py * 0.5f, 0xB8001F, a0, 0xFF1E56, a1);

        curX = nextX;
        curY = nextY;
      }
    }

    BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buf.buildOrThrow());
    RenderSystem.enableDepthTest();
    ps.popPose();
  }

  private static void addGradientQuad(BufferBuilder b, Matrix4f m, float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, float x3, float y3, int rgb0, int a0, int rgb1, int a1) {
    int r0 = (rgb0 >> 16) & 0xFF, g0 = (rgb0 >> 8) & 0xFF, bl0 = rgb0 & 0xFF;
    int r1 = (rgb1 >> 16) & 0xFF, g1 = (rgb1 >> 8) & 0xFF, bl1 = rgb1 & 0xFF;
    b.addVertex(m, x0, y0, 0).setColor(r0, g0, bl0, a0);
    b.addVertex(m, x1, y1, 0).setColor(r0, g0, bl0, a0);
    b.addVertex(m, x2, y2, 0).setColor(r1, g1, bl1, a1);
    b.addVertex(m, x3, y3, 0).setColor(r1, g1, bl1, a1);
  }

  private static float smoothStep(float x) {
    return x * x * (3.0f - 2.0f * x);
  }

  private static int lerpRgb(int c0, int c1, float t) {
    int r = Mth.lerpInt(t, (c0 >> 16) & 0xFF, (c1 >> 16) & 0xFF);
    int g = Mth.lerpInt(t, (c0 >> 8) & 0xFF, (c1 >> 8) & 0xFF);
    int b = Mth.lerpInt(t, c0 & 0xFF, c1 & 0xFF);
    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/DifficultyBannerHud.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class DifficultyBannerHud {

  private static final long TOTAL_DURATION_MS = 6700L; // 350ms slide-in + 6000ms stay + 350ms slide-out
  private static final long SLIDE_MS = 350L;

  private static final TextEffectStyle ETHEREAL_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF38BDF8, 0xFFBAE6FD, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFF38BDF8),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    3.2f, 0.45f, 0.32f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM,
    0x7738BDF8, 0x44E0F2FE, false, false, false
  );

  private static final TextEffectStyle ABSOLUTION_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFF140724, 0xFF581C87, 0xFF9333EA, 0xFF140724),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
    2.0f, 0.70f, 0.35f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM,
    0x889333EA, 0x55E9D5FF, false, false, true
  );

  private static final TextEffectStyle GENESIS_STYLE = new TextEffectStyle(
    List.of(0xFFFF1E56, 0xFF8B0000, 0xFFDC2626, 0xFFFF1E56),
    List.of(0.0f, 0.35f, 0x7f / 255.0f, 1.0f),
    2.4f, 0.95f, 0.40f,
    TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH,
    0x99FF1E56, 0x99660011, false, true, false
  );

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderBanner(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
    if (DifficultyClientState.difficultyBannerText.isEmpty()) return;

    long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - DifficultyClientState.difficultyBannerStartMs;
    if (elapsed >= TOTAL_DURATION_MS) {
      DifficultyClientState.difficultyBannerText = "";
      return;
    }

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    Font font = mc.font;
    int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
    float screenCenterX = screenWidth * 0.5f;

    var boldComp = Component.literal(DifficultyClientState.difficultyBannerText).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
    int maxTextW = Math.min(280, screenWidth - 40);
    List<FormattedCharSequence> seqs = font.split(boldComp, maxTextW);

    List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(seqs.size());
    int widest = 0;
    for (FormattedCharSequence seq : seqs) {
      widest = Math.max(widest, font.width(seq));
      StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
      seq.accept((idx, st, cp) -> {
        sb.appendCodePoint(cp);
        return true;
      });
      lines.add(sb.toString());
    }

    float lineStep = font.lineHeight + 4.0f;
    float boxWidth = widest + 26.0f;
    float boxHeight = (lines.size() * lineStep) + 10.0f;

    long remaining = TOTAL_DURATION_MS - elapsed;
    float slideProgress = 1.0f;
    if (SLIDE_MS > elapsed) {
      slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(((float) elapsed / SLIDE_MS) * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
    } else if (SLIDE_MS > remaining) {
      slideProgress = (float) Math.sin(((float) remaining / SLIDE_MS) * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
    }

    float targetCenterY = 48.0f + (boxHeight * 0.5f);
    float offscreenCenterY = -(boxHeight * 0.5f) - 10.0f;
    float centerY = Mth.lerp(slideProgress, offscreenCenterY, targetCenterY);

    DifficultyTier tier = DifficultyClientState.difficultyBannerTier;
    TextEffectStyle textStyle = switch (tier) {
      case ETHEREAL -> ETHEREAL_STYLE;
      case ABSOLUTION -> ABSOLUTION_STYLE;
      case GENESIS -> GENESIS_STYLE;
      default -> ETHEREAL_STYLE;
    };

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getGuiGraphics().pose();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();

    RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, bufferSource, font,
      screenCenterX, centerY, 0.0f,
      boxWidth, boxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
      0xCC110E1A, tier.accentColor, List.of(), true
    );

    bufferSource.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    bufferSource.endBatch();
    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();

    float startY = centerY - (lines.size() * lineStep * 0.5f) + 2.0f;
    for (int i = 0; lines.size() > i; i++) {
      String line = lines.get(i);
      var lineBold = Component.literal(line).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
      float lx = screenCenterX - (font.width(lineBold) * 0.5f);
      ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2DBold(
        event.getGuiGraphics(), font, line, lx, startY + (i * lineStep), textStyle
      );
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/DifficultyClientState.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyProfile;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public final class DifficultyClientState {
  public static DifficultyTier activeTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
  public static boolean genesisUnlocked = false;
  public static int riposteCharges = 0;
  public static boolean ripostePrimed = false;
  public static long parryFlowEndsAtMs = 0L;
  public static final Map<String, List<String>> TIER_DESCRIPTIONS = new HashMap<>();
  public static final Map<String, HealthBarStyle> TIER_GAUGE_STYLES = new HashMap<>();

  // 500ms (0.5s) cubic-bezier(.35, 0, .65, 1) parry sweep state
  private static final float SWEEP_DURATION_MS = 500.0f;
  public static boolean isHoldingParry = false;
  public static long parryChargeStartMs = 0L;
  public static String lastResultLabel = "";
  public static int lastResultColor = 0xFFFFFFFF;
  public static float lockedCursorProgress = -1.0f;
  public static long resultDisplayUntilMs = 0L;

  // 3D Ring Difficulty Selector state
  public static boolean selectorOpen = false;
  public static float selectorBaseYaw = 0.0f;
  public static String hoveredTierId = "";
  public static boolean flowTapPending = false;
  public static long flowWindowEndsAtMs = 0L;

  public record ParrySoundEntry(String soundId, float volume, float pitch) {}
  public static final Map<String, ParrySoundEntry> PARRY_SOUNDS = new HashMap<>(Map.of(
    "red", new ParrySoundEntry("minecraft:block.amethyst_block.chime", 0.7f, 0.8f),
    "orange", new ParrySoundEntry("minecraft:block.amethyst_block.break", 0.8f, 1.0f),
    "green", new ParrySoundEntry("minecraft:block.amethyst_block.resonate", 0.9f, 1.2f),
    "white", new ParrySoundEntry("minecraft:block.amethyst_cluster.fall", 1.0f, 1.4f)
  ));

  private static boolean hasInitialTierSync = false;
  public static long difficultyBannerStartMs = 0L;
  public static String difficultyBannerText = "";
  public static DifficultyTier difficultyBannerTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;

  public static void handleSync(DifficultyPackets.SyncPayload payload) {
    DifficultyTier nextTier = DifficultyTier.fromId(payload.activeTierId());
    if (hasInitialTierSync && nextTier != activeTier && nextTier != DifficultyTier.NORMAL) {
      DifficultyAtmosphereController.trigger(nextTier);
      difficultyBannerTier = nextTier;
      difficultyBannerStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
      difficultyBannerText = switch (nextTier) {
        case ETHEREAL -> "A fragment of your mind remains, but for how long...";
        case ABSOLUTION -> "Your soul is now bound to the world... You do not know what awaits you";
        case GENESIS -> "Your wretched soul will know no rest... Your last breath is near...";
        default -> "";
      };
    }
    hasInitialTierSync = true;
    activeTier = nextTier;

    genesisUnlocked = payload.genesisUnlocked();
    riposteCharges = payload.riposteCharges();
    ripostePrimed = payload.ripostePrimed();
    parryFlowEndsAtMs = (payload.parryFlowRemainingMs() > 0L)
      ? (System.currentTimeMillis() + payload.parryFlowRemainingMs()) : 0L;

    if (payload.descriptionsJson() != null && !payload.descriptionsJson().isEmpty()) {
      Type mapType = new TypeToken<Map<String, List<String>>>() {}.getType();
      Map<String, List<String>> parsed = new Gson().fromJson(payload.descriptionsJson(), mapType);
      if (parsed != null) {
        TIER_DESCRIPTIONS.clear();
        TIER_DESCRIPTIONS.putAll(parsed);
      }
    }

    if (payload.gaugeBarsJson() != null && !payload.gaugeBarsJson().isEmpty() && !"{}".equals(payload.gaugeBarsJson())) {
      try {
        var obj = JsonParser.parseString(payload.gaugeBarsJson()).getAsJsonObject();
        TIER_GAUGE_STYLES.clear();
        TIER_GAUGE_STYLES.putAll(BossRushReloadListener.parseHealthBarsMap(obj));
      } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    if (payload.parryFlowRemainingMs() > 0L) {
      parryFlowEndsAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + payload.parryFlowRemainingMs();
      flowTapPending = false;
    } else {
      parryFlowEndsAtMs = 0L;
      flowTapPending = false;
    }

    if (payload.parrySoundsJson() != null && !payload.parrySoundsJson().isEmpty() && !"{}".equals(payload.parrySoundsJson())) {
      try {
        var root = JsonParser.parseString(payload.parrySoundsJson()).getAsJsonObject();
        for (String key : List.of("red", "orange", "green", "white")) {
          if (!root.has(key)) continue;
          var elem = root.get(key);
          if (elem.isJsonPrimitive()) {
            PARRY_SOUNDS.put(key, new ParrySoundEntry(elem.getAsString(), 0.9f, 1.0f));
          } else if (elem.isJsonObject()) {
            var obj = elem.getAsJsonObject();
            String id = obj.has("sound") ? obj.get("sound").getAsString() : PARRY_SOUNDS.get(key).soundId();
            float vol = obj.has("volume") ? obj.get("volume").getAsFloat() : 0.9f;
            float pit = obj.has("pitch") ? obj.get("pitch").getAsFloat() : 1.0f;
            PARRY_SOUNDS.put(key, new ParrySoundEntry(id, vol, pit));
          }
        }
      } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
  }


  public static HealthBarStyle getActiveGaugeStyle() {
    return TIER_GAUGE_STYLES.getOrDefault(activeTier.id, DifficultyProfile.defaultGauge(activeTier.accentColor));
  }

  public static boolean isInParryFlow() {
    return parryFlowEndsAtMs > System.currentTimeMillis();
  }

  /** Evaluates CSS cubic-bezier(0.35, 0.0, 0.65, 1.0) for normalized elapsed time x in [0, 1] */
  public static float evalCubicBezier(float x) {
    if (0.0f >= x) return 0.0f;
    if (x >= 1.0f) return 1.0f;
    // Binary search parameter u in [0, 1] such that Bx(u) == x, with x1=0.35, x2=0.65
    float lo = 0.0f, hi = 1.0f, u = x;
    for (int i = 0; 12 > i; i++) {
      u = 0.5f * (lo + hi);
      float inv = 1.0f - u;
      float bx = 3.0f * inv * inv * u * 0.35f + 3.0f * inv * u * u * 0.65f + u * u * u;
      if (x > bx) lo = u;
      else hi = u;
    }
    // Evaluate By(u) with y1=0.0, y2=1.0 -> 3*(1-u)*u^2 + u^3
    float inv = 1.0f - u;
    return 3.0f * inv * u * u + u * u * u;
  }

  public static float getSweepProgress() {
    if (!isHoldingParry) return lockedCursorProgress;
    float linearT = Math.min(1.0f, Math.max(0.0f, (System.currentTimeMillis() - parryChargeStartMs) / SWEEP_DURATION_MS));
    return evalCubicBezier(linearT);
  }

  public static void onParryKeyPressed() {
    if (activeTier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL || selectorOpen) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;

    if (isInParryFlow()) {
      if (flowTapPending) return;
      isHoldingParry = false;
      flowTapPending = true;
      flowWindowEndsAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 400L;
      lockedCursorProgress = 0.5f;
      lastResultLabel = "FLOW PARRY";
      lastResultColor = 0xFF21B7D1;
      resultDisplayUntilMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 300L;
      playParrySound("white");
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("PARRY_WINDOW", "FLOW", 400));
      return;
    }

    if (!isHoldingParry) {
      isHoldingParry = true;
      parryChargeStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
      lockedCursorProgress = -1.0f;
    }
  }

  public static void onParryKeyReleased() {
    if (!isHoldingParry) return;
    float p = getSweepProgress();
    isHoldingParry = false;
    lockedCursorProgress = p;
    evaluateRelease(p);
  }

  public static void playParrySound(String zoneKey) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;
    ParrySoundEntry entry = PARRY_SOUNDS.getOrDefault(zoneKey, PARRY_SOUNDS.get("red"));
    net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation loc = net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation.tryParse(entry.soundId());
    if (loc != null) {
      net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent se = net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.containsKey(loc)
      ? net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.get(loc)
      : net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent.createVariableRangeEvent(loc);
      mc.player.playSound(se, entry.volume(), entry.pitch());
    }
  }

  public static void tickParryAutoFail() {
    long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
    if (isHoldingParry && (now - parryChargeStartMs) >= (long) SWEEP_DURATION_MS) {
      isHoldingParry = false;
      lockedCursorProgress = 1.0f;
      evaluateRelease(1.0f);
    }
    if (flowTapPending && now > flowWindowEndsAtMs) {
      flowTapPending = false;
      parryFlowEndsAtMs = 0L;
      playParrySound("red");
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("BREAK_FLOW", "", 0));
    }
  }

  private static void evaluateRelease(float p) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;

    String zone;
    int windowMs;
    if (p >= 0.45f && 0.55f >= p) {
      zone = "PURE";
      windowMs = 400;
      lastResultLabel = "PURE PARRY";
      lastResultColor = 0xFFFFFFFF;
      playParrySound("white");
    } else if ((p >= 0.30f && 0.45f > p) || (p > 0.55f && 0.70f >= p)) {
      zone = "PARRY";
      windowMs = 400;
      lastResultLabel = "PARRY";
      lastResultColor = 0xFF4ADE80;
      playParrySound("green");
    } else if ((p >= 0.10f && 0.30f > p) || (p > 0.70f && 0.90f >= p)) {
      zone = "SHALLOW";
      windowMs = 400;
      lastResultLabel = "SHALLOW";
      lastResultColor = 0xFFFB923C;
      playParrySound("orange");
    } else {
      zone = "FAIL";
      windowMs = 0;
      lastResultLabel = "FAILED";
      lastResultColor = 0xFFEF4444;
      playParrySound("red");
    }

    resultDisplayUntilMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 450L;
    PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("PARRY_WINDOW", zone, windowMs));
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/DifficultyHudRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiLayerEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.gui.VanillaGuiLayers;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class DifficultyHudRenderer {
  private static final Random SHAKE_RNG = new Random();
  private static boolean hasCamHistory = false;
  private static float lastYaw = 0.0f;
  private static float lastPitch = 0.0f;
  private static float lagX = 0.0f;
  private static float lagY = 0.0f;

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderHud(RenderGuiLayerEvent.Post event) {
    if (!event.getName().equals(VanillaGuiLayers.EXPERIENCE_BAR)) return;
    DifficultyTier tier = DifficultyClientState.activeTier;
    if (tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;

    GuiGraphics gui = event.getGuiGraphics();
    Font font = mc.font;
    int screenW = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
    int screenH = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();
    long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

    // 1. Top-Left Riposte Gauge (shorter 96px bar, zero text, gradient shader + shake when full/primed)
    int maxCharges = Math.max(1, tier.riposteMaxCharges);
    float fillRatio = Math.min(1.0f, DifficultyClientState.riposteCharges / (float) maxCharges);
    boolean isFull = DifficultyClientState.riposteCharges >= maxCharges;
    boolean isPrimed = DifficultyClientState.ripostePrimed;

    float shakeAmp = isPrimed ? 2.4f : (isFull ? 1.2f : 0.0f);
    float shakeX = (shakeAmp > 0.0f) ? ((SHAKE_RNG.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f) * shakeAmp) : 0.0f;
    float shakeY = (shakeAmp > 0.0f) ? ((SHAKE_RNG.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f) * shakeAmp) : 0.0f;

    float curYaw = mc.player.getYRot();
    float curPitch = mc.player.getXRot();
    if (!hasCamHistory) {
      lastYaw = curYaw;
      lastPitch = curPitch;
      hasCamHistory = true;
    } else {
      float dYaw = Mth.wrapDegrees(curYaw - lastYaw);
      float dPitch = curPitch - lastPitch;
      lastYaw = curYaw;
      lastPitch = curPitch;
      lagX = Mth.clamp(lagX - (dYaw * 0.28f), -10.0f, 10.0f);
      lagY = Mth.clamp(lagY - (dPitch * 0.28f), -8.0f, 8.0f);
      float dt = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
      float ease = (float) Math.sin(Math.min(1.0D, dt * 3.5D) * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      lagX = Mth.lerp(ease, lagX, 0.0f);
      lagY = Mth.lerp(ease, lagY, 0.0f);
    }

    double timeSec = (now % 600000L) / 1000.0D;
    float bobX = (float) Math.sin(timeSec * 1.6D) * 1.5f;
    float bobY = (float) Math.cos(timeSec * 2.1D) * 1.8f;

    HealthBarStyle gStyle = DifficultyClientState.getActiveGaugeStyle();
    float gaugeW = 96.0f;
    float gaugeH = 11.0f;
    float gaugeCenterX = 12.0f + (gaugeW * 0.5f) + lagX + bobX + shakeX;
    float gaugeCenterY = 12.0f + (gaugeH * 0.5f) + lagY + bobY + shakeY;

    // 2. Compact 2x Smaller ADOFAI Rhythm Parry Bar (80px x 7px) centered below crosshair
    boolean showParryBar = DifficultyClientState.isHoldingParry
    || DifficultyClientState.isInParryFlow()
    || (DifficultyClientState.resultDisplayUntilMs > now);

    float barW = 80.0f;
    float barH = 7.0f;
    float parryCenterX = screenW * 0.5f;
    float parryCenterY = (screenH * 0.5f) + 20.0f;

    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();

    RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
      gui.pose(), bufferSource, font,
      gaugeCenterX, gaugeCenterY, 0.0f,
      gaugeW, gaugeH, 2.0f, 2.0f,
      gStyle.backgroundColor(), gStyle.borderColor(), List.of(), false
    );

    if (showParryBar) {
      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        gui.pose(), bufferSource, font,
        parryCenterX, parryCenterY, 0.0f,
        barW + 4.0f, barH + 4.0f, 1.5f, 1.5f,
        0xDD0D0B14, 0x00000000, List.of(), false
      );
    }

    bufferSource.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    bufferSource.endBatch();
    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();

    // Render Quantized Gradient Riposte Gauge Fill (using same shader math as BossHealthBarRenderer)
    float pad = 2.5f;
    float innerLeft = gaugeCenterX - (gaugeW * 0.5f) + pad;
    float innerTop = gaugeCenterY - (gaugeH * 0.5f) + pad;
    float innerW = gaugeW - (pad * 2.0f);
    float innerH = gaugeH - (pad * 2.0f);
    int fillW = Math.round(innerW * fillRatio);

    if (fillW > 0) {
      float cellSize = Mth.lerp(gStyle.pixelDensity(), 6.0f, 1.0f);
      int cols = Math.max(1, Math.round(innerW / cellSize));
      int rows = Math.max(1, Math.round(innerH / cellSize));

      double rad = Math.toRadians(gStyle.colorAngle());
      double dirX = Math.cos(rad);
      double dirY = Math.sin(rad);
      double maxProj = Math.max(0.0001D, Math.abs(dirX) + Math.abs(dirY));
      int clipRight = Math.round(innerLeft) + fillW;

      for (int c = 0; cols > c; c++) {
        int ix0 = Math.round(innerLeft + (c * (innerW / cols)));
        int ix1 = Math.min(clipRight, Math.round(innerLeft + ((c + 1) * (innerW / cols))));
        if (ix0 >= ix1) break;
        float u = (c + 0.5f) / (float) cols;

        for (int r = 0; rows > r; r++) {
          int iy0 = Math.round(innerTop + (r * (innerH / rows)));
          int iy1 = Math.round(innerTop + ((r + 1) * (innerH / rows)));
          if (iy0 >= iy1) continue;
          float v = (r + 0.5f) / (float) rows;
          double proj = ((u - 0.5D) * dirX + (v - 0.5D) * dirY) / maxProj + 0.5D;
          gui.fill(ix0, iy0, ix1, iy1, gStyle.sampleGradient((float) proj));
        }
      }
    }

    // Render Compact 2x Smaller ADOFAI Parry Bar + Cubic-Bezier Cyan Line
    if (showParryBar) {
      int bx0 = Math.round(parryCenterX - (barW * 0.5f));
      int by0 = Math.round(parryCenterY - (barH * 0.5f));
      int by1 = Math.round(parryCenterY + (barH * 0.5f));

      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.00f, 0.10f, 0xFFDC2626);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.10f, 0.30f, 0xFFF97316);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.30f, 0.45f, 0xFF22C55E);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.45f, 0.55f, 0xFFFFFFFF);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.55f, 0.70f, 0xFF22C55E);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.70f, 0.90f, 0xFFF97316);
      drawZone(gui, bx0, by0, by1, barW, 0.90f, 1.00f, 0xFFDC2626);

      float cursor = DifficultyClientState.getSweepProgress();
      if (cursor >= 0.0f) {
        int cx = bx0 + Math.round(barW * cursor);
        gui.fill(cx - 1, by0 - 2, cx + 1, by1 + 2, 0xFF00F5FF);
      }
    }
  }

  private static void drawZone(GuiGraphics gui, int bx0, int by0, int by1, float barW, float startPct, float endPct, int color) {
    int x0 = bx0 + Math.round(barW * startPct);
    int x1 = bx0 + Math.round(barW * endPct);
    if (x1 > x0) gui.fill(x0, by0, x1, by1, color);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/DifficultyKeybinds.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyMapping;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RegisterKeyMappingsEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

public final class DifficultyKeybinds {
  public static final KeyMapping PARRY_KEY = new KeyMapping(
    "key.hexacreatecore.parry", InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_R, "key.categories.hexacreatecore"
  );
  public static final KeyMapping UNLEASH_KEY = new KeyMapping(
    "key.hexacreatecore.unleash_riposte", InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_V, "key.categories.hexacreatecore"
  );
  public static final KeyMapping SELECTOR_KEY = new KeyMapping(
    "key.hexacreatecore.difficulty_menu", InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_K, "key.categories.hexacreatecore"
  );

  private static boolean wasParryDown = false;

  @EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
  public static final class ModEvents {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterKeys(RegisterKeyMappingsEvent event) {
      event.register(PARRY_KEY);
      event.register(UNLEASH_KEY);
      event.register(SELECTOR_KEY);
    }
  }

  @EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
  public static final class GameEvents {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
      Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
      if (mc.player == null || mc.screen != null) return;

      while (SELECTOR_KEY.consumeClick()) {
        DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen = !DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen;
        if (DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen) {
          DifficultyClientState.selectorBaseYaw = mc.player.getYRot();
        }
      }

      while (UNLEASH_KEY.consumeClick()) {
        PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("UNLEASH_RIPOSTE", "", 0));
      }

      boolean parryDown = PARRY_KEY.isDown();
      if (parryDown && !wasParryDown) {
        DifficultyClientState.onParryKeyPressed();
      } else if (!parryDown && wasParryDown) {
        DifficultyClientState.onParryKeyReleased();
      }
      wasParryDown = parryDown;
      DifficultyClientState.tickParryAutoFail();
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onMouseClick(InputEvent.MouseButton.Pre event) {
      if (!DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen || event.getAction() != GLFW.GLFW_PRESS) return;
      if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) {
        if (!DifficultyClientState.hoveredTierId.isEmpty()) {
          PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
            new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("SELECT_TIER", DifficultyClientState.hoveredTierId, 0)
          );
          DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen = false;
          event.setCanceled(true);
        }
      } else if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) {
        DifficultyClientState.selectorOpen = false;
        event.setCanceled(true);
      }
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/ParryFlowAfterImageRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.AbstractClientPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.LightTexture;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.player.PlayerRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.OverlayTexture;
import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

@SuppressWarnings({"rawtypes", "unchecked"})
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class ParryFlowAfterImageRenderer {
  private record PoseSnapshot(
    long timestampMs, Vec3 pos, float bodyYaw, float headYaw,
    float headPitch, float walkPos, float walkSpeed, boolean crouching
  ) {}

  private static final Deque HISTORY = new ArrayDeque();
  private static final float[] OPACITIES = { 0.80f, 0.60f, 0.40f, 0.20f };
  private static final long[] SAMPLE_AGE_MS = { 45L, 90L, 135L, 180L };
  private static long lastCaptureMs = 0L;

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderAfterImages(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_ENTITIES) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (!(mc.player instanceof AbstractClientPlayer player)) return;
    if (!DifficultyClientState.isInParryFlow()) {
      if (!HISTORY.isEmpty()) HISTORY.clear();
      return;
    }

    long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
    float pt = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(true);

    if ((now - lastCaptureMs) >= 16L) {
      lastCaptureMs = now;
      HISTORY.addFirst(new PoseSnapshot(
        now, player.getPosition(pt),
        Mth.rotLerp(pt, player.yBodyRotO, player.yBodyRot),
        Mth.rotLerp(pt, player.yHeadRotO, player.yHeadRot),
        Mth.lerp(pt, player.xRotO, player.getXRot()),
        player.walkAnimation.position(pt),
        player.walkAnimation.speed(pt),
        player.isCrouching()
      ));

      while (HISTORY.size() > 24) HISTORY.removeLast();
    }

    if (HISTORY.isEmpty()) return;
    if (!(mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().getRenderer(player) instanceof PlayerRenderer playerRenderer)) return;

    var model = playerRenderer.getModel();
    Vec3 camPos = event.getCamera().getPosition();
    PoseStack poseStack = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();

    for (int i = 0; 4 > i; i++) {

      PoseSnapshot snap = findSnapshotAtAge(now - SAMPLE_AGE_MS[i]);

      if (snap == null) continue;
      if (mc.options.getCameraType().isFirstPerson() && 0.04D > snap.pos.distanceToSqr(player.getPosition(pt))) continue;

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(snap.pos.x - camPos.x, snap.pos.y - camPos.y, snap.pos.z - camPos.z);
      poseStack.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(180.0f - snap.bodyYaw));
      poseStack.scale(-0.9375f, -0.9375f, 0.9375f);
      poseStack.translate(0.0D, -1.501D, 0.0D);

      model.crouching = snap.crouching;
      model.young = false;
      model.setupAnim(player, snap.walkPos, snap.walkSpeed, player.tickCount + pt, snap.headYaw - snap.bodyYaw, snap.headPitch);

      int alpha = Math.round(OPACITIES[i] * 255.0f);
      int argb = FastColor.ARGB32.color(alpha, 76, 232, 255);
      VertexConsumer vc = bufferSource.getBuffer(RenderType.entityTranslucent(player.getSkin().texture()));

      model.renderToBuffer(poseStack, vc, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT, OverlayTexture.NO_OVERLAY, argb);
      poseStack.popPose();
    }

    bufferSource.endBatch();
  }

  private static PoseSnapshot findSnapshotAtAge(long targetTimeMs) {
    PoseSnapshot closest = null;
    long bestDiff = Long.MAX_VALUE;
    for (Object obj : HISTORY) {
      PoseSnapshot snap = (PoseSnapshot) obj;
      long diff = Math.abs(snap.timestampMs - targetTimeMs);
      if (bestDiff > diff) {
        bestDiff = diff;
        closest = snap;
      }
    }
    return closest;
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/client/SoulRecoveryClientRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets.SoulActionPayload;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets.SoulSyncPayload;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.MovementInputUpdateEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class SoulRecoveryClientRenderer {

  public static boolean inSoulForm = false;
  public static Vec3 bodyPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  public static Vec3 launchPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  public static int remainingTicks = 0;
  public static int maxTicks = 240;
  public static int fractureStacks = 0;

  private static Vec3 soulCamPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  private static Vec3 prevSoulCamPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  private static Vec3 soulVel = Vec3.ZERO;
  private static int launchSlideTicks = 0;
  private static int wallHitCooldownTicks = 0;
  private static long wallPenaltyBannerUntilMs = 0L;
  private static boolean reclaimSent = false;

  private static float inputForward = 0.0f;
  private static float inputStrafe = 0.0f;
  private static boolean inputJump = false;
  private static boolean inputSneak = false;

  private static final TextEffectStyle SOUL_ALERT_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.glitch(0xFF21B7D1);
  private static final TextEffectStyle WALL_PENALTY_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.glitch(0xFFFF1E56);
  private static final TextEffectStyle BODY_BEACON_STYLE = TextEffectStyle.spectrum();

  private SoulRecoveryClientRenderer() {}

  public static Vec3 getLerpedSoulCamPos(float pt) {
    return new Vec3(
      Mth.lerp(pt, prevSoulCamPos.x, soulCamPos.x),
      Mth.lerp(pt, prevSoulCamPos.y, soulCamPos.y),
      Mth.lerp(pt, prevSoulCamPos.z, soulCamPos.z)
    );
  }

  public static void handleSoulSync(SoulSyncPayload payload) {
    boolean wasInSoul = inSoulForm;
    inSoulForm = payload.inSoulForm();
    bodyPos = new Vec3(payload.bodyX(), payload.bodyY(), payload.bodyZ());
    launchPos = new Vec3(payload.launchX(), payload.launchY(), payload.launchZ());
    maxTicks = Math.max(1, payload.maxTicks());
    fractureStacks = payload.fractureStacks();

    if (inSoulForm && !wasInSoul) {
      Vec3 startEye = bodyPos.add(0.0D, 1.62D, 0.0D);
      soulCamPos = startEye;
      prevSoulCamPos = startEye;
      soulVel = Vec3.ZERO;
      launchSlideTicks = 0;
      wallHitCooldownTicks = 0;
      reclaimSent = false;
      remainingTicks = payload.remainingTicks();
    } else if (inSoulForm) {
      remainingTicks = Math.min(remainingTicks, payload.remainingTicks());
    } else {
      reclaimSent = false;
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onMovementInput(MovementInputUpdateEvent event) {
    if (!inSoulForm) return;
    var input = event.getInput();
    inputForward = input.forwardImpulse;
    inputStrafe = input.leftImpulse;
    inputJump = input.jumping;
    inputSneak = input.shiftKeyDown;

    input.forwardImpulse = 0.0f;
    input.leftImpulse = 0.0f;
    input.jumping = false;
    input.shiftKeyDown = true;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
    if (!inSoulForm) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;

    prevSoulCamPos = soulCamPos;
    if (wallHitCooldownTicks > 0) wallHitCooldownTicks--;

    if (7 > launchSlideTicks) {
      launchSlideTicks++;
      float t = launchSlideTicks / 7.0f;
      double sineEase = Math.sin(t * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
      Vec3 startEye = bodyPos.add(0.0D, 1.62D, 0.0D);
      soulCamPos = startEye.lerp(launchPos, sineEase);
    } else {
      float yawRad = (float) Math.toRadians(mc.player.getYRot());
      float pitchRad = (float) Math.toRadians(mc.player.getXRot());
      double cosP = Math.cos(pitchRad);
      Vec3 forward = new Vec3(-Math.sin(yawRad) * cosP, -Math.sin(pitchRad), Math.cos(yawRad) * cosP);
      Vec3 right = new Vec3(Math.cos(yawRad), 0.0D, Math.sin(yawRad));

      double vert = (inputJump ? 1.0D : 0.0D) - (inputSneak ? 1.0D : 0.0D);
      Vec3 wishDir = forward.scale(inputForward).add(right.scale(inputStrafe)).add(0.0D, vert, 0.0D);
      if (wishDir.lengthSqr() > 1.0D) wishDir = wishDir.normalize();

      double speed = mc.options.keySprint.isDown() ? 0.95D : 0.65D;
      soulVel = soulVel.scale(0.68D).add(wishDir.scale(speed * 0.35D));

      boolean hitWall = false;
      Vec3 nextPos = soulCamPos;

      Vec3 tryX = new Vec3(nextPos.x + soulVel.x, nextPos.y, nextPos.z);
      if (isSolidBlockAt(mc, tryX)) {
        hitWall = true;
        soulVel = new Vec3(-soulVel.x * 0.4D, soulVel.y, soulVel.z);
      } else {
        nextPos = tryX;
      }

      Vec3 tryY = new Vec3(nextPos.x, nextPos.y + soulVel.y, nextPos.z);
      if (isSolidBlockAt(mc, tryY)) {
        hitWall = true;
        soulVel = new Vec3(soulVel.x, -soulVel.y * 0.4D, soulVel.z);
      } else {
        nextPos = tryY;
      }

      Vec3 tryZ = new Vec3(nextPos.x, nextPos.y, nextPos.z + soulVel.z);
      if (isSolidBlockAt(mc, tryZ)) {
        hitWall = true;
        soulVel = new Vec3(soulVel.x, soulVel.y, -soulVel.z * 0.4D);
      } else {
        nextPos = tryZ;
      }

      soulCamPos = nextPos;

      if (hitWall && wallHitCooldownTicks <= 0) {
        wallHitCooldownTicks = 12;
        remainingTicks = Math.max(0, remainingTicks - 40);
        wallPenaltyBannerUntilMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 900L;
        PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new SoulActionPayload("WALL_HIT"));
      }
    }

    Vec3 bodyCenter = bodyPos.add(0.0D, 1.0D, 0.0D);
    if (!reclaimSent && launchSlideTicks >= 7 && soulCamPos.distanceToSqr(bodyCenter) <= 2.72D) {
      reclaimSent = true;
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new SoulActionPayload("RECLAIM_BODY"));
      return;
    }

    remainingTicks--;
    if (!reclaimSent && remainingTicks <= 0) {
      reclaimSent = true;
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new SoulActionPayload("EXPIRED"));
    }
  }

  private static boolean isSolidBlockAt(Minecraft mc, Vec3 pos) {
    BlockPos bp = BlockPos.containing(pos);
    return mc.level.getBlockState(bp).isSuffocating(mc.level, bp);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderWorld(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (!inSoulForm) return;
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null) return;

    Vec3 camPos = event.getCamera().getPosition();
    PoseStack poseStack = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    int accent = DifficultyClientState.activeTier.accentColor;

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(bodyPos.x - camPos.x, bodyPos.y - camPos.y + 1.95D, bodyPos.z - camPos.z);

    float faceYaw = (float) Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(camPos.x - bodyPos.x, camPos.z - bodyPos.z)) + 180.0f;
    poseStack.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(faceYaw));
    poseStack.scale(-0.015f, -0.015f, -0.015f);

    String marker = "✦ RECLAIM YOUR BODY ✦";
    var boldMarker = Component.literal(marker).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));
    float mw = mc.font.width(boldMarker) + 24.0f;
    float mh = mc.font.lineHeight + 10.0f;
    RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, bufferSource, mc.font,
      0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, mw, mh, 3.0f, 3.0f,
      0xDD110E1A, accent, List.of(), false
    );
    poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 3.5f);
    ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3DBold(
      poseStack, bufferSource, mc.font, marker, -mc.font.width(boldMarker) * 0.5f, -mc.font.lineHeight * 0.5f, BODY_BEACON_STYLE
    );
    poseStack.popPose();
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
    if (!inSoulForm) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    Font font = mc.font;
    int screenW = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
    float centerX = screenW * 0.5f;

    boolean showWallPenalty = System.currentTimeMillis() < wallPenaltyBannerUntilMs;
    float secLeft = Math.max(0.0f, remainingTicks / 20.0f);
    String alert = showWallPenalty
    ? String.format("WALL IMPACT (-2.0s) — RETURN TO BODY (%.1fs)", secLeft)
    : String.format("SOUL SEVERED — FLY TO YOUR BODY (%.1fs) | WALLS COST -2.0s", secLeft);
    var boldAlert = Component.literal(alert).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true));

    float boxW = font.width(boldAlert) + 28.0f;
    float boxH = font.lineHeight + 16.0f;
    float centerY = 38.0f;

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getGuiGraphics().pose();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    int accent = showWallPenalty ? 0xFFFF1E56 : DifficultyClientState.activeTier.accentColor;

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();

    RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, bufferSource, font,
      centerX, centerY, 0.0f, boxW, boxH, 3.0f, 3.0f,
      0xDD110E1A, accent, List.of(), true
    );

    bufferSource.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    bufferSource.endBatch();
    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();

    ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw2DBold(
      event.getGuiGraphics(), font, alert, centerX - (font.width(boldAlert) * 0.5f), centerY - (font.lineHeight * 0.5f) - 2.0f,
      showWallPenalty ? WALL_PENALTY_STYLE : SOUL_ALERT_STYLE
    );

    float pct = Mth.clamp((float) remainingTicks / (float) maxTicks, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    int barX0 = Math.round(centerX - (boxW * 0.5f) + 6.0f);
    int barX1 = Math.round(centerX + (boxW * 0.5f) - 6.0f);
    int barY = Math.round(centerY + (boxH * 0.5f) - 5.0f);
    int fillX = barX0 + Math.round((barX1 - barX0) * pct);
    event.getGuiGraphics().fill(barX0, barY, barX1, barY + 2, 0xFF1A1429);
    event.getGuiGraphics().fill(barX0, barY, fillX, barY + 2, accent);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/data/DifficultyData.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle;

public final class DifficultyData {

  public enum DifficultyTier {
    NORMAL("normal", "Normal", 0, 0xFFAAAAAA),
    ETHEREAL("ethereal", "Ethereal", 10, 0xFF21B7D1),
    ABSOLUTION("absolution", "Absolution", 15, 0xFFB233FF),
    GENESIS("genesis", "Genesis", 20, 0xFFFF1E56);

    public final String id;
    public final String displayName;
    public final int riposteMaxCharges;
    public final int accentColor;

    DifficultyTier(String id, String displayName, int riposteMaxCharges, int accentColor) {
      this.id = id;
      this.displayName = displayName;
      this.riposteMaxCharges = riposteMaxCharges;
      this.accentColor = accentColor;
    }

    public static DifficultyTier fromId(String id) {
      for (DifficultyTier t : values()) {
        if (t.id.equalsIgnoreCase(id)) return t;
      }
      return NORMAL;
    }
  }

  public record MobEffectEntry(
    String effectId,
    int durationTicks,
    int amplifier,
    float chance
  ) {}

  public record EntityOverride(
    double hpMultiplier,
    double damageMultiplier,
    List<MobEffectEntry> spawnBuffs,
    List<MobEffectEntry> onHitDebuffs,
    List<String> attackCallbacks
  ) {}

  public record DifficultyProfile(
    String id,
    List<String> description,
    double globalHpMultiplier,
    double globalDamageMultiplier,
    HealthBarStyle gaugeStyle,
    List<MobEffectEntry> globalSpawnBuffs,
    List<MobEffectEntry> globalOnHitDebuffs,
    List<String> globalAttackCallbacks,
    Map<String, EntityOverride> entityOverrides
  ) {
    public static HealthBarStyle defaultGauge(int accent) {
      return new HealthBarStyle(
        "", accent, 0xCC110E1A, List.of(0xFF1A1626, accent, 0xFFFFFFFF),
        List.of(0.15f, 0.65f, 0.95f), 0.5f, -15.0f, "right", 1.5f, false, List.of()
      );
    }

    public static DifficultyProfile empty(String id) {
      DifficultyTier tier = DifficultyTier.fromId(id);
      return new DifficultyProfile(id, List.of(), 1.0D, 1.0D, defaultGauge(tier.accentColor), List.of(), List.of(), List.of(), Map.of());
    }
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/data/DifficultyReloadListener.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyProfile;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.EntityOverride;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.MobEffectEntry;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.ResourceManager;
import net.minecraft.server.packs.resources.SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener;
import net.minecraft.util.profiling.ProfilerFiller;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.AddReloadListenerEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class DifficultyReloadListener extends SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener {
  private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().create();
  private static final Map<String, DifficultyProfile> PROFILES = new HashMap<>();
  private static String rawGaugeBarsJson = "{}";
  private static String rawParrySoundsJson = "{}";

  public DifficultyReloadListener() {
    super(GSON, "difficulty");
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onAddReloadListeners(AddReloadListenerEvent event) {
    event.addListener(new DifficultyReloadListener());
  }

  public static DifficultyProfile getProfile(String tierId) {
    return PROFILES.getOrDefault(tierId.toLowerCase(), DifficultyProfile.empty(tierId));
  }

  @Override
  protected void apply(Map<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> objects, ResourceManager manager, ProfilerFiller profiler) {
    PROFILES.clear();
    JsonObject combinedGauges = new JsonObject();

    for (Map.Entry<ResourceLocation, JsonElement> entry : objects.entrySet()) {
      ResourceLocation loc = entry.getKey();
      if (!HexaCreateCore.MODID.equals(loc.getNamespace()) || !entry.getValue().isJsonObject()) continue;

      String tierId = loc.getPath().toLowerCase();
      JsonObject root = entry.getValue().getAsJsonObject();

      if ("parry_sounds".equals(tierId)) {
        rawParrySoundsJson = root.toString();
        HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("Loaded custom parry_sounds.json configuration.");
        continue;
      }

      List<String> desc = parseStringList(root, "description");
      double globalHp = root.has("hp_multiplier") ? root.get("hp_multiplier").getAsDouble() : 1.0D;
      double globalDmg = root.has("damage_multiplier") ? root.get("damage_multiplier").getAsDouble() : 1.0D;

      DifficultyData.DifficultyTier tierEnum = DifficultyData.DifficultyTier.fromId(tierId);
      com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossHealthBarData.HealthBarStyle gaugeStyle = DifficultyProfile.defaultGauge(tierEnum.accentColor);
      if (root.has("gauge_bar") && root.get("gauge_bar").isJsonObject()) {
        JsonObject wrapper = new JsonObject();
        wrapper.add(tierId, root.getAsJsonObject("gauge_bar"));
        combinedGauges.add(tierId, root.getAsJsonObject("gauge_bar"));
        var parsedMap = com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.data.BossRushReloadListener.parseHealthBarsMap(wrapper);
        if (parsedMap.containsKey(tierId)) {
          gaugeStyle = parsedMap.get(tierId);
        }
      }

      List<MobEffectEntry> spawnBuffs = parseEffects(root, "spawn_buffs");
      List<MobEffectEntry> hitDebuffs = parseEffects(root, "on_hit_debuffs");
      List<String> callbacks = parseStringList(root, "attack_callbacks");

      Map<String, EntityOverride> overrides = new HashMap<>();
      if (root.has("entities") && root.get("entities").isJsonObject()) {
        JsonObject ents = root.getAsJsonObject("entities");
        for (Map.Entry<String, JsonElement> e : ents.entrySet()) {
          if (!e.getValue().isJsonObject()) continue;
          JsonObject eObj = e.getValue().getAsJsonObject();
          double eHp = eObj.has("hp_multiplier") ? eObj.get("hp_multiplier").getAsDouble() : globalHp;
          double eDmg = eObj.has("damage_multiplier") ? eObj.get("damage_multiplier").getAsDouble() : globalDmg;
          overrides.put(e.getKey(), new EntityOverride(
            eHp, eDmg,
            parseEffects(eObj, "spawn_buffs"),
            parseEffects(eObj, "on_hit_debuffs"),
            parseStringList(eObj, "attack_callbacks")
          ));
        }
      }

      PROFILES.put(tierId, new DifficultyProfile(tierId, desc, globalHp, globalDmg, gaugeStyle, spawnBuffs, hitDebuffs, callbacks, overrides));
      HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("Loaded difficulty profile '{}' ({} entity overrides).", tierId, overrides.size());
    }

    rawGaugeBarsJson = combinedGauges.toString();
  }

  private static List<MobEffectEntry> parseEffects(JsonObject obj, String key) {
    List<MobEffectEntry> list = new ArrayList<>();
    if (!obj.has(key) || !obj.get(key).isJsonArray()) return list;
    for (JsonElement el : obj.getAsJsonArray(key)) {
      if (!el.isJsonObject()) continue;
      JsonObject eo = el.getAsJsonObject();
      String id = eo.has("effect") ? eo.get("effect").getAsString() : "";
      int dur = eo.has("duration") ? eo.get("duration").getAsInt() : 100;
      int amp = eo.has("amplifier") ? eo.get("amplifier").getAsInt() : 0;
      float chance = eo.has("chance") ? eo.get("chance").getAsFloat() : 1.0f;
      if (!id.isEmpty()) list.add(new MobEffectEntry(id, dur, amp, chance));
    }
    return list;
  }

  private static List<String> parseStringList(JsonObject obj, String key) {
    List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
    if (!obj.has(key) || !obj.get(key).isJsonArray()) return list;
    for (JsonElement el : obj.getAsJsonArray(key)) {
      list.add(el.getAsString());
    }
    return list;
  }

  public static String getRawGaugeBarsJson() { return rawGaugeBarsJson;}
  public static String getRawParrySoundsJson() { return rawParrySoundsJson; }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/DifficultyManager.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.DifficultyAttackCallback;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.*;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.StringArgumentType;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.EntityJoinLevelEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingIncomingDamageEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.neoforged.neoforgespi.language.ModFileScanData;
import org.objectweb.asm.Type;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class DifficultyManager {
  private static final ResourceLocation HP_MOD_ID = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "difficulty_hp_scale");
  private static final Map<String, AttackCallbackFn> CALLBACKS = new HashMap<>();
  private static final Map<UUID, PlayerCombatState> PLAYER_STATES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  private static boolean callbacksScanned = false;
  private static int genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining = 0;

  @FunctionalInterface
  public interface AttackCallbackFn {
    void invoke(ServerLevel level, LivingEntity attacker, LivingEntity target, float damage);
  }

  public static final class PlayerCombatState {
    public String activeParryZone = "NONE";
    public long parryExpiresAtMs = 0L;
    public long parryFlowExpiresAtMs = 0L;
    public int riposteCharges = 0;
    public boolean ripostePrimed = false;
  }

  public static PlayerCombatState getState(UUID uuid) {
    return PLAYER_STATES.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new PlayerCombatState());
  }

  public static void scanCallbacksIfNeeded() {
    if (callbacksScanned) return;
    callbacksScanned = true;
    Type targetType = Type.getType(DifficultyAttackCallback.class);
    for (ModFileScanData scanData : ModList.get().getAllScanData()) {
      for (ModFileScanData.AnnotationData ad : scanData.getAnnotations()) {
        if (!targetType.equals(ad.annotationType())) continue;
        try {
          Class<?> owner = Class.forName(ad.clazz().getClassName());
          String methodName = ad.memberName().substring(0, ad.memberName().indexOf('('));
          Method m = owner.getDeclaredMethod(methodName, ServerLevel.class, LivingEntity.class, LivingEntity.class, float.class);
          m.setAccessible(true);
          String id = (String) ad.annotationData().get("value");
          CALLBACKS.put(id, (lvl, atk, tgt, dmg) -> {
            try { m.invoke(null, lvl, atk, tgt, dmg); } catch (Exception e) { HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("Callback error {}", id, e); }
          });
        } catch (Exception e) {
          HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("Failed registering @DifficultyAttackCallback", e);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  public static void handleClientAction(ServerPlayer player, DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload payload) {
    DifficultySavedData data = DifficultySavedData.get(player.serverLevel());
    PlayerCombatState state = getState(player.getUUID());
    long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

    switch (payload.actionType()) {
      case "SELECT_TIER" -> {
        DifficultyTier chosen = DifficultyTier.fromId(payload.stringParam());
        if (chosen == data.getActiveTier()) {
          return;
        }
        if (chosen == DifficultyTier.GENESIS && !data.isGenesisUnlocked()) {
          return;
        }
        ServerLevel sLevel = player.serverLevel();
        data.setActiveTier(chosen);
        data.setDirty();
        for (ServerPlayer p : sLevel.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
          syncToPlayer(p);
        }

        if (chosen == DifficultyTier.ETHEREAL) {
          genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining = 0;
          sLevel.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.ALLAY_AMBIENT_WITHOUT_ITEM, SoundSource.MASTER, 1.0f, 0.5f);
        } else if (chosen == DifficultyTier.ABSOLUTION) {
          genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining = 0;
          sLevel.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.WITHER_SPAWN, SoundSource.MASTER, 1.0f, 0.7f);
        } else if (chosen == DifficultyTier.GENESIS) {
          sLevel.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.ENDER_DRAGON_DEATH, SoundSource.MASTER, 0.8f, 0.5f);
          genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining = 500; // 25 seconds at 20 TPS
        } else {
          genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining = 0;
        }
      }

      case "PARRY_WINDOW" -> {
        if (data.getActiveTier() == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) return;
        state.activeParryZone = payload.stringParam();
        state.parryExpiresAtMs = now + Math.max(80L, payload.intParam());
      }
      case "UNLEASH_RIPOSTE" -> {
        DifficultyTier tier = data.getActiveTier();
        if (tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL || tier.riposteMaxCharges == 0) return;
        if (state.riposteCharges >= tier.riposteMaxCharges && !state.ripostePrimed) {
          state.ripostePrimed = true;
          player.serverLevel().playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.TRIDENT_THUNDER.value(), SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 1.6f);
          syncToPlayer(player);
        }
      }
      case "BREAK_FLOW" -> {
        state.parryFlowExpiresAtMs = 0L;
        state.parryExpiresAtMs = 0L;
      }
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onEntityJoin(EntityJoinLevelEvent event) {
    if (event.getLevel().isClientSide() || !(event.getEntity() instanceof Mob mob)) return;
    if (mob.getPersistentData().getBoolean("hexacreate_scaled")) return;

    DifficultyTier tier = DifficultySavedData.get((ServerLevel) event.getLevel()).getActiveTier();
    if (tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) return;

    DifficultyProfile profile = DifficultyReloadListener.getProfile(tier.id);
    String typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(mob.getType()).toString();
    EntityOverride ov = profile.entityOverrides().get(typeId);
    if (ov == null && !(mob instanceof Enemy)) return;

    double hpMult = (ov != null) ? ov.hpMultiplier() : profile.globalHpMultiplier();
    if (hpMult != 1.0D && mob.getAttribute(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH) != null) {
      var attr = mob.getAttribute(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH);
      attr.removeModifier(HP_MOD_ID);
      attr.addPermanentModifier(new AttributeModifier(HP_MOD_ID, hpMult - 1.0D, AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL));
      mob.setHealth(mob.getMaxHealth());
    }

    applyEffectList(mob, profile.globalSpawnBuffs());
    if (ov != null) applyEffectList(mob, ov.spawnBuffs());
    mob.getPersistentData().putBoolean("hexacreate_scaled", true);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onIncomingDamage(LivingIncomingDamageEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity().level().isClientSide()) return;
    scanCallbacksIfNeeded();
    ServerLevel level = (ServerLevel) event.getEntity().level();
    DifficultyTier tier = DifficultySavedData.get(level).getActiveTier();
    if (tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) return;

    // 1. Player attacking an entity with Primed Riposte (flat 5% Max HP true damage)
    if (event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer attacker && event.getEntity() != attacker) {
      PlayerCombatState atkState = getState(attacker.getUUID());
      if (atkState.ripostePrimed) {
        atkState.ripostePrimed = false;
        atkState.riposteCharges = 0;
        LivingEntity target = event.getEntity();
        float trueDamage = Math.max(1.0f, target.getMaxHealth() * 0.05f);
        float newHp = Math.max(0.0f, target.getHealth() - trueDamage);
        target.setHealth(newHp);
        if (0.0f >= newHp) {
          target.die(attacker.damageSources().playerAttack(attacker));
        }
        level.playSound(null, target.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.ANVIL_LAND, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 1.5f);
        syncToPlayer(attacker);
      }
    }

    // 2. Player receiving ANY damage (melee, projectiles, explosions, fall, void, or even /kill)
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
      PlayerCombatState state = getState(player.getUUID());
      long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

      if (event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity mobAttacker) {
        DifficultyProfile profile = DifficultyReloadListener.getProfile(tier.id);
        String typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(mobAttacker.getType()).toString();
        EntityOverride ov = profile.entityOverrides().get(typeId);
        double dmgMult = (ov != null) ? ov.damageMultiplier() : profile.globalDamageMultiplier();
        event.setAmount((float) (event.getAmount() * dmgMult));

        for (String cb : profile.globalAttackCallbacks()) {
          if (CALLBACKS.containsKey(cb)) CALLBACKS.get(cb).invoke(level, mobAttacker, player, event.getAmount());
        }
        if (ov != null) {
          for (String cb : ov.attackCallbacks()) {
            if (CALLBACKS.containsKey(cb)) CALLBACKS.get(cb).invoke(level, mobAttacker, player, event.getAmount());
          }
        }
      }

      if (state.parryExpiresAtMs >= now && !"FAIL".equals(state.activeParryZone) && !"NONE".equals(state.activeParryZone)) {
        String zone = state.activeParryZone;
        state.parryExpiresAtMs = 0L;
        if ("PURE".equals(zone)) {
          event.setCanceled(true);
          if (event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity atk) {
            atk.hurt(player.damageSources().thorns(player), event.getAmount());
          }
          rewardParrySuccess(player, state, tier, 1.8f);
          return;
        } else if ("PARRY".equals(zone) || "FLOW".equals(zone)) {
          event.setCanceled(true);
          player.invulnerableTime = 2;
          rewardParrySuccess(player, state, tier, 1.4f);
          return;
        } else if ("SHALLOW".equals(zone)) {
          event.setAmount(event.getAmount() * 0.25f);
          level.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.SHIELD_BLOCK, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 0.9f);
          return;
        }
      }

      // Unparried hit: lose 1/5 of max gauge and break Parry Flow
      if (!isPassiveDoTDamage(event.getSource())) {
        int penalty = Math.max(1, tier.riposteMaxCharges / 5);
        state.riposteCharges = Math.max(0, state.riposteCharges - penalty);
        state.parryFlowExpiresAtMs = 0L;
        syncToPlayer(player);
      }

      if (event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity mobAttacker) {
        DifficultyProfile profile = DifficultyReloadListener.getProfile(tier.id);
        String typeId = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(mobAttacker.getType()).toString();
        EntityOverride ov = profile.entityOverrides().get(typeId);
        applyEffectList(player, profile.globalOnHitDebuffs());
        if (ov != null) applyEffectList(player, ov.onHitDebuffs());
      }
    }
  }

  private static void rewardParrySuccess(ServerPlayer player, PlayerCombatState state, DifficultyTier tier, float pitch) {
    state.riposteCharges = Math.min(tier.riposteMaxCharges, state.riposteCharges + 1);
    state.parryFlowExpiresAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 3000L;
    syncToPlayer(player);
  }

  private static void applyEffectList(LivingEntity target, List<MobEffectEntry> effects) {
    for (MobEffectEntry entry : effects) {
      if (target.getRandom().nextFloat() > entry.chance()) continue;
      ResourceLocation loc = ResourceLocation.tryParse(entry.effectId());
      if (loc != null && BuiltInRegistries.MOB_EFFECT.containsKey(loc)) {
        var holder = BuiltInRegistries.MOB_EFFECT.getHolder(loc);
        holder.ifPresent(h -> target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(h, entry.durationTicks(), entry.amplifier())));
      }
    }
  }

  public static void unlockGenesisForServer(ServerLevel level) {
    DifficultySavedData data = DifficultySavedData.get(level);
    if (!data.isGenesisUnlocked()) {
      data.unlockGenesis();
      for (ServerPlayer sp : level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
        syncToPlayer(sp);
        sp.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("§4§l[SECRET UNLOCKED] §cGenesis Difficulty is now available!"));
      }
    }
  }

  public static void syncToPlayer(ServerPlayer player) {
    DifficultySavedData data = DifficultySavedData.get(player.serverLevel());
    PlayerCombatState state = getState(player.getUUID());
    long flowLeft = Math.max(0L, state.parryFlowExpiresAtMs - System.currentTimeMillis());

    Map<String, List<String>> descMap = new HashMap<>();
    for (DifficultyTier t : DifficultyTier.values()) {
      if (t == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) continue;
      descMap.put(t.id, DifficultyReloadListener.getProfile(t.id).description());
    }

    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new DifficultyPackets.SyncPayload(
      data.getActiveTier().id, data.isGenesisUnlocked(), state.riposteCharges,
      state.ripostePrimed, flowLeft, new Gson().toJson(descMap),
      DifficultyReloadListener.getRawGaugeBarsJson(), DifficultyReloadListener.getRawParrySoundsJson()
    ));
  }

  private static boolean isPassiveDoTDamage(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource source) {
    if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_FIRE)) return true;
    if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_FREEZING)) return true;
    if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_DROWNING)) return true;

    // Any status-effect tick (vanilla Poison/Wither or ANY modded DoT effect) has no attacker and no projectile
    if (source.getEntity() == null && source.getDirectEntity() == null) {
      if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_EXPLOSION)) return false;
      if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_LIGHTNING)) return false;
      if (source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.IS_FALL)) return false;
      return true;
    }

    // Some mods attribute DoTs to the caster with no direct projectile while a harmful effect is active
    if (source.getDirectEntity() == null && source.is(net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags.WITCH_RESISTANT_TO)) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onServerTick(net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
    if (genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining > 0) {
      int elapsed = 500 - genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining;
      int beatOffset = elapsed % 20;
      if (beatOffset == 0 || beatOffset == 4) {
        for (ServerPlayer p : event.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
          p.serverLevel().playSound(null, p.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.WARDEN_HEARTBEAT, SoundSource.MASTER, 2.0f, 0.8f);
        }
      }
      genesisHeartbeatTicksRemaining--;
    }
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerLogin(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedInEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) syncToPlayer(sp);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
    event.getDispatcher().register(Commands.literal("hexadifficulty")
      .then(Commands.argument("tier", StringArgumentType.word())
        .executes(ctx -> {
          ServerPlayer sp = ctx.getSource().getPlayerOrException();
          String arg = StringArgumentType.getString(ctx, "tier");
          handleClientAction(sp, new DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload("SELECT_TIER", arg, 0));
          return 1;
        })
      )
    );
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/DifficultySavedData.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.DifficultyTier;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.SavedData;

public final class DifficultySavedData extends SavedData {
  private static final String DATA_NAME = "hexacreatecore_difficulty";

  private DifficultyTier activeTier = DifficultyTier.NORMAL;
  private boolean genesisUnlocked = false;

  public DifficultyTier getActiveTier() {
    return activeTier;
  }

  public void setActiveTier(DifficultyTier tier) {
    if (tier == DifficultyTier.GENESIS && !genesisUnlocked) return;
    this.activeTier = tier;
    setDirty();
  }

  public boolean isGenesisUnlocked() {
    return genesisUnlocked;
  }

  public void unlockGenesis() {
    if (!this.genesisUnlocked) {
      this.genesisUnlocked = true;
      setDirty();
    }
  }

  @Override
  public CompoundTag save(CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.Provider registries) {
    tag.putString("active_tier", activeTier.id);
    tag.putBoolean("genesis_unlocked", genesisUnlocked);
    return tag;
  }

  public static DifficultySavedData load(CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.Provider registries) {
    DifficultySavedData data = new DifficultySavedData();
    data.genesisUnlocked = tag.getBoolean("genesis_unlocked");
    DifficultyTier loaded = DifficultyTier.fromId(tag.getString("active_tier"));
    data.activeTier = (loaded == DifficultyTier.GENESIS && !data.genesisUnlocked) ? DifficultyTier.ABSOLUTION : loaded;
    return data;
  }

  public static DifficultySavedData get(ServerLevel level) {
    ServerLevel overworld = level.getServer().overworld();
    return overworld.getDataStorage().computeIfAbsent(
      new SavedData.Factory<>(DifficultySavedData::new, DifficultySavedData::load),
      DATA_NAME
    );
  }
}
