package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.effect;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectCategory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.EffectCure;

import java.util.Set;

public class SoulFractureEffect extends MobEffect {

  public SoulFractureEffect() {
    super(MobEffectCategory.HARMFUL, 0xFF8B0000);
    // Amplifier 0 -> -33%, Amplifier 1 -> -66%, Amplifier 2 -> -99% Max HP
    this.addAttributeModifier(
      Attributes.MAX_HEALTH,
      ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "soul_fracture_hp_penalty"),
      -0.33D,
      AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL
    );
  }

  @Override
  public void fillEffectCures(Set<EffectCure> cures, net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance effectInstance) {
    // Empty set = completely uncurable by Milk Buckets, Honey, or standard cures
    cures.clear();
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/network/DifficultyNetworking.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.DifficultyManager;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client.DifficultyClientState;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.event.RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.registration.PayloadRegistrar;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class DifficultyNetworking {

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRegisterPayloads(RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent event) {
    PayloadRegistrar registrar = event.registrar("1");

    registrar.playToClient(
      DifficultyPackets.SyncPayload.TYPE,
      DifficultyPackets.SyncPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
    (payload, ctx) -> ctx.enqueueWork(() -> DifficultyClientState.handleSync(payload))
    );

    registrar.playToClient(
      DifficultyPackets.SoulSyncPayload.TYPE,
      DifficultyPackets.SoulSyncPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
    (payload, context) -> context.enqueueWork(() ->
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.client.SoulRecoveryClientRenderer.handleSoulSync(payload)
      )
    );

    registrar.playToServer(
      DifficultyPackets.SoulActionPayload.TYPE,
      DifficultyPackets.SoulActionPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
    (payload, context) -> context.enqueueWork(() -> {
        if (context.player() instanceof net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer sp) {
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.SoulRecoveryManager.handleSoulAction(sp, payload.action());
        }
      })
    );

    registrar.playToServer(
      DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload.TYPE,
      DifficultyPackets.ActionPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
    (payload, ctx) -> ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
        if (ctx.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) {
          DifficultyManager.handleClientAction(sp, payload);
        }
      })
    );
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/network/DifficultyPackets.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;

public final class DifficultyPackets {

  public record SyncPayload(
    String activeTierId,
    boolean genesisUnlocked,
    int riposteCharges,
    boolean ripostePrimed,
    long parryFlowRemainingMs,
    String descriptionsJson,
    String gaugeBarsJson,
    String parrySoundsJson
  ) implements CustomPacketPayload {
    public static final Type<SyncPayload> TYPE =
      new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "difficulty_sync"));

    public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, SyncPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
      (buf, p) -> {
        buf.writeUtf(p.activeTierId(), 64);
        buf.writeBoolean(p.genesisUnlocked());
        buf.writeInt(p.riposteCharges());
        buf.writeBoolean(p.ripostePrimed());
        buf.writeLong(p.parryFlowRemainingMs());
        buf.writeUtf(p.descriptionsJson(), 65536);
        buf.writeUtf(p.gaugeBarsJson(), 65536);
        buf.writeUtf(p.parrySoundsJson(), 16384);
      },
      buf -> new SyncPayload(
        buf.readUtf(64), buf.readBoolean(), buf.readInt(),
        buf.readBoolean(), buf.readLong(), buf.readUtf(65536), buf.readUtf(65536), buf.readUtf(16384)
      )

    );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() { return TYPE; }
  }


  public record ActionPayload(
    String actionType,
    String stringParam,
    int intParam
  ) implements CustomPacketPayload {
    public static final Type<ActionPayload> TYPE =
    new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "difficulty_action"));

    public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, ActionPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
    (buf, p) -> {
        buf.writeUtf(p.actionType(), 64);
        buf.writeUtf(p.stringParam(), 128);
        buf.writeInt(p.intParam());
      },
      buf -> new ActionPayload(buf.readUtf(64), buf.readUtf(128), buf.readInt())
    );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() { return TYPE; }
  }

  public record SoulSyncPayload(
    boolean inSoulForm,
    double bodyX,
    double bodyY,
    double bodyZ,
    double launchX,
    double launchY,
    double launchZ,
    int remainingTicks,
    int maxTicks,
    int fractureStacks
  ) implements CustomPacketPayload {
    public static final Type<SoulSyncPayload> TYPE =
      new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "soul_sync"));

    public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, SoulSyncPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
      (buf, p) -> {
        buf.writeBoolean(p.inSoulForm);
        buf.writeDouble(p.bodyX);
        buf.writeDouble(p.bodyY);
        buf.writeDouble(p.bodyZ);
        buf.writeDouble(p.launchX);
        buf.writeDouble(p.launchY);
        buf.writeDouble(p.launchZ);
        buf.writeInt(p.remainingTicks);
        buf.writeInt(p.maxTicks);
        buf.writeInt(p.fractureStacks);
      },
      buf -> new SoulSyncPayload(
        buf.readBoolean(),
        buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble(),
        buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble(),
        buf.readInt(), buf.readInt(), buf.readInt()
      )
    );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
      return TYPE;
    }
  }

  public record SoulActionPayload(String action) implements CustomPacketPayload {
    public static final Type<SoulActionPayload> TYPE =
    new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "soul_action"));

    public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, SoulActionPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
    (buf, p) -> buf.writeUtf(p.action),
      buf -> new SoulActionPayload(buf.readUtf())
    );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
      return TYPE;
    }
  }


}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/difficulty/SoulRecoveryManager.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.data.DifficultyData.*;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.difficulty.network.DifficultyPackets.SoulSyncPayload;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.registry.ModEffects;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Pose;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.EventPriority;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingDeathEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.LivingIncomingDamageEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.living.MobEffectEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class SoulRecoveryManager {

  private static final int SOUL_DURATION_TICKS = 240; // 12.0s total timer (never resets)
  private static final int WALL_PENALTY_TICKS = 40;   // -2.0s penalty per wall impact
  private static final Map<UUID, ActiveSoul> ACTIVE_SOULS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  private static final java.util.Set<UUID> TRUE_DEATH_PENDING = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();


  private static final class ActiveSoul {
    final Vec3 bodyPos;
    final Vec3 launchPos;
    final float bodyYaw;
    final float bodyPitch;
    final int nextStacks;
    int ticksRemaining;

    ActiveSoul(Vec3 bodyPos, Vec3 launchPos, float bodyYaw, float bodyPitch, int nextStacks, int ticksRemaining) {
      this.bodyPos = bodyPos;
      this.launchPos = launchPos;
      this.bodyYaw = bodyYaw;
      this.bodyPitch = bodyPitch;
      this.nextStacks = nextStacks;
      this.ticksRemaining = ticksRemaining;
    }
  }


  private SoulRecoveryManager() {}

  public static boolean isInSoulForm(UUID uuid) {
    return ACTIVE_SOULS.containsKey(uuid);
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onIncomingDamageWhileSoul(LivingIncomingDamageEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player && ACTIVE_SOULS.containsKey(player.getUUID())) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
  public static void onPlayerFatalDamage(LivingDeathEvent event) {
    if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
    if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;
    if (TRUE_DEATH_PENDING.remove(player.getUUID())) {
      ACTIVE_SOULS.remove(player.getUUID());
      player.setInvulnerable(false);
      player.setPose(Pose.STANDING);
      syncSoul(player, null, 0);
      return;
    }

    ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();
    DifficultyTier tier = DifficultySavedData.get(level).getActiveTier();
    if (tier == DifficultyTier.NORMAL) return;

    if (ACTIVE_SOULS.containsKey(player.getUUID())) {
      ACTIVE_SOULS.remove(player.getUUID());
      player.setInvulnerable(false);
      syncSoul(player, null, 0);
      return;
    }

    MobEffectInstance existing = player.getEffect(ModEffects.SOUL_FRACTURE);
    int currentStacks = (existing != null) ? (existing.getAmplifier() + 1) : 0;
    if (currentStacks >= 3) {
      return;
    }

    event.setCanceled(true);
    player.setHealth(1.0f);
    player.setInvulnerable(true);
    player.removeAllEffects();
    if (existing != null) {
      player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.SOUL_FRACTURE, -1, existing.getAmplifier(), false, true, true));
    }

    Vec3 bodyPos = player.position();
    Vec3 launchPos = findSafeSoulLaunchPos(level, bodyPos, player.getLookAngle());

    ActiveSoul soul = new ActiveSoul(bodyPos, launchPos, player.getYRot(), player.getXRot(), currentStacks + 1, SOUL_DURATION_TICKS);
    ACTIVE_SOULS.put(player.getUUID(), soul);

    level.playSound(null, BlockPos.containing(bodyPos), SoundEvents.SOUL_ESCAPE.value(), SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.4f, 0.65f);
    level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SOUL, bodyPos.x, bodyPos.y + 1.0D, bodyPos.z, 35, 0.4D, 0.7D, 0.4D, 0.08D);
    syncSoul(player, soul, currentStacks);
  }

  public static void handleSoulAction(ServerPlayer player, String action) {
    ActiveSoul soul = ACTIVE_SOULS.get(player.getUUID());
    if (soul == null) return;
    ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();

    switch (action) {
      case "RECLAIM_BODY" -> reviveAtBody(player, level, soul);
      case "WALL_HIT" -> {
        soul.ticksRemaining = Math.max(0, soul.ticksRemaining - WALL_PENALTY_TICKS);
        level.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.WARDEN_TENDRIL_CLICKS, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.2f, 0.6f);
        if (soul.ticksRemaining <= 0) {
          killLostSoul(player, level);
        } else {
          syncSoul(player, soul, soul.nextStacks - 1);
        }
      }
      case "EXPIRED" -> killLostSoul(player, level);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
    if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
    if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

    ActiveSoul soul = ACTIVE_SOULS.get(player.getUUID());
    if (soul == null) return;

    ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();

    player.setInvulnerable(true);
    player.setPose(Pose.CROUCHING);
    player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
    if (player.position().distanceToSqr(soul.bodyPos) > 0.05D) {
      player.teleportTo(level, soul.bodyPos.x, soul.bodyPos.y, soul.bodyPos.z, soul.bodyYaw, soul.bodyPitch);
    }

    if (soul.ticksRemaining % 4 == 0) {
      level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SOUL_FIRE_FLAME, soul.bodyPos.x, soul.bodyPos.y + 0.9D, soul.bodyPos.z, 4, 0.25D, 0.45D, 0.25D, 0.01D);
    }

    soul.ticksRemaining--;
    if (soul.ticksRemaining <= 0) {
      killLostSoul(player, level);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onEffectRemove(MobEffectEvent.Remove event) {
    if (event.getEffect() == ModEffects.SOUL_FRACTURE) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onLogout(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedOutEvent event) {
    if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
      ActiveSoul soul = ACTIVE_SOULS.remove(player.getUUID());
      if (soul != null) {
        player.setInvulnerable(false);
      }
    }
  }

  private static void reviveAtBody(ServerPlayer player, ServerLevel level, ActiveSoul soul) {
    ACTIVE_SOULS.remove(player.getUUID());
    player.setInvulnerable(false);
    player.setPose(Pose.STANDING);
    player.teleportTo(level, soul.bodyPos.x, soul.bodyPos.y, soul.bodyPos.z, soul.bodyYaw, soul.bodyPitch);
    player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);

    int amp = Math.min(2, soul.nextStacks - 1);
    player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.SOUL_FRACTURE, -1, amp, false, true, true));

    float targetHp = Math.max(1.0f, player.getMaxHealth() * 0.40f);
    player.setHealth(targetHp);
    player.invulnerableTime = 50;

    level.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.TOTEM_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 1.35f);
    level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SOUL_FIRE_FLAME, soul.bodyPos.x, soul.bodyPos.y + 1.0D, soul.bodyPos.z, 40, 0.5D, 0.8D, 0.5D, 0.1D);
    syncSoul(player, null, soul.nextStacks);
  }

  private static void killLostSoul(ServerPlayer player, ServerLevel level) {
    ACTIVE_SOULS.remove(player.getUUID());
    TRUE_DEATH_PENDING.add(player.getUUID());
    player.setInvulnerable(false);
    player.setPose(Pose.STANDING);
    syncSoul(player, null, 0);
    level.playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.WARDEN_SONIC_BOOM, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 0.6f);
    player.kill();
  }

  private static Vec3 findSafeSoulLaunchPos(ServerLevel level, Vec3 origin, Vec3 lookDir) {
    Vec3 backDir = new Vec3(-lookDir.x, 0.0D, -lookDir.z);
    if (backDir.lengthSqr() < 0.01D) backDir = new Vec3(0.0D, 0.0D, -1.0D);
    backDir = backDir.normalize();

    for (double dist = 12.0D; dist >= 3.0D; dist -= 1.0D) {
      Vec3 candidate = origin.add(backDir.x * dist, 2.8D, backDir.z * dist);
      BlockPos feet = BlockPos.containing(candidate);
      BlockPos eye = feet.above();
      if (!level.getBlockState(feet).isSuffocating(level, feet) && !level.getBlockState(eye).isSuffocating(level, eye)) {
        return candidate;
      }
    }
    return origin.add(0.0D, 3.5D, 0.0D);
  }

  private static void syncSoul(ServerPlayer player, ActiveSoul soul, int stacks) {
    if (soul == null) {
      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new SoulSyncPayload(false, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0.0D, 0, SOUL_DURATION_TICKS, stacks));
    } else {
      PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, new SoulSyncPayload(
        true, soul.bodyPos.x, soul.bodyPos.y, soul.bodyPos.z,
        soul.launchPos.x, soul.launchPos.y, soul.launchPos.z,
        soul.ticksRemaining, SOUL_DURATION_TICKS, stacks
      ));
    }
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.entity.client;

import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.entity.custom.CursedBlacksmithEntity;

import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.AnimationState;
import software.bernie.geckolib.cache.object.GeoBone;
import software.bernie.geckolib.constant.DataTickets;
import software.bernie.geckolib.model.GeoModel;
import software.bernie.geckolib.model.data.EntityModelData;

public class CursedBlacksmithModel extends GeoModel<CursedBlacksmithEntity>{

  @Override
  public ResourceLocation getAnimationResource(CursedBlacksmithEntity model) {
    return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "animations/entity/cursed_blacksmith.animation.json");
  }

  @Override
  public ResourceLocation getModelResource(CursedBlacksmithEntity model) {
    return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "geo/entity/cursed_blacksmith.geo.json");
  }

  @Override
  public ResourceLocation getTextureResource(CursedBlacksmithEntity model) {
    return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "textures/entity/cursed_blacksmith.png");
  }

  @Override
  public void setCustomAnimations(CursedBlacksmithEntity animatable, long instanceId, AnimationState<CursedBlacksmithEntity> animationState) {
    super.setCustomAnimations(animatable, instanceId, animationState);
    GeoBone head = getAnimationProcessor().getBone("head");

    if (head != null) {
      EntityModelData entityData = animationState.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY_MODEL_DATA);

      float pitch = entityData.headPitch() * ((float) Math.PI / 180F);
      float yaw = entityData.netHeadYaw() * ((float) Math.PI / 180F);

      // Optional: Clamp the head yaw to roughly 75 degrees max left/right 
      // so the neck doesn't realistically snap backwards
      yaw = net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(yaw, -1.3f, 1.3f);

      head.setRotX(pitch);
      head.setRotY(yaw);
    }
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.entity.custom;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.animations.EntityAnimationStateMachine;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.components.IProceduralAnimConfig;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.ThreeDified;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.DialoguePlaybackState;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.DialogueTransformState;

import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataAccessor;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataSerializers;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.SynchedEntityData;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Mob;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Monster;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeSupplier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.control.BodyRotationControl;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.FloatGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.LookAtPlayerGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animatable.GeoEntity;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animatable.instance.AnimatableInstanceCache;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.AnimatableManager;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animation.AnimationController;
import software.bernie.geckolib.util.GeckoLibUtil;

public class CursedBlacksmithEntity extends Monster implements GeoEntity, ThreeDified, IProceduralAnimConfig {
  private final AnimatableInstanceCache cache = GeckoLibUtil.createInstanceCache(this);
  
  public static final EntityDataAccessor<Integer> DIALOGUE_TICKS =
      SynchedEntityData.defineId(CursedBlacksmithEntity.class, EntityDataSerializers.INT);

  private final DialogueTransformState dialogueTransform = new DialogueTransformState();
  private final DialoguePlaybackState dialoguePlayback = new DialoguePlaybackState();

  // Pseudo-ECS Component attachment
  private final EntityAnimationStateMachine animationComponent;

  public CursedBlacksmithEntity(EntityType<? extends Monster> type, Level level) {
    super(type, level);

    this.animationComponent = new EntityAnimationStateMachine(() -> 
        this.entityData.get(DIALOGUE_TICKS) > 0 && !this.dialoguePlayback.isForceClosed()
    );
    this.animationComponent.onInit(this);
  }

  public static AttributeSupplier.Builder createAttributes() {
    return Mob.createMobAttributes()
      .add(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH, 7046.0D)
      .add(Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, 0.0D)
      .add(Attributes.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE, 1.0D);
  }

  @Override
  protected void registerGoals() {
    super.registerGoals();
    this.goalSelector.addGoal(0, new FloatGoal(this));
    this.goalSelector.addGoal(1, new LookAtPlayerGoal(this, Player.class, 8));
  }

  // --- IProceduralAnimConfig Declarative Abstract Configuration Mappings ---

  @Override
  public int getMinRandomInterval() {
    return 80;  // 4 seconds minimum between random secondary behaviors
  }

  @Override
  public int getMaxRandomInterval() {
    return 180; // 9 seconds maximum between random secondary behaviors
  }

  @Override
  public String getContinuousAmbientAnim() {
    return "breathing"; // The generic continuous loop
  }

  @Override
  public String getRandomActionAnim() {
    return "blink"; // The generic randomized one-shot clip
  }

  // --- GeckoLib Multi-Controller Layer Setup ---

  @Override
  public void registerControllers(AnimatableManager.ControllerRegistrar controllers) {
    // LAYER 1: Core Sequence System (Idle -> Talking -> Custom overrides)
    controllers.add(new AnimationController<>(this, "cursed_blacksmith_controller", 5, 
        this.animationComponent::handleAnimationState
    ));

    // LAYER 2: Universal Ambient & Random Action Layer
    controllers.add(new AnimationController<>(this, "cursed_blacksmith_overlay", 4, 
        this.animationComponent::handleOverlayAnimationState
    ));
  }

  @Override
  protected BodyRotationControl createBodyControl() {
    return new BodyRotationControl(this) {
      @Override
      public void clientTick() {}
    };
  }

  @Override
  public void setYBodyRot(float bodyRot) {
    super.setYBodyRot(this.getYRot());
  }

  @Override
  public void aiStep() {
    float fixedSpawnDirection = this.getYRot();
    super.aiStep();
    this.setYRot(fixedSpawnDirection);
    this.yRotO = fixedSpawnDirection;
  }

  @Override
  public void tick() {
    super.tick();

    if (!this.level().isClientSide) {
      int activeTicks = this.entityData.get(DIALOGUE_TICKS);
      if (activeTicks > 0) {
        this.entityData.set(DIALOGUE_TICKS, activeTicks - 1);
      }
    }

    this.animationComponent.onTick();
  }

  @Override
  public InteractionResult mobInteract(Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
    if (hand != InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND) return super.mobInteract(player, hand);

    if (this.level().isClientSide) {
      if (this.shouldShowDialogue() && this.dialoguePlayback.hoveredOptionIndex != -1) {
        return InteractionResult.SUCCESS;
      }
      if (!this.shouldShowDialogue() || this.dialoguePlayback.isForceClosed()) {
        this.dialoguePlayback.resetTypewriter();
      }
    } else {
      this.entityData.set(DIALOGUE_TICKS, 100);
    }
    return InteractionResult.sidedSuccess(this.level().isClientSide);
  }

  @Override
  public AnimatableInstanceCache getAnimatableInstanceCache() {
    return this.cache;
  }

  @Override
  public DialogueTransformState getDialogueTransform() {
    return this.dialogueTransform;
  }

  @Override
  public DialoguePlaybackState getDialoguePlayback() {
    return this.dialoguePlayback;
  }

  @Override
  public boolean shouldShowDialogue() {
    return this.entityData.get(DIALOGUE_TICKS) > 0;
  }

  @Override
  protected void defineSynchedData(SynchedEntityData.Builder builder) {
    super.defineSynchedData(builder);
    builder.define(DIALOGUE_TICKS, 0);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/HexaCreateCore.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.datagen.ModModelProvider;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.data.DialogueReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.hologram.BlueprintReloadListener;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.entity.ModEntities;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModItems;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.ModCreativeModeTabs;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.registry.ModEffects;
import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;

import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.Mod;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLEnvironment;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.data.event.GatherDataEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.BuildCreativeModeTabContentsEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModContainer;

@Mod(HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public class HexaCreateCore {
  public static final String MODID = "hexacreatecore";
  public static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();

  public HexaCreateCore(IEventBus modEventBus, ModContainer modContainer) {
    modEventBus.addListener(this::addCreative);
    modEventBus.addListener(this::gatherData);

    ModCreativeModeTabs.register(modEventBus);
    ModItems.register(modEventBus);
    ModEntities.register(modEventBus);
    ModEffects.register(modEventBus);

    if (FMLEnvironment.dist.isClient()) {
      preWarmHexXplat();
      modEventBus.addListener(DialogueReloadListener::onRegisterClientReloadListeners);
      modEventBus.addListener(BlueprintReloadListener::onRegisterClientReloadListeners);
      modEventBus.addListener(com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.animations.AnimationSequenceReloadListener::onRegisterClientReloadListeners);
      modEventBus.addListener((net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent event) -> {
        event.enqueueWork(com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.HexagonalGridManager::loadExternalGridConfig);
      });
    }
  }

  private void addCreative(BuildCreativeModeTabContentsEvent event) {
    // Hook for appending items directly to creative tabs dynamically
  }

  private void gatherData(final GatherDataEvent event) {
    event.getGenerator().addProvider(
      event.includeClient(), 
      new ModModelProvider(event.getGenerator().getPackOutput(), event.getExistingFileHelper())
    );
  }

  private static void preWarmHexXplat() {
    ClassLoader prev = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();
    try {
      Thread.currentThread().setContextClassLoader(HexaCreateCore.class.getClassLoader());
      Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.xplat.IXplatAbstractions").getField("INSTANCE").get(null);
      if (net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLEnvironment.dist.isClient()) {
        Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.xplat.IClientXplatAbstractions").getField("INSTANCE").get(null);
      }
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
    } finally {
      Thread.currentThread().setContextClassLoader(prev);
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/actions/HexCastingActions.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.actions;

import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.eval.vm.CastingImage;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.eval.vm.CastingVM;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.Iota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.PatternIota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexPattern;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.xplat.IXplatAbstractions;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.DialogueAction;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.LiveStackUpdatePayload;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.lang.reflect.Constructor;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class HexCastingActions {

  @DialogueAction(value = "hexacreatecore:cast_3d_spell", selfTargeted = true)
  public static void cast3DSpell(ServerPlayer serverPlayer, Entity proxyEntity) {
    try {
      String rawActionString = serverPlayer.getPersistentData().getString("hexacreatecore_raw_action");
      int directionOrdinal = 0;
      String anglesSignature = "w";

      if (rawActionString != null && rawActionString.contains(";")) {
        String[] tokensArray = rawActionString.split(";");
        if (tokensArray.length >= 3) {
          directionOrdinal = Integer.parseInt(tokensArray[1]);
          anglesSignature = tokensArray[2];
        }
      }


      HexDir[] directionValues = HexDir.values();
      if (directionOrdinal < 0 || directionOrdinal >= directionValues.length) directionOrdinal = 0;
      HexDir startDir = directionValues[directionOrdinal];

      HexPattern pattern = null;
      try {
        Class<?> signatureClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexSignature");
        Method factoryMethod = signatureClass.getMethod("fromAnglesStringUnchecked", String.class);
        factoryMethod.setAccessible(true);
        Object hexSignatureInstance = factoryMethod.invoke(null, anglesSignature);

        if (hexSignatureInstance != null) {
          Class<?> patternClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexPattern");
          Constructor<?> patternCtor = patternClass.getConstructor(HexDir.class, signatureClass);
          patternCtor.setAccessible(true);
          pattern = (HexPattern) patternCtor.newInstance(startDir, hexSignatureInstance);
        }
      } catch (Throwable fatalAllocationError) {
        fatalAllocationError.printStackTrace();
      }

      if (pattern == null) return;

      CastingVM liveCastingVM = IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.getStaffcastVM(serverPlayer, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
      if (liveCastingVM != null) {
        liveCastingVM.queueExecuteAndWrapIota(new PatternIota(pattern), serverPlayer.serverLevel());
        IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.setStaffcastImage(serverPlayer, liveCastingVM.getImage());

        CastingImage updatedImageSnapshot = liveCastingVM.getImage();
        if (updatedImageSnapshot != null) {
          List<Iota> activeIotaStack = updatedImageSnapshot.getStack();
          List<String> synchronizedStackList = new ArrayList<>();

          if (activeIotaStack != null && !activeIotaStack.isEmpty()) {
            for (Iota activeItemIota : activeIotaStack) {
              if (activeItemIota == null) continue;
              Component localizedDisplayComponent = activeItemIota.display();
              String readableIotaText = (localizedDisplayComponent != null) ? localizedDisplayComponent.getString() : activeItemIota.toString();
              synchronizedStackList.add(readableIotaText);
            }
          }

          String readableRavensmind = "";
          try {
            Field compField = updatedImageSnapshot.getClass().getDeclaredField("components");
            compField.setAccessible(true);
            Object compObj = compField.get(updatedImageSnapshot);

            if (compObj instanceof Map<?, ?> componentsMap) {
              for (Map.Entry<?, ?> entry : componentsMap.entrySet()) {
                if (entry.getKey() != null && entry.getKey().toString().contains("ravenmind")) {
                  if (entry.getValue() instanceof Iota ravenIota) {
                    Component comp = ravenIota.display();
                    readableRavensmind = (comp != null) ? comp.getString() : ravenIota.toString();
                  }
                  break;
                }
              }
            }
          } catch (Throwable t) {
            t.printStackTrace();
          }

          PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(
            serverPlayer,
            new LiveStackUpdatePayload(synchronizedStackList, readableRavensmind)
          );
        }
      }
    } catch (Exception serverException) {
      serverException.printStackTrace();
    }
  }

  @DialogueAction(value = "hexacreatecore:clear_3d_stack", selfTargeted = true)
  public static void clear3DStack(ServerPlayer serverPlayer, Entity proxyEntity) {
    IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.clearCastingData(serverPlayer);
    IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.setPatterns(serverPlayer, List.of());
    IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.setStaffcastImage(serverPlayer, new CastingImage());

    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(serverPlayer, new LiveStackUpdatePayload(List.of(), ""));

    try {
      Class<?> packetClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.common.msgs.MsgClearSpiralPatternsS2C");
      Constructor<?> ctor = packetClass.getConstructor(UUID.class);
      CustomPacketPayload msg = (CustomPacketPayload) ctor.newInstance(serverPlayer.getUUID());
      IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.sendPacketToPlayer(serverPlayer, msg);
      IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.sendPacketTracking(serverPlayer, msg);
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      t.printStackTrace();
    }
  }

  @DialogueAction(value = "hexacreatecore:overreach_penalty", selfTargeted = true)
  public static void overreachPenalty(ServerPlayer serverPlayer, Entity proxyEntity) {
    var darkness = serverPlayer.getEffect(MobEffects.DARKNESS);
    if (darkness == null || darkness.getDuration() < 60) {
      serverPlayer.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.DARKNESS, 60, 0, false, false, false));
    }

    var blindness = serverPlayer.getEffect(MobEffects.BLINDNESS);
    if (blindness == null || blindness.getDuration() < 60) {
      serverPlayer.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.BLINDNESS, 60, 0, false, false, false));
    }

    String rawAction = serverPlayer.getPersistentData().getString("hexacreatecore_raw_action");
    if (rawAction != null && rawAction.endsWith(";wither")) {
      var wither = serverPlayer.getEffect(MobEffects.WITHER);
      if (wither == null || wither.getAmplifier() < 1 || wither.getDuration() < 60) {
        serverPlayer.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.WITHER, 60, 1, false, false, false));
      }
    }
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/ArcaneGridRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;

import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "hexacreatecore", value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class ArcaneGridRenderer {

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderLevelStage(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;
    if (!HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;
    if (EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode()) return;


    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;

    List<ArcaneNode3D> nodes = HexagonalGridManager.getActiveNodes();
    if (nodes.isEmpty()) return;

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getPoseStack();
    Vec3 cam = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera.getPosition();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

    com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();

    // 1. Render Arcane 3D Node Prisms
    for (ArcaneNode3D node : nodes) {
      Vec3 pos = node.worldPos();
      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(pos.x - cam.x, pos.y - cam.y, pos.z - cam.z);

      var camera = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera;
      poseStack.mulPose(com.mojang.math.Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(-camera.getYRot() - 180.0F));
      poseStack.mulPose(com.mojang.math.Axis.XP.rotationDegrees(-camera.getXRot()));

      float size = node.isActivated() ? 0.12f : 0.06f;
      RawGeometry.drawArcaneNodePrism(poseStack, bufferSource, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, size, node.nodeColor());
      poseStack.popPose();
    }

    // 2. Render Lines (Both persistent history and current drawing path threads)
    VertexConsumer lineBuffer = bufferSource.getBuffer(RenderType.lines());
    
    // Draw past drawn lines
    for (List<Integer> pastPath : HexagonalGridManager.getPersistentLines()) {
      for (int i = 0; pastPath.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
        renderLineSegment(poseStack, lineBuffer, cam, nodes.get(pastPath.get(i)), nodes.get(pastPath.get(i + 1)), 140);
      }
    }
    // Draw current active line thread
    List<Integer> currentPath = HexagonalGridManager.getActiveDrawingPath();
    for (int i = 0; currentPath.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      renderLineSegment(poseStack, lineBuffer, cam, nodes.get(currentPath.get(i)), nodes.get(currentPath.get(i + 1)), 255);
    }

    bufferSource.endBatch(RenderType.gui());
    bufferSource.endBatch(RenderType.lines());

    com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem.enableDepthTest();
  }

  private static void renderLineSegment(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 cam, ArcaneNode3D start, ArcaneNode3D end, int alpha) {
    if (start == null || end == null) return;
    buffer.addVertex(poseStack.last().pose(), (float)(start.worldPos().x - cam.x), (float)(start.worldPos().y - cam.y), (float)(start.worldPos().z - cam.z)).setColor(34, 183, 209, alpha).setNormal(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    buffer.addVertex(poseStack.last().pose(), (float)(end.worldPos().x - cam.x), (float)(end.worldPos().y - cam.y), (float)(end.worldPos().z - cam.z)).setColor(34, 183, 209, alpha).setNormal(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
  }

  /**
   * Draws a multi-row scrollable stack menu floating gracefully in the air inside the hexagonal cage.
   */

  private static void drawStackRow(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource, Minecraft mc, String text, float x, float y, float w, float h, int borderColor) {
    List<FormattedCharSequence> splitLines = mc.font.split(Component.literal(text), (int) w - 8);
    RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, (net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource) bufferSource, mc.font,
      x, y, 0.0f, w, h, 2.0f, 2.0f,
      0xDD0D0814, borderColor, splitLines, true
    );
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/ClientRenderSubscriber.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "hexacreatecore", value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class ClientRenderSubscriber {

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderLevelStage(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() == RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_ENTITIES) {
      Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
      if (mc.player == null || !HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;

      Vec3 projectCameraPos = mc.gameRenderer.getMainCamera().getPosition();

      // Pass 1: Render the solid 3D cell-shaded purple pattern trace lines
      HexCastingGeometry.render3DCellShadedLines(
        event.getPoseStack(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getPersistentLines(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getActiveDrawingPath(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getActiveNodes(),
        projectCameraPos,
        HexagonalGridManager.getPointColorRaw()
      );

      // Compute active 2D horizontal cylinder shell distance parameters matching our tick handler
      double deltaX = projectCameraPos.x - HexagonalGridManager.getCenterAnchorPoint().x;
      double deltaZ = projectCameraPos.z - HexagonalGridManager.getCenterAnchorPoint().z;
      double cameraHorizontalDist = Math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaZ * deltaZ);

      // 2.0D is your defined HEX_RADIUS layout boundary threshold parameter
      int activeShellIndex = (int) (cameraHorizontalDist / 2.0D); 

      MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

      // =========================================================================
      // SINGLE VALID HUD PASS: Invokes your verified vertical multi-box geometry panel layout!
      // DELETED: The stray call to HexCastingHudGeometry has been completely removed to purge the cyan box!
      HexCastingGeometry.render3DStackHUD(
        event.getPoseStack(),
        bufferSource,
        HexagonalGridManager.getCenterAnchorPoint(),
        projectCameraPos,
        HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamYaw(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPitch(),
        activeShellIndex,
        HexagonalGridManager.getStackStrings(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getRavensmindString(),
        HexagonalGridManager.getScrollIndex()
      );
      // =========================================================================

      // Command the buffer source to immediately submit your custom quads straight to shader layers
      bufferSource.endBatch(com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
      bufferSource.endBatch(); 
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookData.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.CustomData;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class EverbookData {

  private static final String NBT_ROOT = "HexaEverbook";

  public record PatternEntry(
    String id,
    String angles,
    String startDir,
    String label,
    float x,
    float y,
    int orderIndex
  ) {
    public PatternEntry withPos(float nx, float ny) {
      return new PatternEntry(id, angles, startDir, label, nx, ny, orderIndex);
    }

    public PatternEntry withOrder(int newOrder) {
      return new PatternEntry(id, angles, startDir, label, x, y, newOrder);
    }

    public PatternEntry duplicateWithOffset(float dx, float dy, int newOrder) {
      return new PatternEntry(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), angles, startDir, label, x + dx, y + dy, newOrder);
    }

    public CompoundTag toNbt() {
      CompoundTag t = new CompoundTag();
      t.putString("id", id);
      t.putString("angles", angles);
      t.putString("startDir", startDir);
      t.putString("label", label);
      t.putFloat("x", x);
      t.putFloat("y", y);
      t.putInt("order", orderIndex);
      return t;
    }

    public static PatternEntry fromNbt(CompoundTag t) {
      return new PatternEntry(
        t.contains("id") ? t.getString("id") : UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
        t.getString("angles"),
        t.contains("startDir") ? t.getString("startDir") : "EAST",
        t.getString("label"),
        t.getFloat("x"),
        t.getFloat("y"),
        t.getInt("order")
      );
    }
  }

  public record BookPage(
    String title,
    List<PatternEntry> entries
  ) {
    public List<PatternEntry> orderedEntries() {
      List<PatternEntry> sorted = new ArrayList<>(entries);
      sorted.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PatternEntry::orderIndex));
      return sorted;
    }

    public CompoundTag toNbt() {
      CompoundTag t = new CompoundTag();
      t.putString("title", title);
      ListTag list = new ListTag();
      for (PatternEntry e : entries) list.add(e.toNbt());
      t.put("entries", list);
      return t;
    }

    public static BookPage fromNbt(CompoundTag t) {
      String title = t.contains("title") ? t.getString("title") : "Untitled Chapter";
      List<PatternEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
      if (t.contains("entries", Tag.TAG_LIST)) {
        ListTag list = t.getList("entries", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
        for (int i = 0; list.size() > i; i++) {
          entries.add(PatternEntry.fromNbt(list.getCompound(i)));
        }
      }
      return new BookPage(title, entries);
    }
  }

  public record BookState(
    int activePage,
    List<BookPage> pages
  ) {
    public BookPage currentPage() {
      if (pages.isEmpty()) return new BookPage("Chapter I", new ArrayList<>());
      int idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(activePage, pages.size() - 1));
      return pages.get(idx);
    }

    public CompoundTag toNbt() {
      CompoundTag t = new CompoundTag();
      t.putInt("activePage", activePage);
      ListTag pageList = new ListTag();
      for (BookPage p : pages) pageList.add(p.toNbt());
      t.put("pages", pageList);
      return t;
    }

    public static BookState fromNbt(CompoundTag t) {
      int active = t.getInt("activePage");
      List<BookPage> pages = new ArrayList<>();
      if (t.contains("pages", Tag.TAG_LIST)) {
        ListTag list = t.getList("pages", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
        for (int i = 0; list.size() > i; i++) {
          pages.add(BookPage.fromNbt(list.getCompound(i)));
        }
      }
      if (pages.isEmpty()) {
        pages.add(new BookPage("Chapter I", new ArrayList<>()));
      }
      return new BookState(Math.max(0, Math.min(active, pages.size() - 1)), pages);
    }
  }

  private EverbookData() {}

  public static BookState read(ItemStack stack) {
    CustomData custom = stack.get(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA);
    if (custom == null) {
      return new BookState(0, new ArrayList<>(List.of(new BookPage("Chapter I", new ArrayList<>()))));
    }
    CompoundTag root = custom.copyTag();
    if (!root.contains(NBT_ROOT, Tag.TAG_COMPOUND)) {
      return new BookState(0, new ArrayList<>(List.of(new BookPage("Chapter I", new ArrayList<>()))));
    }
    return BookState.fromNbt(root.getCompound(NBT_ROOT));
  }

  public static void write(ItemStack stack, BookState state) {
    CustomData.update(DataComponents.CUSTOM_DATA, stack, tag -> tag.put(NBT_ROOT, state.toNbt()));
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookEvalPayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.StringTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public record EverbookEvalPayload(
  boolean isResponse,
  CompoundTag data
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public static final Type<EverbookEvalPayload> TYPE = new Type<>(
    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "everbook_eval")
  );

  public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, EverbookEvalPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
    ByteBufCodecs.BOOL, EverbookEvalPayload::isResponse,
    ByteBufCodecs.COMPOUND_TAG, EverbookEvalPayload::data,
    EverbookEvalPayload::new
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
    return TYPE;
  }

  public static EverbookEvalPayload request(List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries) {
    CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
    ListTag list = new ListTag();
    for (EverbookData.PatternEntry e : entries) list.add(e.toNbt());
    tag.put("entries", list);
    return new EverbookEvalPayload(false, tag);
  }

  public static void handle(EverbookEvalPayload payload, IPayloadContext ctx) {
    ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
      if (!payload.isResponse() && ctx.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) {
        List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        if (payload.data().contains("entries", Tag.TAG_LIST)) {
          ListTag list = payload.data().getList("entries", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
          for (int i = 0; list.size() > i; i++) {
            entries.add(EverbookData.PatternEntry.fromNbt(list.getCompound(i)));
          }
        }
        EverbookSandboxEvaluator.EvalResult res = EverbookSandboxEvaluator.evaluateNonDestructive(sp, entries);
        CompoundTag out = new CompoundTag();
        out.putBoolean("success", res.success());
        out.putBoolean("collapsed", res.collapsedRift());
        out.putInt("recursions", res.recursionCount());
        out.putString("summary", res.summaryText());
        ListTag stackList = new ListTag();
        for (String s : res.stackPreview()) stackList.add(StringTag.valueOf(s));
        out.put("stack", stackList);
        PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(sp, new EverbookEvalPayload(true, out));
      } else if (payload.isResponse()) {
        CompoundTag d = payload.data();
        List<String> stack = new ArrayList<>();
        if (d.contains("stack", Tag.TAG_LIST)) {
          ListTag list = d.getList("stack", Tag.TAG_STRING);
          for (int i = 0; list.size() > i; i++) stack.add(list.getString(i));
        }
        EverbookSessionState.onEvaluationResult(
          d.getBoolean("success"),
          d.getBoolean("collapsed"),
          d.getInt("recursions"),
          d.getString("summary"),
          stack
        );
      }
    });
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookHexWriter.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.HexAPI;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.Iota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.ListIota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.PatternIota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexPattern;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.xplat.IXplatAbstractions;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class EverbookHexWriter {

  private EverbookHexWriter() {}

  public static void inscribeToHexItem(ServerPlayer player, ItemStack targetStack, List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries) {
    if (targetStack.isEmpty() || entries.isEmpty()) return;

    List<Iota> iotas = new ArrayList<>(entries.size());
    for (EverbookData.PatternEntry entry : entries) {
      HexDir startDir;
      try { startDir = HexDir.valueOf(entry.startDir()); } catch (Exception ignored) { startDir = HexDir.EAST; }
      HexPattern pattern = buildPattern(startDir, entry.angles());
      if (pattern != null) iotas.add(new PatternIota(pattern));
    }
    if (iotas.isEmpty()) return;

    var iotaHolder = IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.findDataHolder(targetStack);
    if (iotaHolder != null) {
      ListIota listIota = new ListIota(iotas);
      if (iotaHolder.writeIota(listIota, true)) {
        iotaHolder.writeIota(listIota, false);
        player.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§d[Everbook] §aInscribed " + iotas.size() + " Iotas!"), true);
        return;
      }
    }

    var hexHolder = IXplatAbstractions.INSTANCE.findHexHolder(targetStack);
    if (hexHolder != null && !hexHolder.hasHex()) {
      hexHolder.writeHex(iotas, HexAPI.instance().getColorizer(player), 0L);
      player.displayClientMessage(Component.literal("§d[Everbook] §aInscribed " + iotas.size() + " Iotas into casting item!"), true);
    }
  }

  private static HexPattern buildPattern(HexDir startDir, String angles) {
    try {
      Class<?> signatureClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexSignature");
      var factoryMethod = signatureClass.getMethod("fromAnglesStringUnchecked", String.class);
      Object signature = factoryMethod.invoke(null, angles);
      var constructor = HexPattern.class.getConstructor(HexDir.class, signatureClass);
      constructor.setAccessible(true);
      return constructor.newInstance(startDir, signature);
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookMiniGridConfig.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import net.neoforged.fml.loading.FMLPaths;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public final class EverbookMiniGridConfig {

  public static int pointColor = 0xFFFFD700;
  public static int pointHoverColor = 0xFF10B981;
  public static int pointActiveColor = 0xFF34D399;
  public static int lineColor = 0xFF10B981;
  public static int outlineColor = 0xFF4A0416;
  public static float lineSize = 0.011f;
  public static float outlineSizeMultiplier = 1.95f;
  public static double wobbleIntensity = 0.015D;

  private static boolean loaded = false;

  private EverbookMiniGridConfig() {}

  public static void reload() {
    loaded = false;
    ensureLoaded();
  }

  public static void ensureLoaded() {
    if (loaded) return;
    loaded = true;
    try {
      Path cfgPath = FMLPaths.CONFIGDIR.get().resolve("hexacreatecore").resolve("everbook_minigrid.json");
      if (!Files.exists(cfgPath)) {
        Files.createDirectories(cfgPath.getParent());
        JsonObject def = new JsonObject();
        def.addProperty("point_color", "0xFFFFD700");
        def.addProperty("point_hover_color_hex", "0xFF10B981");
        def.addProperty("line_color", "0xFF10B981");
        def.addProperty("outline_color", "0xFF4A0416");
        def.addProperty("line_size", 0.011f);
        def.addProperty("outline_size", 1.95f);
        def.addProperty("wobble_intensity", 0.015D);
        Gson gson = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
        Files.writeString(cfgPath, gson.toJson(def), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return;
      }

      JsonObject obj = JsonParser.parseString(Files.readString(cfgPath, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).getAsJsonObject();
      if (obj.has("point_color")) pointColor = parseHex(obj.get("point_color").getAsString(), pointColor);
      if (obj.has("point_hover_color_hex")) {
        pointHoverColor = parseHex(obj.get("point_hover_color_hex").getAsString(), pointHoverColor);
        pointActiveColor = pointHoverColor;
      }
      if (obj.has("line_color")) lineColor = parseHex(obj.get("line_color").getAsString(), lineColor);
      if (obj.has("outline_color")) outlineColor = parseHex(obj.get("outline_color").getAsString(), outlineColor);
      if (obj.has("line_size")) lineSize = obj.get("line_size").getAsFloat();
      if (obj.has("outline_size")) outlineSizeMultiplier = obj.get("outline_size").getAsFloat();
      if (obj.has("wobble_intensity")) wobbleIntensity = obj.get("wobble_intensity").getAsDouble();
    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
  }

  private static int parseHex(String raw, int fallback) {
    try {
      String clean = raw.replace("#", "").trim();
      if (clean.startsWith("0x") || clean.startsWith("0X")) clean = clean.substring(2);
      return (int) Long.parseLong(clean, 16);
    } catch (Exception e) {
      return fallback;
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookRiftMath.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class EverbookRiftMath {

  public record HitResult(
    int patternIndex,
    boolean inscribeButtonHovered,
    boolean evalButtonHovered,
    int miniGridNodeIndex,
    int totalRows,
    int autoScrollDir
  ) {}

  public record Point2D(float x, float y) {}
  private record AxialStep(int dq, int dr) {}

  public static final float RIFT_HALF_W = 1.70f;
  public static final float RIFT_HALF_H = 1.12f;
  public static final float CELL_SIZE = 0.28f;
  public static final int COLS_PER_ROW = 9;
  public static final float PATTERN_TOP_Y = 0.68f;

  public static final int MINI_COLS = 8;
  public static final int MINI_ROWS = 9;
  public static final float MINI_CENTER_X = 2.25f;
  public static final float MINI_CENTER_Y = 0.0f;
  public static final float MINI_STEP = 0.11f;

  private static final List<AxialStep> AXIAL_STEPS = List.of(
    new AxialStep(1, -1),
    new AxialStep(1, 0),
    new AxialStep(0, 1),
    new AxialStep(-1, 1),
    new AxialStep(-1, 0),
    new AxialStep(0, -1)
  );

  private EverbookRiftMath() {}

  public static Point2D getMiniNodePoint(int rowIndex, int colIndex) {
    float rowShift = (rowIndex % 2 == 1) ? (MINI_STEP * 0.5f) : 0.0f;
    float posX = MINI_CENTER_X + ((colIndex - (MINI_COLS - 1) * 0.5f) * MINI_STEP) + rowShift;
    float posY = MINI_CENTER_Y + (((MINI_ROWS - 1) * 0.5f - rowIndex) * (MINI_STEP * 0.866f));
    return new Point2D(posX, posY);
  }

  public static float[] getMiniNodeLocalPos(int rowIndex, int colIndex) {
    Point2D pt = getMiniNodePoint(rowIndex, colIndex);
    return new float[]{ pt.x(), pt.y() };
  }


  public static void drawPatternStrokes(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer quadConsumer, float centerX, float centerY, float depthZ, float scale, String angles, String startDirName, boolean isSelected, boolean isHovered, int introspectionDepth, float timeSeconds) {
    if (angles == null || angles.isEmpty()) return;
    HexDir startDir;
    try { startDir = HexDir.valueOf(startDirName); } catch (Exception ignored) { startDir = HexDir.EAST; }

    // Exact 60-degree screen-space unit vectors matching HexDir enum order:
    // NORTH_EAST (60°), EAST (0°), SOUTH_EAST (-60°), SOUTH_WEST (-120°), WEST (180°), NORTH_WEST (120°)
    double[] dirAnglesRad = {
      Math.toRadians(60.0D),
      Math.toRadians(0.0D),
      Math.toRadians(-60.0D),
      Math.toRadians(-120.0D),
      Math.toRadians(180.0D),
      Math.toRadians(120.0D)
    };

    List<Point2D> points = new ArrayList<>(angles.length() + 2);
    float curX = 0.0f, curY = 0.0f;
    points.add(new Point2D(curX, curY));

    int dirIdx = startDir.ordinal();
    curX += (float) Math.cos(dirAnglesRad[dirIdx]);
    curY += (float) Math.sin(dirAnglesRad[dirIdx]);
    points.add(new Point2D(curX, curY));

    for (int i = 0; angles.length() > i; i++) {
      int turn = switch (angles.charAt(i)) {
        case 'w' -> 0; // straight
        case 'e' -> 1; // 60° right (clockwise)
        case 'd' -> 2; // 120° right
        case 's' -> 3; // 180° back
        case 'a' -> 4; // 120° left (counter-clockwise)
        default -> 5;  // 'q': 60° left (counter-clockwise)
      };
      dirIdx = Math.floorMod(dirIdx + turn, 6);
      curX += (float) Math.cos(dirAnglesRad[dirIdx]);
      curY += (float) Math.sin(dirAnglesRad[dirIdx]);
      points.add(new Point2D(curX, curY));
    }

    float minX = Float.MAX_VALUE, maxX = -Float.MAX_VALUE, minY = Float.MAX_VALUE, maxY = -Float.MAX_VALUE;
    for (Point2D point : points) {
      minX = Math.min(minX, point.x()); maxX = Math.max(maxX, point.x());
      minY = Math.min(minY, point.y()); maxY = Math.max(maxY, point.y());
    }

    float span = Math.max(1.0f, Math.max(maxX - minX, maxY - minY));
    float midX = (minX + maxX) * 0.5f, midY = (minY + maxY) * 0.5f;
    float normScale = (scale * 2.0f) / span;
    float halfThick = isSelected ? 0.0115f : 0.0095f;

    Matrix4f matrix = poseStack.last().pose();
    for (int i = 0; points.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      Point2D p0 = points.get(i), p1 = points.get(i + 1);
      float x0 = centerX + (p0.x() - midX) * normScale, y0 = centerY + (p0.y() - midY) * normScale;
      float x1 = centerX + (p1.x() - midX) * normScale, y1 = centerY + (p1.y() - midY) * normScale;

      float dx = x1 - x0, dy = y1 - y0;
      float len = (float) Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (len < 1.0e-5f) continue;
      float nx = (-dy / len) * halfThick, ny = (dx / len) * halfThick;

      float wave0 = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((i * 1.4f) - (timeSeconds * 4.2f) + (centerX * 3.0f));
      float wave1 = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(((i + 1) * 1.4f) - (timeSeconds * 4.2f) + (centerX * 3.0f));

      int depthPrimary = switch (Math.floorMod(introspectionDepth, 3)) {
        case 1 -> 0xFF00F5FF; // Introspection depth 1: Bright Cyan -> Genesis Red
        case 2 -> 0xFF2563EB; // Introspection depth 2: Deep Blue -> Genesis Red
        default -> 0xFFA855F7; // Depth 0 / 3+: Purple -> Genesis Red
      };

      int col0 = isSelected ? lerpColor(0xFF10B981, 0xFF6EE7B7, wave0)
        : (isHovered ? lerpColor(0xFFFFD700, 0xFFFF1E56, wave0) : lerpColor(depthPrimary, 0xFFFF1E56, wave0));
      int col1 = isSelected ? lerpColor(0xFF10B981, 0xFF6EE7B7, wave1)
        : (isHovered ? lerpColor(0xFFFFD700, 0xFFFF1E56, wave1) : lerpColor(depthPrimary, 0xFFFF1E56, wave1));

      addQuadVertex(quadConsumer, matrix, x0 - nx, y0 - ny, depthZ, col0);
      addQuadVertex(quadConsumer, matrix, x1 - nx, y1 - ny, depthZ, col1);
      addQuadVertex(quadConsumer, matrix, x1 + nx, y1 + ny, depthZ, col1);
      addQuadVertex(quadConsumer, matrix, x0 + nx, y0 + ny, depthZ, col0);
    }
  }


  public static void compileMiniGridStroke(List<Integer> path) {
    if (path == null || path.size() < 2) return;
    StringBuilder anglesBuilder = new StringBuilder();
    HexDir previousDir = null, initialDir = HexDir.EAST;

    for (int i = 0; path.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      int nodeA = path.get(i), nodeB = path.get(i + 1);
      Point2D ptA = getMiniNodePoint(nodeA / MINI_COLS, nodeA % MINI_COLS);
      Point2D ptB = getMiniNodePoint(nodeB / MINI_COLS, nodeB % MINI_COLS);

      // Snap exact visual screen-space angle (in degrees) to HexDir!
      double deg = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(ptB.y() - ptA.y(), ptB.x() - ptA.x()));
      if (0.0D > deg) deg += 360.0D;
      int sector = (int) Math.round(deg / 60.0D) % 6;
      HexDir stepDir = switch (sector) {
        case 0 -> HexDir.EAST;       // 0°
        case 1 -> HexDir.NORTH_EAST; // 60°
        case 2 -> HexDir.NORTH_WEST; // 120°
        case 3 -> HexDir.WEST;       // 180°
        case 4 -> HexDir.SOUTH_WEST; // 240°
        default -> HexDir.SOUTH_EAST;// 300°
      };

      if (i == 0) initialDir = stepDir;
      else if (previousDir != null) {
        int turnDiff = Math.floorMod(stepDir.ordinal() - previousDir.ordinal(), 6);
        anglesBuilder.append(switch (turnDiff) { case 0 -> 'w'; case 1 -> 'e'; case 2 -> 'd'; case 3 -> 's'; case 4 -> 'a'; default -> 'q'; });
      }
      previousDir = stepDir;
    }
    EverbookSessionState.appendDrawnPattern(anglesBuilder.toString(), initialDir.name(), "Sigil");
  }


  public static HitResult raycastRiftWorkspace(Vec3 eyePos, Vec3 lookVec, Vec3 anchorPos, float yaw, float pitch, List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries, float scrollOffset) {
    int totalRows = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(entries.size() / (double) COLS_PER_ROW));
    Quaternionf rotation = new Quaternionf().rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-yaw - 180.0f)).rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(-pitch));
    Quaternionf invRotation = new Quaternionf(rotation).conjugate();

    Vector3f localOrigin = new Vector3f((float) (eyePos.x - anchorPos.x), (float) (eyePos.y - anchorPos.y), (float) (eyePos.z - anchorPos.z));
    invRotation.transform(localOrigin);
    Vector3f localDir = new Vector3f((float) lookVec.x, (float) lookVec.y, (float) lookVec.z);
    invRotation.transform(localDir);

    if (Math.abs(localDir.z) < 1.0e-5f) return new HitResult(-1, false, false, -1, totalRows, 0);
    float hitDistance = -localOrigin.z / localDir.z;
    if (hitDistance <= 0.0f) return new HitResult(-1, false, false, -1, totalRows, 0);

    float hitX = localOrigin.x + (localDir.x * hitDistance);
    float hitY = localOrigin.y + (localDir.y * hitDistance);
    boolean inscribeHovered = Math.abs(hitX - (-2.42f)) < 0.44f && Math.abs(hitY - 0.72f) < 0.15f;
    boolean evalHovered = Math.abs(hitX - (-2.42f)) < 0.44f && Math.abs(hitY - 0.36f) < 0.15f;

    int hitMiniNode = -1;
    float bestMiniDistSq = 0.055f * 0.055f;
    for (int row = 0; MINI_ROWS > row; row++) {
      for (int col = 0; MINI_COLS > col; col++) {
        Point2D nodePos = getMiniNodePoint(row, col);
        float dx = hitX - nodePos.x();
        float dy = hitY - nodePos.y();
        float distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (bestMiniDistSq > distSq) {
          bestMiniDistSq = distSq;
          hitMiniNode = (row * MINI_COLS) + col;
        }
      }
    }

    int autoScroll = (Math.abs(hitX) < RIFT_HALF_W) ? ((hitY > 0.78f) ? -1 : ((-0.82f > hitY) ? 1 : 0)) : 0;
    int hitPattern = -1;
    float startX = -((COLS_PER_ROW - 1) * CELL_SIZE) * 0.5f;
    for (int i = 0; entries.size() > i; i++) {
      float visibleRow = (i / COLS_PER_ROW) - scrollOffset;
      if (-0.6f > visibleRow || visibleRow > 5.2f) continue;
      float cellX = startX + ((i % COLS_PER_ROW) * CELL_SIZE);
      float cellY = PATTERN_TOP_Y - (visibleRow * CELL_SIZE);
      if (Math.abs(hitX - cellX) < (CELL_SIZE * 0.46f) && Math.abs(hitY - cellY) < (CELL_SIZE * 0.46f)) {
        hitPattern = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    return new HitResult(hitPattern, inscribeHovered, evalHovered, hitMiniNode, totalRows, autoScroll);
  }

  public static void drawBoldAbsolutionPattern(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer quadConsumer, float centerX, float centerY, float depthZ, float scale, String angles, String startDirName, boolean isSelected, boolean isHovered, float timeSeconds) {
    if (angles == null || angles.isEmpty()) return;
    HexDir startDir;
    try { startDir = HexDir.valueOf(startDirName); } catch (Exception ignored) { startDir = HexDir.EAST; }

    List<Point2D> points = new ArrayList<>(angles.length() + 2);
    float axialQ = 0.0f, axialR = 0.0f;
    points.add(new Point2D(0.0f, 0.0f));
    int currentOrdinal = startDir.ordinal();
    AxialStep firstStep = AXIAL_STEPS.get(currentOrdinal);
    axialQ += firstStep.dq(); axialR += firstStep.dr();
    points.add(new Point2D((float) (axialQ + axialR * 0.5D), (float) (-axialR * 0.8660254D)));

    for (int i = 0; angles.length() > i; i++) {
      int turnOffset = switch (angles.charAt(i)) { case 'w' -> 0; case 'e' -> 1; case 'd' -> 2; case 's' -> 3; case 'a' -> 4; default -> 5; };
      currentOrdinal = Math.floorMod(currentOrdinal + turnOffset, 6);
      AxialStep step = AXIAL_STEPS.get(currentOrdinal);
      axialQ += step.dq(); axialR += step.dr();
      points.add(new Point2D((float) (axialQ + axialR * 0.5D), (float) (-axialR * 0.8660254D)));
    }

    float minX = Float.MAX_VALUE, maxX = -Float.MAX_VALUE, minY = Float.MAX_VALUE, maxY = -Float.MAX_VALUE;
    for (Point2D pt : points) {
      minX = Math.min(minX, pt.x()); maxX = Math.max(maxX, pt.x());
      minY = Math.min(minY, pt.y()); maxY = Math.max(maxY, pt.y());
    }
    float span = Math.max(1.0f, Math.max(maxX - minX, maxY - minY));
    float midX = (minX + maxX) * 0.5f, midY = (minY + maxY) * 0.5f;
    float normScale = (scale * 2.0f) / span;

    // Outer glow color: Green when selected, warm gold when hovered, deep purple-crimson normally
    int glowArgb = isSelected ? 0x8810B981 : (isHovered ? 0x77FFD700 : 0x557E22CE);
    Matrix4f matrix = poseStack.last().pose();

    for (int i = 0; points.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      Point2D p0 = points.get(i), p1 = points.get(i + 1);
      float x0 = centerX + (p0.x() - midX) * normScale, y0 = centerY + (p0.y() - midY) * normScale;
      float x1 = centerX + (p1.x() - midX) * normScale, y1 = centerY + (p1.y() - midY) * normScale;

      float wave0 = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((i * 1.1f) - (timeSeconds * 4.2f));
      float wave1 = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(((i + 1) * 1.1f) - (timeSeconds * 4.2f));
      int core0 = lerpColor(0xFFA855F7, 0xFFFF4D79, wave0); // Purple -> Genesis Light Red
      int core1 = lerpColor(0xFFA855F7, 0xFFFF4D79, wave1);

      emitRibbonSegment(quadConsumer, matrix, x0, y0, x1, y1, depthZ, 0.022f, glowArgb, glowArgb);
      emitRibbonSegment(quadConsumer, matrix, x0, y0, x1, y1, depthZ + 0.002f, 0.011f, core0, core1);
    }
  }

  private static void emitRibbonSegment(VertexConsumer vc, Matrix4f m, float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float z, float halfThick, int c0, int c1) {
    float dx = x1 - x0, dy = y1 - y0;
    float len = (float) Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (len < 1.0e-5f) return;
    float nx = (-dy / len) * halfThick, ny = (dx / len) * halfThick;
    vc.addVertex(m, x0 - nx, y0 - ny, z).setColor(FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0));
    vc.addVertex(m, x0 + nx, y0 + ny, z).setColor(FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0));
    vc.addVertex(m, x1 + nx, y1 + ny, z).setColor(FastColor.ARGB32.red(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c1));
    vc.addVertex(m, x1 - nx, y1 - ny, z).setColor(FastColor.ARGB32.red(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c1), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c1));
  }

  private static void addQuadVertex(VertexConsumer vc, Matrix4f m, float x, float y, float z, int argb) {
    vc.addVertex(m, x, y, z).setColor(FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb), FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(argb));
  }

  private static int lerpColor(int c0, int c1, float t) {
    int a = (int) (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0) + (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c1) - FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0)) * t);
    int r = (int) (FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0) + (FastColor.ARGB32.red(c1) - FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0)) * t);
    int g = (int) (FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0) + (FastColor.ARGB32.green(c1) - FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0)) * t);
    int b = (int) (FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0) + (FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c1) - FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0)) * t);
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(a, r, g, b);
  }

}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookRiftRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.HexagonalGridManager;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.CompositeDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.ShaderDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks.PortalShaderHook;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

import java.util.List;

import static com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookRiftMath.*;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class EverbookRiftRenderer {

  private static Vec3 smoothedRiftPos = null;
  private static float smoothedRiftYaw = 0.0f;
  private static double smoothedRiftDropY = 0.0D;
  private static final long RENDER_EPOCH_NANOS = System.nanoTime();

  private EverbookRiftRenderer() {}

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderWorld(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;
    if (!EverbookSessionState.isActive()) { smoothedRiftPos = null; smoothedRiftDropY = 0.0D; return; }
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;

    Vec3 cam = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera.getPosition();
    Vec3 center = HexagonalGridManager.getCenterAnchorPoint();
    float camYaw = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamYaw();

    float dt = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
    double sineEase = Math.sin(Math.min(1.0D, dt * 2.8D) * (Math.PI / 2.0D));
    smoothedRiftDropY = Mth.lerp(sineEase, smoothedRiftDropY, EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode() ? -2.0D : 0.0D);

    if (!EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode() && center != null) {
      double dx = cam.x - center.x, dz = cam.z - center.z;
      double orbitRadius = 2.0D * ((int) (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz) / 2.0D) + 2);
      double yawRad = Math.toRadians(camYaw), lookX = -Math.sin(yawRad), lookZ = Math.cos(yawRad);
      double b = dx * lookX + dz * lookZ, disc = (b * b) - ((dx * dx + dz * dz) - (orbitRadius * orbitRadius));
      double goalX = (disc >= 0.0D) ? (cam.x + lookX * (-b + Math.sqrt(disc))) : (center.x + lookX * orbitRadius);
      double goalZ = (disc >= 0.0D) ? (cam.z + lookZ * (-b + Math.sqrt(disc))) : (center.z + lookZ * orbitRadius);
      Vec3 goalPos = new Vec3(goalX, center.y + 4.50D + Math.sin(System.currentTimeMillis() / 900.0D) * 0.06D, goalZ);

      if (smoothedRiftPos == null || smoothedRiftPos.distanceToSqr(goalPos) > 100.0D) { smoothedRiftPos = goalPos; smoothedRiftYaw = camYaw; }
      else { smoothedRiftPos = smoothedRiftPos.lerp(goalPos, sineEase); smoothedRiftYaw += Mth.wrapDegrees(camYaw - smoothedRiftYaw) * (float) sineEase; }
    }

    Vec3 renderAnchor = ((smoothedRiftPos != null) ? smoothedRiftPos : EverbookSessionState.getRiftAnchorPos()).add(0.0D, smoothedRiftDropY, 0.0D);
    float renderYaw = (smoothedRiftPos != null) ? smoothedRiftYaw : EverbookSessionState.getRiftYaw();
    EverbookSessionState.syncLockedTransform(renderAnchor, renderYaw, 0.0f);

    PoseStack ps = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buf = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();
    Font font = mc.font;
    float timeSec = (float) ((System.nanoTime() - RENDER_EPOCH_NANOS) * 1.0e-9D);

    RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();
    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(renderAnchor.x - cam.x, renderAnchor.y - cam.y, renderAnchor.z - cam.z);
    ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(-renderYaw - 180.0f));

    PortalShaderHook.bind(CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_DEFAULT)
      .size(RIFT_HALF_W, RIFT_HALF_H)
      .resolution(64, 12)
      .render(ps, buf, timeSec);

    renderWrappedPatterns(ps, buf, font, timeSec);
    if (EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode()) { renderLeftEvaluationPanel(ps, buf, font, timeSec); renderRightFlatMiniGrid(ps, buf); }
    ps.popPose();

    if (EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode()) TruncatedIcosahedronCage.renderCage(ps, buf, cam, renderAnchor, TruncatedIcosahedronCage.CAGE_RADIUS, timeSec);
    buf.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    buf.endBatch(RenderType.lines());
    buf.endBatch();
    RenderSystem.enableDepthTest();
  }

  private static void renderWrappedPatterns(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Font font, float timeSeconds) {
    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> patternEntries = EverbookSessionState.getCurrentEntries();
    float gridStartX = -((COLS_PER_ROW - 1) * CELL_SIZE) * 0.5f;
    float scrollRowOffset = EverbookSessionState.getScrollRowOffset();
    VertexConsumer quadConsumer = bufferSource.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);

    int introspectionDepth = 0;
    for (int index = 0; patternEntries.size() > index; index++) {
      EverbookData.PatternEntry patternEntry = patternEntries.get(index);
      String anglesSignature = patternEntry.angles();

      // Retrospection ("eee") closes the current introspection scope on itself
      if ("eee".equals(anglesSignature) && introspectionDepth > 0) {
        introspectionDepth--;
      }

      float visibleRow = (index / COLS_PER_ROW) - scrollRowOffset;
      if (visibleRow >= -0.6f && 5.2f >= visibleRow) {
        float cellCenterX = gridStartX + ((index % COLS_PER_ROW) * CELL_SIZE);
        float cellCenterY = PATTERN_TOP_Y - (visibleRow * CELL_SIZE);
        boolean isSelected = EverbookSessionState.getSelectedIds().contains(patternEntry.id());
        boolean isHovered = index == EverbookSessionState.getHoveredPatternIdx();

        drawPatternStrokes(
          poseStack, quadConsumer, cellCenterX, cellCenterY, 0.008f, CELL_SIZE * 0.36f,
          anglesSignature, patternEntry.startDir(), isSelected, isHovered, introspectionDepth, timeSeconds
        );
      }

      // Introspection ("qqq") shifts all glyphs AFTER it to the next color tier (Cyan -> Deep Blue -> Purple)
      if ("qqq".equals(anglesSignature)) {
        introspectionDepth++;
      }
    }

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.96f, 0.01f);
    poseStack.scale(0.0085f, -0.0085f, 0.0085f);

    String modeLabel = EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode() ? "[RIFT LOCKED]" : "[SOUL CASTING]";
    String iotaCountLabel = "  " + patternEntries.size() + (patternEntries.size() == 1 ? " Iota" : " Iotas");
    float totalHeaderWidth = font.width(modeLabel + iotaCountLabel);

    ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(poseStack, bufferSource, font, modeLabel, -totalHeaderWidth * 0.5f, 0.0f, TextEffectStyle.spectrum());
    font.drawInBatch(
      Component.literal("§f" + iotaCountLabel),
      (-totalHeaderWidth * 0.5f) + font.width(modeLabel),
      0.0f,
      0xFFFFFFFF,
      false,
      poseStack.last().pose(),
      bufferSource,
      Font.DisplayMode.SEE_THROUGH,
      0,
      15728880
    );
    poseStack.popPose();
  }


  private static void renderLeftEvaluationPanel(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource, Font font, float timeSeconds) {
