    boolean inscribeHover = EverbookSessionState.isInscribeHovered();
    boolean evalHover = EverbookSessionState.isEvalHovered();
    float panelX = -2.42f;

    // 1. "Inscribe" Nebula Portal + Borderless Absolution (spectrum) Text
    PortalShaderHook.bind(inscribeHover ? CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_EMERALD : CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_DEFAULT)
      .at(panelX, 0.72f)
      .size(0.44f, 0.15f)
      .resolution(48, 8)
      .flushBatch()
      .render(poseStack, bufferSource, timeSeconds);

    TextEffectStyle rawSpectrum = TextEffectStyle.spectrum();
    TextEffectStyle borderlessAbsolution = new TextEffectStyle(
      rawSpectrum.colors(), rawSpectrum.stops(), rawSpectrum.scrollSpeed(),
      rawSpectrum.waveAmplitude(), rawSpectrum.waveFrequency(),
      TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.NONE,
      rawSpectrum.glowColor(), rawSpectrum.secondaryGlowColor(), true, true, false, 0.0f
    );

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(panelX, 0.75f, 0.045f);
    poseStack.scale(0.0085f, -0.0085f, 0.0085f);
    String inscribeLabel = "Inscribe";
    ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(poseStack, bufferSource, font, inscribeLabel, -font.width(inscribeLabel) * 0.5f, 0.0f, borderlessAbsolution);
    poseStack.popPose();

    // 2. "Evaluate" Nebula Portal + Borderless Starly Text
    PortalShaderHook.bind(evalHover ? CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_EMERALD : CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_DEFAULT)
      .at(panelX, 0.36f)
      .size(0.44f, 0.15f)
      .resolution(48, 8)
      .flushBatch()
      .render(poseStack, bufferSource, timeSeconds);

    TextEffectStyle baseStarly = TextEffectStyle.starly(evalHover ? 0xFFFFD700 : 0xFFD8DEE9);
    TextEffectStyle borderlessStarly = new TextEffectStyle(
      baseStarly.colors(), baseStarly.stops(), baseStarly.scrollSpeed(),
      baseStarly.waveAmplitude(), baseStarly.waveFrequency(),
      TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.NONE,
      baseStarly.glowColor(), baseStarly.secondaryGlowColor(), true
    );

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(panelX, 0.39f, 0.045f);
    poseStack.scale(0.0085f, -0.0085f, 0.0085f);
    String evalLabel = "Evaluate";
    ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(poseStack, bufferSource, font, evalLabel, -font.width(evalLabel) * 0.5f, 0.0f, borderlessStarly);
    poseStack.popPose();

    // 3. Evaluation Result Portal
    if (!EverbookSessionState.hasEvalResult()) return;
    boolean collapsed = EverbookSessionState.isEvalCollapsed();
    boolean success = EverbookSessionState.isEvalSuccess();

    ShaderDictionary resultDictionary = collapsed ? CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_CRIMSON
      : (success ? CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_EMERALD : CompositeDictionary.EVERBOOK_DEFAULT);

    PortalShaderHook.bind(resultDictionary)
      .at(panelX, -0.06f)
      .size(0.58f, 0.22f)
      .resolution(48, 8)
      .flushBatch()
      .render(poseStack, bufferSource, timeSeconds);

    drawCenteredText(poseStack, bufferSource, font, panelX, 0.05f, 0.0068f, (success ? "§aResult: §f" : "§cResult: §f") + EverbookSessionState.getEvalSummary());
    drawCenteredText(poseStack, bufferSource, font, panelX, -0.04f, 0.0068f, collapsed ? "§cToo many recursions, collapsed rift" : ("§7Recursions: §b" + EverbookSessionState.getEvalRecursions()));
  }

  private static void drawCenteredText(PoseStack ps, MultiBufferSource buf, Font font, float x, float y, float sc, String raw) {
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(x, y, 0.045f);
    ps.scale(sc, -sc, sc);
    Component c = Component.literal(raw);
    font.drawInBatch(c, -font.width(c) * 0.5f, 0.0f, 0xFFFFFFFF, false, ps.last().pose(), buf, Font.DisplayMode.SEE_THROUGH, 0, 15728880);
    ps.popPose();
  }

  private static void renderRightFlatMiniGrid(PoseStack ps, MultiBufferSource buf) {
    EverbookMiniGridConfig.ensureLoaded();
    int hov = EverbookSessionState.getHoveredMiniNode();
    List<Integer> path = EverbookSessionState.getMiniGridPath();
    for (int r = 0; MINI_ROWS > r; r++) {
      for (int c = 0; MINI_COLS > c; c++) {
        int id = (r * MINI_COLS) + c;
        Point2D p = getMiniNodePoint(r, c);
        int col = (id == hov) ? EverbookMiniGridConfig.pointHoverColor : (path.contains(id) ? EverbookMiniGridConfig.pointActiveColor : EverbookMiniGridConfig.pointColor);
        RawGeometry.drawArcaneNodePrism(ps, buf, p.x(), p.y(), 0.01f, (id == hov || path.contains(id)) ? 0.025f : 0.015f, col);
      }
    }
    if (2 > path.size()) return;
    VertexConsumer vc = buf.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    Matrix4f mat = ps.last().pose();
    float core = EverbookMiniGridConfig.lineSize, out = core * EverbookMiniGridConfig.outlineSizeMultiplier;
    for (int i = 0; path.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      Point2D pa = getMiniNodePoint(path.get(i) / MINI_COLS, path.get(i) % MINI_COLS), pb = getMiniNodePoint(path.get(i + 1) / MINI_COLS, path.get(i + 1) % MINI_COLS);
      drawMiniRibbon(vc, mat, pa.x(), pa.y(), pb.x(), pb.y(), 0.011f, out, EverbookMiniGridConfig.outlineColor);
      drawMiniRibbon(vc, mat, pa.x(), pa.y(), pb.x(), pb.y(), 0.013f, core, EverbookMiniGridConfig.lineColor);
    }
  }

  private static void drawMiniRibbon(VertexConsumer vc, Matrix4f mat, float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float z, float hw, int argb) {
    float dx = x1 - x0, dy = y1 - y0, len = (float) Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (1.0e-5f > len) return;
    float nx = (-dy / len) * hw, ny = (dx / len) * hw;
    RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x0 - nx, y0 - ny, z, argb); RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x1 - nx, y1 - ny, z, argb);
    RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x1 + nx, y1 + ny, z, argb); RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x0 + nx, y0 + ny, z, argb);
    RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x0 + nx, y0 + ny, z, argb); RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x1 + nx, y1 + ny, z, argb);
    RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x1 - nx, y1 - ny, z, argb); RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, x0 - nx, y0 - ny, z, argb);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookSandboxEvaluator.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.eval.CastingEnvironment;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.eval.vm.CastingImage;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.eval.vm.CastingVM;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.Iota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.iota.PatternIota;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir;
import at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexPattern;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public final class EverbookSandboxEvaluator {

  public record EvalResult(
    boolean success,
    boolean collapsedRift,
    int recursionCount,
    String summaryText,
    List<String> stackPreview
  ) {}

  // Patterns that mutate the physical world (intercepted in dry-run to return True/False instead of grieving/executing)
  private static final Set<String> WORLD_EVENT_SIGNATURES = Set.of(
    "qaqqqqq",   // Break Block
    "eeeeedw",   // Place Block
    "waaw",      // Ignite / Fire
    "waadw",     // Summon Lightning
    "aawaawaa",  // Explosion
    "awddw",     // Impulse
    "edqde",     // Create Water / Lava
    "wqaqw"      // Conjure Block / Light
  );

  private static final Set<String> RECURSIVE_SIGNATURES = Set.of(
    "deaqq",     // Hermes' Gambit
    "dadad",     // Thoth's Gambit
    "qwaeawqaeaqa" // Iris's Gambit
  );

  private EverbookSandboxEvaluator() {}

  public static EvalResult evaluateNonDestructive(ServerPlayer player, List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries) {
    if (entries == null || entries.isEmpty()) {
      return new EvalResult(false, false, 0, "Empty Rift", List.of());
    }

    int maxRecursions = 256;

    // 1. Pre-scan recursion depth & world-mutating patterns
    int estimatedRecursions = 0;
    boolean containsWorldEvent = false;
    List<Iota> iotas = new ArrayList<>();

    for (EverbookData.PatternEntry entry : entries) {
      String sig = entry.angles();
      if (RECURSIVE_SIGNATURES.contains(sig)) {
        estimatedRecursions++;
      }
      if (WORLD_EVENT_SIGNATURES.contains(sig)) {
        containsWorldEvent = true;
      }
      HexDir dir = parseDir(entry.startDir());
      HexPattern pat = buildPatternFromAngles(dir, sig);
      iotas.add(new PatternIota(pat));
    }

    // 2. If world-mutating actions are present, validate safely without mutating the world -> True / False
    if (containsWorldEvent) {
      if (estimatedRecursions > maxRecursions) {
        return new EvalResult(false, true, estimatedRecursions, "False (Too many recursions, collapsed rift)", List.of("False"));
      }
      boolean validArgs = entries.size() >= 2;
      return new EvalResult(
        validArgs,
        false,
        estimatedRecursions,
        validArgs ? "True (World Event Simulated)" : "False (Missing Target/Args)",
        List.of(validArgs ? "True" : "False")
      );
    }

    // 3. Pure stack evaluation via HexCasting's VM on a fresh isolated CastingImage (never consumes Everbook entries!)
    try {
      CastingEnvironment env = new EverbookDryRunEnv(player);
      CastingImage image = new CastingImage();
      CastingVM vm = new CastingVM(image, env);

      var outcome = vm.queueExecuteAndWrapIotas(iotas, player.serverLevel());
      int actualRecursions = Math.max(estimatedRecursions, countFrames(vm.getImage()));

      if (actualRecursions > maxRecursions || outcome.getResolutionType().name().contains("ERROR")) {
        boolean collapsed = actualRecursions > maxRecursions;
        String msg = collapsed ? "False (Too many recursions, collapsed rift)" : "False (Mishap)";
        return new EvalResult(false, collapsed, actualRecursions, msg, List.of("False"));
      }

      List<String> preview = new ArrayList<>();
      for (Iota iota : vm.getImage().getStack()) {
        preview.add(iota.display().getString());
      }
      String topStr = preview.isEmpty() ? "True" : preview.get(preview.size() - 1);
      return new EvalResult(true, false, actualRecursions, topStr, preview);
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      if (estimatedRecursions > maxRecursions) {
        return new EvalResult(false, true, estimatedRecursions, "False (Too many recursions, collapsed rift)", List.of("False"));
      }
      return new EvalResult(false, false, estimatedRecursions, "False", List.of("False"));
    }
  }

  private static int countFrames(CastingImage img) {
    try {
      return img.getParenCount();
    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  private static HexDir parseDir(String name) {
    try {
      return HexDir.valueOf(name);
    } catch (Exception e) {
      return HexDir.EAST;
    }
  }

  private static HexPattern buildPatternFromAngles(HexDir startDir, String angles) {
    try {
      Class<?> signatureClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexSignature");
      var factoryMethod = signatureClass.getMethod("fromAnglesStringUnchecked", String.class);
      Object hexSignature = factoryMethod.invoke(null, angles);
      var patternCtor = HexPattern.class.getConstructor(HexDir.class, signatureClass);
      patternCtor.setAccessible(true);
      return patternCtor.newInstance(startDir, hexSignature);
    } catch (Throwable t) {
      throw new RuntimeException("Failed to build HexPattern for angles: " + angles, t);
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookSessionState.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.HexagonalGridManager;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.custom.EverbookItem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class EverbookSessionState {

  private static boolean active = false;
  private static boolean riftLocked = false; // false = Soul/Casting mode (follows player), true = Static Rift Edit Mode
  private static boolean usingOffhand = false;

  private static EverbookData.BookState bookState = new EverbookData.BookState(0, new ArrayList<>(List.of(new EverbookData.BookPage("Chapter I", new ArrayList<>()))));
  private static final Set<String> SELECTED_IDS = new LinkedHashSet<>();
  private static final List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> CLIPBOARD = new ArrayList<>();

  // Rift world-space anchor
  private static Vec3 riftAnchorPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  private static float riftYaw = 0.0f;
  private static float riftPitch = 0.0f;

  // Scroll & interactive hit-testing state
  private static float scrollRowOffset = 0.0f;
  private static int totalWrappedRows = 1;
  private static int hoveredPatternIdx = -1;
  private static boolean draggingSelected = false;
  private static boolean sweepSelecting = false;
  private static boolean evalHovered = false;

  // Half-size flat mini-grid (right side of Rift in Rift Mode)
  private static final List<Integer> MINI_GRID_PATH = new ArrayList<>();
  private static int hoveredMiniNode = -1;

  // Left-side Dry-Run Evaluation Result
  private static boolean hasEvalResult = false;
  private static boolean evalSuccess = false;
  private static boolean evalCollapsed = false;
  private static int evalRecursions = 0;
  private static String evalSummary = "Ready";
  private static List<String> evalStackLines = List.of();

  private static Vec3 cageCenterPos = Vec3.ZERO;
  private static int riftOverreachTicks = 0;
  private static long lastRiftPenaltyTick = 0L;
  private static boolean sweepDeselecting = false;
  private static boolean inscribeHovered = false;

  public static boolean isInscribeHovered() { return inscribeHovered; }
  public static Vec3 getCageCenterPos() { return cageCenterPos; }

  private EverbookSessionState() {}

  public static boolean isActive() { return active; }
  public static boolean isRiftMode() { return active && riftLocked; }
  public static Vec3 getRiftAnchorPos() { return riftAnchorPos; }
  public static float getRiftYaw() { return riftYaw; }
  public static float getRiftPitch() { return riftPitch; }
  public static float getScrollRowOffset() { return scrollRowOffset; }
  public static int getHoveredPatternIdx() { return hoveredPatternIdx; }
  public static Set<String> getSelectedIds() { return SELECTED_IDS; }
  public static boolean isEvalHovered() { return evalHovered; }
  public static List<Integer> getMiniGridPath() { return MINI_GRID_PATH; }
  public static int getHoveredMiniNode() { return hoveredMiniNode; }
  public static boolean hasEvalResult() { return hasEvalResult; }
  public static boolean isEvalSuccess() { return evalSuccess; }
  public static boolean isEvalCollapsed() { return evalCollapsed; }
  public static int getEvalRecursions() { return evalRecursions; }
  public static String getEvalSummary() { return evalSummary; }
  public static List<String> getEvalStackLines() { return evalStackLines; }
  private static String draggedAnchorId = null;

  public static List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> getCurrentEntries() {
    return bookState.currentPage().entries();
  }

  public static void activateForPlayer(Player player, ItemStack stack, InteractionHand hand) {
    active = true;
    riftLocked = false;
    usingOffhand = (hand == InteractionHand.OFF_HAND);
    bookState = EverbookData.read(stack);
    SELECTED_IDS.clear();
    MINI_GRID_PATH.clear();
    scrollRowOffset = 0.0f;
    hasEvalResult = false;
    updateRiftFollowCamera();
  }

  public static void deactivate() {
    if (active) {
      syncToServer("SAVE");
    }
    active = false;
    riftLocked = false;
    SELECTED_IDS.clear();
    MINI_GRID_PATH.clear();
    draggingSelected = false;
    sweepSelecting = false;
  }

  public static void appendDrawnPattern(String angles, String startDir, String label) {
    if (!active) return;
    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = getCurrentEntries();
    entries.add(new EverbookData.PatternEntry(
      UUID.randomUUID().toString(), angles, startDir, label, 0.0f, 0.0f, entries.size()
    ));
    // Auto-scroll to bottom when adding new patterns
    scrollRowOffset = Math.max(0.0f, totalWrappedRows - 4.0f);
    syncToServer("SAVE");
  }

  public static void triggerEvaluate() {
    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> all = getCurrentEntries();
    if (all.isEmpty()) return;

    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> toEval = new ArrayList<>();
    if (!SELECTED_IDS.isEmpty()) {
      for (EverbookData.PatternEntry e : all) {
        if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(e.id())) toEval.add(e);
      }
    } else {
      toEval.addAll(all);
    }
    PacketDistributor.sendToServer(EverbookEvalPayload.request(toEval));
  }

  public static void onEvaluationResult(boolean success, boolean collapsed, int recursions, String summary, List<String> stack) {
    hasEvalResult = true;
    evalSuccess = success;
    evalCollapsed = collapsed;
    evalRecursions = recursions;
    evalSummary = summary;
    evalStackLines = stack;
  }

  private static void syncToServer(String action) {
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player != null) {
      ItemStack held = mc.player.getItemInHand(usingOffhand ? InteractionHand.OFF_HAND : InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
      if (held.getItem() instanceof EverbookItem) {
        EverbookData.write(held, bookState);
      }
      PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new EverbookSyncPayload(usingOffhand, action, bookState.toNbt()));
    }
  }

  private static void updateRiftFollowCamera() {
    Vec3 camPos = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPos();
    float yaw = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamYaw();
    float pitch = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPitch();
    Vec3 look = Vec3.directionFromRotation(pitch, yaw);
    Vec3 right = look.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0)).normalize();
    // Follows player smoothly on the left-forward side like the 3D stack
    riftAnchorPos = camPos.add(look.scale(2.15D)).add(right.scale(-0.85D));
    riftYaw = yaw + 18.0f;
    riftPitch = pitch * 0.45f;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Pre event) {
    if (!HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) {
      if (active) deactivate();
      return;
    }
    if (!active) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;
    if (!riftLocked) return;

    Vec3 eye = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPos();

    // 3D Truncated Icosahedron Containment Penalty (restricts X, Y, and Z movement)
    if (TruncatedIcosahedronCage.isOutsideCage(eye, riftAnchorPos, TruncatedIcosahedronCage.CAGE_RADIUS)) {
      riftOverreachTicks++;
      long gameTime = mc.level.getGameTime();
      if (gameTime - lastRiftPenaltyTick >= 20L) {
        lastRiftPenaltyTick = gameTime;
        String penaltyCmd = (riftOverreachTicks >= 200)
        ? "hexacreatecore:overreach_penalty;wither"
        : "hexacreatecore:overreach_penalty;normal";
        PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
          new com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.DialogueActionPayload(mc.player.getId(), penaltyCmd, false)
        );
      }
    } else {
      riftOverreachTicks = 0;
    }

    Vec3 look = Vec3.directionFromRotation(HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPitch(), HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamYaw());
    EverbookRiftMath.HitResult hit = EverbookRiftMath.raycastRiftWorkspace(eye, look, riftAnchorPos, riftYaw, riftPitch, getCurrentEntries(), scrollRowOffset);

    hoveredPatternIdx = hit.patternIndex();
    evalHovered = hit.evalButtonHovered();
    hoveredMiniNode = hit.miniGridNodeIndex();
    totalWrappedRows = Math.max(1, hit.totalRows());
    inscribeHovered = hit.inscribeButtonHovered();

    boolean sprintHeld = mc.options.keySprint.isDown();
    long windowHandle = mc.getWindow().getWindow();
    boolean rightHeld = GLFW.glfwGetMouseButton(windowHandle, GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
    boolean leftHeld = GLFW.glfwGetMouseButton(windowHandle, GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;

    if (sweepDeselecting && !sprintHeld && leftHeld && hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && getCurrentEntries().size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
      SELECTED_IDS.remove(getCurrentEntries().get(hoveredPatternIdx).id());
    }
    if (!leftHeld) {
      sweepDeselecting = false;
    }

    if (sweepSelecting && sprintHeld && rightHeld && hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && getCurrentEntries().size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
      SELECTED_IDS.add(getCurrentEntries().get(hoveredPatternIdx).id());
    }
    if (!rightHeld) {
      sweepSelecting = false;
      if (draggingSelected) {
        draggingSelected = false;
        syncToServer("SAVE");
      }
      if (!MINI_GRID_PATH.isEmpty()) {
        if (MINI_GRID_PATH.size() >= 2) {
          EverbookRiftMath.compileMiniGridStroke(MINI_GRID_PATH);
        }
        MINI_GRID_PATH.clear();
      }
    }

    if (draggingSelected && rightHeld) {
      if (hit.autoScrollDir() != 0) {
        float maxScroll = Math.max(0.0f, totalWrappedRows - 4.0f);
        scrollRowOffset = Mth.clamp(scrollRowOffset + (hit.autoScrollDir() * 0.14f), 0.0f, maxScroll);
      }
      if (hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && getCurrentEntries().size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
        shiftSelectedBlockTo(hoveredPatternIdx);
      }
    }

    if (!sprintHeld && !draggingSelected && rightHeld && hoveredMiniNode >= 0) {
      int sz = MINI_GRID_PATH.size();
      if (sz >= 2 && MINI_GRID_PATH.get(sz - 2) == hoveredMiniNode) {
        MINI_GRID_PATH.remove(sz - 1);
      } else if (MINI_GRID_PATH.isEmpty() || MINI_GRID_PATH.get(sz - 1) != hoveredMiniNode) {
        MINI_GRID_PATH.add(hoveredMiniNode);
        mc.level.playSound(mc.player, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(),
          SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_STEP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6f, 1.1f + (MINI_GRID_PATH.size() * 0.05f));
      }
    }
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onMouseButton(InputEvent.MouseButton.Pre event) {
    if (!active || !HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.screen != null || mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;

    boolean sprintHeld = mc.options.keySprint.isDown();

    if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS && sprintHeld) {
      riftLocked = !riftLocked;
      draggingSelected = false;
      sweepSelecting = false;
      MINI_GRID_PATH.clear();
      riftOverreachTicks = 0;
      if (riftLocked) {
        cageCenterPos = HexagonalGridManager.getFreeCamPos();
      }
      mc.level.playSound(mc.player, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(),
        SoundEvents.ENDER_EYE_DEATH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, riftLocked ? 1.25f : 0.85f);
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    if (!riftLocked) return;

    if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS && inscribeHovered) {
      syncToServer("INSCRIBE_OFFHAND");
      mc.level.playSound(
        mc.player, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(),
        SoundEvents.ENCHANTMENT_TABLE_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.85f, 1.2f
      );
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS && evalHovered) {
      triggerEvaluate();
      mc.level.playSound(mc.player, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(),
        SoundEvents.BEACON_POWER_SELECT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 1.35f);
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS && !sprintHeld) {
      List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = getCurrentEntries();
      if (hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && entries.size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
        String clickedId = entries.get(hoveredPatternIdx).id();
        SELECTED_IDS.remove(clickedId);
        sweepDeselecting = true;
      } else {
        SELECTED_IDS.clear();
        sweepDeselecting = true;
      }
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    if (event.getButton() == GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS) {
      List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = getCurrentEntries();
      if (sprintHeld) {
        // Start sweep multi-select even if the swipe begins in empty space between patterns!
        sweepSelecting = true;
        if (hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && entries.size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
          String clickedId = entries.get(hoveredPatternIdx).id();
          if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(clickedId)) {
            SELECTED_IDS.remove(clickedId);
          } else {
            SELECTED_IDS.add(clickedId);
          }
        } else {
          SELECTED_IDS.clear();
        }
        event.setCanceled(true);
      } else if (hoveredPatternIdx >= 0 && entries.size() > hoveredPatternIdx) {
        String clickedId = entries.get(hoveredPatternIdx).id();
        if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(clickedId)) {
          draggingSelected = true;
          draggedAnchorId = clickedId;
          event.setCanceled(true);
        }
      }
    }
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onKeyInput(InputEvent.Key event) {
    if (!active || !riftLocked || event.getAction() != InputConstants.PRESS) return;
    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.screen != null) return;

    int key = event.getKey();
    boolean ctrl = (event.getModifiers() & GLFW.GLFW_MOD_CONTROL) != 0;

    // Evaluate non-destructively (R or Enter)
    if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_R || key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ENTER) {
      triggerEvaluate();
    }
    // Batch Delete via configurable KeyMapping (never touches Backspace!)
    else if (EverbookKeybinds.DELETE_IOTA_KEY.matches(event.getKey(), event.getScanCode()) && !SELECTED_IDS.isEmpty()) {
      getCurrentEntries().removeIf(e -> SELECTED_IDS.contains(e.id()));
      SELECTED_IDS.clear();
      syncToServer("SAVE");
    }
    // Batch Copy (Ctrl+C)
    else if (ctrl && key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_C && !SELECTED_IDS.isEmpty()) {
      CLIPBOARD.clear();
      for (EverbookData.PatternEntry e : getCurrentEntries()) {
        if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(e.id())) CLIPBOARD.add(e);
      }
    }
    // Batch Paste (Ctrl+V)
    else if (ctrl && key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_V && !CLIPBOARD.isEmpty()) {
      List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = getCurrentEntries();
      SELECTED_IDS.clear();
      for (EverbookData.PatternEntry c : CLIPBOARD) {
        EverbookData.PatternEntry copy = c.duplicateWithOffset(0, 0, entries.size());
        entries.add(copy);
        SELECTED_IDS.add(copy.id());
      }
      syncToServer("SAVE");
    }
  }


  public static void handleRiftScroll(float delta) {
    float maxScroll = Math.max(0.0f, totalWrappedRows - 4.0f);
    scrollRowOffset = Mth.clamp(scrollRowOffset - (delta * 0.65f), 0.0f, maxScroll);
  }

  public static void syncLockedTransform(Vec3 pos, float yaw, float pitch) {
    riftAnchorPos = pos;
    riftYaw = yaw;
    riftPitch = pitch;
  }

  private static void shiftSelectedBlockTo(int targetIdx) {
    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> entries = getCurrentEntries();
    if (SELECTED_IDS.isEmpty() || 0 > targetIdx || targetIdx >= entries.size()) return;
    if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(entries.get(targetIdx).id())) return;

    int anchorIdx = -1;
    for (int i = 0; entries.size() > i; i++) {
      String id = entries.get(i).id();
      if (id.equals(draggedAnchorId) || (anchorIdx == -1 && SELECTED_IDS.contains(id))) {
        anchorIdx = i;
        if (id.equals(draggedAnchorId)) break;
      }
    }
    boolean movingRight = targetIdx > anchorIdx;

    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> selected = new ArrayList<>();
    List<EverbookData.PatternEntry> remaining = new ArrayList<>();
    int insertAt = 0;

    for (int i = 0; entries.size() > i; i++) {
      EverbookData.PatternEntry entry = entries.get(i);
      if (SELECTED_IDS.contains(entry.id())) {
        selected.add(entry);
      } else {
        if (movingRight ? (targetIdx >= i) : (targetIdx > i)) {
          insertAt++;
        }
        remaining.add(entry);
      }
    }
    insertAt = Mth.clamp(insertAt, 0, remaining.size());
    remaining.addAll(insertAt, selected);
    entries.clear();
    entries.addAll(remaining);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/EverbookSyncPayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.custom.EverbookItem;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record EverbookSyncPayload(
  boolean isOffhand,
  String action,
  CompoundTag bookNbt
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

  public static final Type<EverbookSyncPayload> TYPE = new Type<>(
    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(HexaCreateCore.MODID, "everbook_sync")
  );

  public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, EverbookSyncPayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.composite(
    ByteBufCodecs.BOOL, EverbookSyncPayload::isOffhand,
    ByteBufCodecs.STRING_UTF8, EverbookSyncPayload::action,
    ByteBufCodecs.COMPOUND_TAG, EverbookSyncPayload::bookNbt,
    EverbookSyncPayload::new
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
    return TYPE;
  }

  public static void handleServer(EverbookSyncPayload payload, IPayloadContext ctx) {
    ctx.enqueueWork(() -> {
      if (!(ctx.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;
      InteractionHand hand = payload.isOffhand() ? InteractionHand.OFF_HAND : InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND;
      ItemStack bookStack = sp.getItemInHand(hand);
      if (!(bookStack.getItem() instanceof EverbookItem)) return;

      EverbookData.BookState state = EverbookData.BookState.fromNbt(payload.bookNbt());
      EverbookData.write(bookStack, state);
      if ("INSCRIBE_OFFHAND".equals(payload.action())) {
        InteractionHand targetHand = (hand == InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND) ? InteractionHand.OFF_HAND : InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND;
        EverbookHexWriter.inscribeToHexItem(sp, sp.getItemInHand(targetHand), state.currentPage().entries());
      }
    });
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/everbook/TruncatedIcosahedronCage.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class TruncatedIcosahedronCage {

  public static final float CAGE_RADIUS = 5.25f;

  private static final List<Vec3> VERTICES = new ArrayList<>(60);
  private static final List<int[]> EDGES = new ArrayList<>(90);
  private static final List<Vec3> PENTAGON_NORMALS = new ArrayList<>(12);
  private static final List<Vec3> HEXAGON_NORMALS = new ArrayList<>(20);

  private static final List<List<Vec3>> FACES = new ArrayList<>(32);
  private record FacePlane(Vec3 normal, double unitInradius) {}
  private static final List<FacePlane> FACE_PLANES = new ArrayList<>(32);
  private static final double PLANE_EPSILON = 1.0e-3D;

  private static final double PENTAGON_INRADIUS_RATIO = 0.93417235896D;
  private static final double HEXAGON_INRADIUS_RATIO = 0.98224694688D;


  static {
    double phi = (1.0D + Math.sqrt(5.0D)) * 0.5D;
    double norm = Math.sqrt(10.0D + 9.0D * phi);

    // 60 vertices: even permutations of (0, +-1, +-3phi), (+-1, +-(2+phi), +-2phi), (+-phi, +-2, +-(2phi+1))
    addEvenPermutations(0.0D, 1.0D, 3.0D * phi, norm);
    addEvenPermutations(1.0D, 2.0D + phi, 2.0D * phi, norm);
    addEvenPermutations(phi, 2.0D, 2.0D * phi + 1.0D, norm);

    // 90 edges: pairs separated by normalized edge length (~0.40355)
    for (int i = 0; VERTICES.size() > i; i++) {
      for (int j = i + 1; VERTICES.size() > j; j++) {
        double dist = VERTICES.get(i).distanceTo(VERTICES.get(j));
        if (dist > 0.38D && 0.43D > dist) {
          EDGES.add(new int[]{ i, j });
        }
      }
    }

    // 12 Pentagon face normals (icosahedron vertices)
    double icoNorm = Math.sqrt(1.0D + phi * phi);
    addNormalPermutations(PENTAGON_NORMALS, 0.0D, 1.0D, phi, icoNorm);

    // 20 Hexagon face normals (dodecahedron vertices)
    double dodNorm = Math.sqrt(3.0D);
    for (int sx : new int[]{ -1, 1 }) {
      for (int sy : new int[]{ -1, 1 }) {
        for (int sz : new int[]{ -1, 1 }) {
          HEXAGON_NORMALS.add(new Vec3(sx / dodNorm, sy / dodNorm, sz / dodNorm));
        }
      }
    }
    addNormalPermutations(HEXAGON_NORMALS, 0.0D, phi, 1.0D / phi, dodNorm);
    buildOrderedFaces(PENTAGON_NORMALS);
    buildOrderedFaces(HEXAGON_NORMALS);
  }

  private TruncatedIcosahedronCage() {}

  private static void addEvenPermutations(double a, double b, double c, double norm) {
    for (int sa : (a == 0.0D ? new int[]{ 1 } : new int[]{ -1, 1 })) {
      for (int sb : (b == 0.0D ? new int[]{ 1 } : new int[]{ -1, 1 })) {
        for (int sc : (c == 0.0D ? new int[]{ 1 } : new int[]{ -1, 1 })) {
          double x = (a * sa) / norm, y = (b * sb) / norm, z = (c * sc) / norm;
          VERTICES.add(new Vec3(x, y, z));
          VERTICES.add(new Vec3(y, z, x));
          VERTICES.add(new Vec3(z, x, y));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static void addNormalPermutations(List<Vec3> target, double a, double b, double c, double norm) {
    for (int sb : new int[]{ -1, 1 }) {
      for (int sc : new int[]{ -1, 1 }) {
        double y = (b * sb) / norm, z = (c * sc) / norm;
        target.add(new Vec3(0.0D, y, z));
        target.add(new Vec3(y, z, 0.0D));
        target.add(new Vec3(z, 0.0D, y));
      }
    }
  }

  public static boolean isOutsideCage(Vec3 playerEyePos, Vec3 cageCenter, float radius) {
    Vec3 rel = playerEyePos.subtract(cageCenter);
    for (FacePlane plane : FACE_PLANES) {
      if (rel.dot(plane.normal()) > radius * plane.unitInradius()) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  public static void renderCage(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Vec3 camPos, Vec3 cageCenter, float radius, float timeSeconds) {
    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(cageCenter.x - camPos.x, cageCenter.y - camPos.y, cageCenter.z - camPos.z);

    // 1. Cosmic Ocean Nebula shader across all 32 polyhedron faces (12 pentagons + 20 hexagons, no aurora rim)
    com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks.PolyhedronFaceShaderHook
      .bind(com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.CosmicOceanDictionary.DEFAULT)
      .opacity(0.38f)
      .rings(3)
      .renderFaces(poseStack, bufferSource, FACES, radius, timeSeconds);

    // 2. Dark-nebula vertex nodes at the 60 corners
    for (int i = 0; VERTICES.size() > i; i++) {
      Vec3 v = VERTICES.get(i);
      float pulse = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((timeSeconds * 2.4f) + (i * 0.35f));
      int nodeCol = (pulse > 0.5f) ? 0xCC7E22CE : 0xCC10B981;
      RawGeometry.drawArcaneNodePrism(
        poseStack, bufferSource,
        (float) (v.x * radius), (float) (v.y * radius), (float) (v.z * radius),
        0.045f, nodeCol
      );
    }

    // 3. Wireframe edges of the 12 pentagons & 20 hexagons
    VertexConsumer lineConsumer = bufferSource.getBuffer(RenderType.lines());
    Matrix4f mat = poseStack.last().pose();
    float wave = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(timeSeconds * 2.0f);
    int r = Mth.lerpInt(wave, 88, 16);
    int g = Mth.lerpInt(wave, 28, 185);
    int b = Mth.lerpInt(wave, 135, 129);

    for (int[] edge : EDGES) {
      Vec3 p0 = VERTICES.get(edge[0]);
      Vec3 p1 = VERTICES.get(edge[1]);
      lineConsumer.addVertex(mat, (float) (p0.x * radius), (float) (p0.y * radius), (float) (p0.z * radius))
        .setColor(r, g, b, 195).setNormal(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
      lineConsumer.addVertex(mat, (float) (p1.x * radius), (float) (p1.y * radius), (float) (p1.z * radius))
        .setColor(r, g, b, 195).setNormal(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    poseStack.popPose();
  }

  private static void buildOrderedFaces(List<Vec3> normals) {
    for (Vec3 normal : normals) {
      double maxDot = -Double.MAX_VALUE;
      for (Vec3 v : VERTICES) {
        double d = v.dot(normal);
        if (d > maxDot) maxDot = d;
      }

      List<Vec3> faceVerts = new ArrayList<>(6);
      for (Vec3 v : VERTICES) {
        if (Math.abs(v.dot(normal) - maxDot) < PLANE_EPSILON) {
          faceVerts.add(v);
        }
      }
      if (faceVerts.size() < 3) continue;

      Vec3 center = Vec3.ZERO;
      for (Vec3 v : faceVerts) center = center.add(v);
      final Vec3 faceCenter = center.scale(1.0D / faceVerts.size());

      Vec3 tangentU = faceVerts.get(0).subtract(faceCenter).normalize();
      Vec3 tangentV = normal.cross(tangentU).normalize();

      faceVerts.sort((a, b) -> {
        Vec3 da = a.subtract(faceCenter);
        Vec3 db = b.subtract(faceCenter);
        double angA = Math.atan2(da.dot(tangentV), da.dot(tangentU));
        double angB = Math.atan2(db.dot(tangentV), db.dot(tangentU));
        return Double.compare(angA, angB);
      });

      FACE_PLANES.add(new FacePlane(normal, maxDot));
      FACES.add(List.copyOf(faceVerts));
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/HexagonalGridManager.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "hexacreatecore", value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class HexagonalGridManager {
  private static final List<ArcaneNode3D> ACTIVE_GRID_NODES = new ArrayList<>();
  private static final List<Integer> ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH = new ArrayList<>();
  private static final List<List<Integer>> PERSISTENT_SPELL_LINES = new ArrayList<>();
  private static final List<String> STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS = new ArrayList<>();

  private static Vec3 centerAnchorPoint = null;
  private static boolean isGridActive = false;
  private static String ravensmindString = null;
  private static int stackScrollIndex = 0;

  private static double camX = 0.0D;
  private static double camY = 0.0D;
  private static double camZ = 0.0D;
  private static double prevCamX = 0.0D;
  private static double prevCamY = 0.0D;
  private static double prevCamZ = 0.0D;

  private static float camYaw = 0.0f;
  private static float camPitch = 0.0f;
  private static float prevCamYaw = 0.0f;
  private static float prevCamPitch = 0.0f;

  private static final double FLY_SPEED_MOD = 0.14D;
  private static final double HEX_RADIUS = 2.0D;
  private static final double SELECTION_RADIUS_SQ = 0.09D; 

  private static int pointColorRaw = 0xFF21B7D1;
  private static int pointHoverColorRaw = 0xFFFFFF66;
  private static int lineColorRaw = 0xFFB233FF;
  private static int outlineColorRaw = 0xFF000000;
  private static float configLineSize = 0.0225f;
  private static float configOutlineSizeMultiplier = 2.0f;
  private static double configWobbleIntensity = 0.015D;

  private static int activeShellLayers = 1;
  private static long lastPenaltyTick = 0L;
  private static int penaltyZoneTicks = 0;

  public static Vec3 getFreeCamPos() { return new Vec3(camX, camY, camZ); }
  public static float getFreeCamYaw() { return camYaw; }
  public static float getFreeCamPitch() { return camPitch; }
  public static int getPointColorRaw() { return pointColorRaw; }

  public static int getShellIndex(int nodeId) {
    return (int) Math.floor((-1.0D + Math.sqrt(1.0D + (nodeId / 24.0D))) / 2.0D);
  }

  @SuppressWarnings({"rawtypes", "unchecked"})
  public static void activateCastingGrid(Player player) {
    loadExternalGridConfig();

    ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.clear();
    ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.clear();
    PERSISTENT_SPELL_LINES.clear();
    STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.clear();

    centerAnchorPoint = player.position(); 
    ravensmindString = null;
    stackScrollIndex = 0;
    isGridActive = true;

    camX = player.getX(); camY = player.getEyeY(); camZ = player.getZ();
    prevCamX = camX; prevCamY = camY; prevCamZ = camZ;

    camYaw = player.getYRot(); camPitch = player.getXRot();
    prevCamYaw = camYaw; prevCamPitch = camPitch;

    double gridZoomAttributeValue = 1.0D;
    net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation attributeId = net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("hexcasting", "grid_zoom");
    java.util.Optional optionalHolder = net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.ATTRIBUTE.getHolder(attributeId);

    if (optionalHolder != null && optionalHolder.isPresent()) {
      net.minecraft.core.Holder zoomAttributeHolder = (net.minecraft.core.Holder) optionalHolder.get();
      if (player.getAttributes().hasAttribute(zoomAttributeHolder)) {
        gridZoomAttributeValue = player.getAttributeValue(zoomAttributeHolder);
      }
    }

    int targetShellLayersCount = (int) Math.ceil(gridZoomAttributeValue);
    if (gridZoomAttributeValue > 1.0D && targetShellLayersCount == 1) targetShellLayersCount = 2;
    if (targetShellLayersCount < 1) targetShellLayersCount = 1;
    activeShellLayers = targetShellLayersCount;

    int nodeId = 0;
    double[] yOffsets = {-0.5D, 0.0D, 0.5D, 1.0D, 1.5D, 2.0D, 2.5D, 3.0D};

    for (int shellIndex = 0; targetShellLayersCount > shellIndex; shellIndex++) {
      double currentShellRadius = HEX_RADIUS * (shellIndex + 1);
      int pointsPerRing = 24 * (shellIndex + 1); 

      for (int rowIndex = 0; yOffsets.length > rowIndex; rowIndex++) {
        double nodeY = centerAnchorPoint.y + yOffsets[rowIndex] + 1.0D;
        double shiftParity = rowIndex % 2;
        double indexOffset = (shiftParity == 1) ? 0.5D : 0.0D;

        for (int i = 0; pointsPerRing > i; i++) {
          double currentStepIndex = i + indexOffset;
          double radiansAngle = (currentStepIndex * 2.0D * Math.PI) / pointsPerRing;

          Vec3 nodePosition = new Vec3(
            centerAnchorPoint.x + (currentShellRadius * Math.cos(radiansAngle)),
            nodeY,
            centerAnchorPoint.z + (currentShellRadius * Math.sin(radiansAngle))
          );

          int structuralColor = (shellIndex % 2 == 0) ? pointColorRaw : 0xFF9433FF;
          ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.add(new ArcaneNode3D(nodeId++, nodePosition, false, structuralColor));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  public static void deactivateGrid() {
    isGridActive = false;
    ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.clear(); ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.clear(); PERSISTENT_SPELL_LINES.clear(); STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.clear();
    centerAnchorPoint = null; ravensmindString = null; stackScrollIndex = 0;
  }

  public static void handleFreeCamMovement(net.minecraft.client.player.Input input) {
    prevCamX = camX; prevCamY = camY; prevCamZ = camZ;
    prevCamYaw = camYaw; prevCamPitch = camPitch;

    float yawRad = (float) Math.toRadians(camYaw);
    Vec3 forwardVec = new Vec3(-Math.sin(yawRad), 0.0D, Math.cos(yawRad)).normalize();
    Vec3 rightVec = new Vec3(Math.cos(yawRad), 0.0D, Math.sin(yawRad)).normalize();

    Vec3 moveVector = Vec3.ZERO;
    if (input.up) moveVector = moveVector.add(forwardVec);
    if (input.down) moveVector = moveVector.subtract(forwardVec);
    if (input.left) moveVector = moveVector.add(rightVec);
    if (input.right) moveVector = moveVector.subtract(rightVec);

    if (input.jumping) moveVector = moveVector.add(0.0D, 1.0D, 0.0D);
    if (input.shiftKeyDown) moveVector = moveVector.subtract(0.0D, 1.0D, 0.0D);

    if (moveVector.lengthSqr() > 0.001D) {
      moveVector = moveVector.normalize().scale(FLY_SPEED_MOD);
      camX += moveVector.x; camY += moveVector.y; camZ += moveVector.z;
    }

    if (centerAnchorPoint != null) {
      // Horizontal cylinder clamp: 3 blocks for 1 ring, 5 blocks for 2 rings, 7 blocks for 3 rings, etc.
      double maxHorizontalRadius = (HEX_RADIUS * activeShellLayers) + 1.0D;
      double dx = camX - centerAnchorPoint.x;
      double dz = camZ - centerAnchorPoint.z;
      double horizDist = Math.sqrt((dx * dx) + (dz * dz));

      if (horizDist > maxHorizontalRadius) {
        double scale = maxHorizontalRadius / horizDist;
        camX = centerAnchorPoint.x + (dx * scale);
        camZ = centerAnchorPoint.z + (dz * scale);
      }

      double minY = centerAnchorPoint.y + 0.5D;
      double maxY = centerAnchorPoint.y + 5.0D;
      camY = net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(camY, minY, maxY);
    }
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Pre event) {
    if (!isGridActive) return;
    if (com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode()) {
      ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.clear();
      return;
    }

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;

    if (mc.screen instanceof net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.PauseScreen) {
      mc.setScreen(null);
      return;
    }

    Vec3 eyePos = getFreeCamPos();
    float f = camPitch * ((float)Math.PI / 180F);
    float f1 = -camYaw * ((float)Math.PI / 180F);
    float f2 = net.minecraft.util.Mth.cos(f1); float f3 = net.minecraft.util.Mth.sin(f1);
    float f4 = net.minecraft.util.Mth.cos(f); float f5 = net.minecraft.util.Mth.sin(f);
    Vec3 lookVec = new Vec3((double)(f3 * f4), (double)(-f5), (double)(f2 * f4)).normalize();

    double cameraDistanceToCenter = 0.0D;
    if (centerAnchorPoint != null) {
      double deltaX = eyePos.x - centerAnchorPoint.x;
      double deltaZ = eyePos.z - centerAnchorPoint.z;
      cameraDistanceToCenter = Math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaZ * deltaZ);
    }
    int currentActiveZoneShell = (int) (cameraDistanceToCenter / HEX_RADIUS);

    double outerRingRadius = HEX_RADIUS * activeShellLayers;
    if (cameraDistanceToCenter > outerRingRadius) {
      penaltyZoneTicks++;
      long gameTime = mc.level.getGameTime();
      if (gameTime - lastPenaltyTick >= 20L) {
        lastPenaltyTick = gameTime;
        String actionPayload = (penaltyZoneTicks >= 200)
          ? "hexacreatecore:overreach_penalty;wither"
          : "hexacreatecore:overreach_penalty;normal";

        net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
          new com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.DialogueActionPayload(mc.player.getId(), actionPayload, false)
        );
      }
    } else {
      penaltyZoneTicks = 0;
    }


    int highlightedNodeId = -1;
    double closestDistSq = Double.MAX_VALUE;

    for (int i = 0; ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.size() > i; i++) {
      ArcaneNode3D node = ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.get(i);
      int nodeShellIndex = getShellIndex(node.id());
      if (nodeShellIndex != currentActiveZoneShell) continue;

      Vec3 targetVec = node.worldPos().subtract(eyePos);
      double projection = targetVec.dot(lookVec);
      if (0.0D > projection) continue; 

      Vec3 closestPointOnGazeLine = eyePos.add(lookVec.scale(projection));
      double distSq = node.worldPos().distanceToSqr(closestPointOnGazeLine);

      if (SELECTION_RADIUS_SQ > distSq && distSq < closestDistSq) {
        closestDistSq = distSq; highlightedNodeId = i;
      }
    }

    boolean isRightClickHeld = mc.options.keyUse.isDown();

    for (int i = 0; ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.size() > i; i++) {
      ArcaneNode3D node = ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.get(i);
      boolean isCurrentlyHovered = (i == highlightedNodeId);

      int nodeShellIndex = getShellIndex(i);
      int baseShellColor = (nodeShellIndex % 2 == 0) ? pointColorRaw : 0xFF9433FF;
      int targetColor = isCurrentlyHovered ? pointHoverColorRaw : 
    (nodeShellIndex == currentActiveZoneShell ? baseShellColor : (baseShellColor & 0x44FFFFFF));

      if (isCurrentlyHovered && isRightClickHeld) {
        int pathSize = ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.size();
        if (pathSize >= 2 && ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.get(pathSize - 2) == i) {
          ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.remove(pathSize - 1);
          mc.level.playSound(mc.player, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(), 
            net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents.UI_BUTTON_CLICK.value(), net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.45f, 0.65f);
        } else if (ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.isEmpty() || ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.get(pathSize - 1) != i) {
          ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.add(i);
          try {
            net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation soundId = net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("hexcasting", "casting.pattern.add_segment");
            net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent authenticSound = mc.level.registryAccess().registryOrThrow(net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries.SOUND_EVENT).get(soundId);
            float risingPitchMultiplier = 0.8f + Math.min(0.6f, (float) ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.size() * 0.05f);
            if (authenticSound != null) {
              mc.level.playSound(mc.player, node.worldPos().x, node.worldPos().y, node.worldPos().z, authenticSound, net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.75f, risingPitchMultiplier);
            }
          } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
      }
      ACTIVE_GRID_NODES.set(i, new ArcaneNode3D(node.id(), node.worldPos(), false, targetColor));
    }

    if (!isRightClickHeld && !ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.isEmpty()) {
      if (ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.size() >= 2) {
        ArrayList<Integer> pathSnapshot = new ArrayList<>(ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH);
        PERSISTENT_SPELL_LINES.add(pathSnapshot);
        compileAndCast3DPattern(mc.player, pathSnapshot);
      }
      ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH.clear();
    }
  }

  public static void updateLiveCastingStack(List<String> incomingStack, String incomingRavensmind) {
    STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.clear();
    if (incomingStack != null) {
      STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.addAll(incomingStack);
    }
    ravensmindString = (incomingRavensmind == null || incomingRavensmind.isEmpty()) ? null : incomingRavensmind;
  }

  public static void compileAndCast3DPattern(Player player, List<Integer> explicitPath) {
    if (explicitPath == null || explicitPath.size() < 2) return;
    java.util.List<at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir> directions = new java.util.ArrayList<>();

    for (int i = 0; explicitPath.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      int startId = explicitPath.get(i); 
      int endId = explicitPath.get(i + 1);

      int startShell = getShellIndex(startId);
      int endShell = getShellIndex(endId);

      int startWidth = 24 * (startShell + 1);
      int endWidth = 24 * (endShell + 1);

      int baseStartId = startId - (96 * startShell * (startShell + 1));
      int baseEndId = endId - (96 * endShell * (endShell + 1));

      int startCol = baseStartId % startWidth; 
      int startRow = baseStartId / startWidth;
      int endCol = baseEndId % endWidth; 
      int endRow = baseEndId / endWidth;

      int maxPerimeter = Math.max(startWidth, endWidth);
      if (startWidth != endWidth) {
        startCol = (int) (startCol * ((double) maxPerimeter / startWidth));
        endCol = (int) (endCol * ((double) maxPerimeter / endWidth));
      }

      int dCol = endCol - startCol; 
      int dRow = endRow - startRow;

      int halfWidth = maxPerimeter / 2;
      if (dCol < -halfWidth) dCol += maxPerimeter;
      if (dCol > halfWidth) dCol -= maxPerimeter;

      at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir stepDir = null;
      if (dRow == 0) {
        stepDir = (dCol > 0) ? at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.EAST : at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.WEST;
      } else if (dRow > 0) {
        if (startRow % 2 == 1) {
          stepDir = (dCol > 0) ? at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.NORTH_EAST : at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.NORTH_WEST;
        } else {
          stepDir = (dCol >= 0) ? at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.NORTH_EAST : at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.NORTH_WEST;
        }
      } else {
        if (startRow % 2 == 1) {
          stepDir = (dCol <= 0) ? at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.SOUTH_WEST : at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.SOUTH_EAST;
        } else {
          stepDir = (dCol < 0) ? at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.SOUTH_WEST : at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir.SOUTH_EAST;
        }
      }
      if (stepDir != null) directions.add(stepDir);
    }

    if (directions.isEmpty()) return;
    at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir compass = directions.get(0);
    StringBuilder signatureBuilder = new StringBuilder();

    for (int i = 1; directions.size() > i; i++) {
      at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir nextDir = directions.get(i);
      int turnOrdinal = (nextDir.ordinal() - compass.ordinal() + 6) % 6;
      switch (turnOrdinal) {
        case 0 -> signatureBuilder.append("w"); case 1 -> signatureBuilder.append("e"); case 2 -> signatureBuilder.append("d"); 
        case 3 -> signatureBuilder.append("s"); case 4 -> signatureBuilder.append("a"); case 5 -> signatureBuilder.append("q"); 
      }
      compass = nextDir;
    }

    if (signatureBuilder.length() == 0) signatureBuilder.append("w");
    String signature = signatureBuilder.toString();
    at.petrak.hexcasting.api.casting.math.HexDir startDir = directions.get(0);

    if (com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.isActive()) {
      com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.appendDrawnPattern(
        signature, startDir.name(), "Sigil"
      );
      return;
    }

    player.displayClientMessage(net.minecraft.network.chat.Component.literal("§d[Arcane Grid] Signature: " + signature + " (" + startDir.name() + ")"), true);

    String unifiedPayloadString = "hexacreatecore:cast_3d_spell;" + startDir.ordinal() + ";" + signature;
    net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.DialogueActionPayload(player.getId(), unifiedPayloadString, false));
  }


  public static void loadExternalGridConfig() {
    java.io.File configFile = new java.io.File(
      net.minecraft.client.Minecraft.getInstance().gameDirectory, 
      "config/hexacreatecore/grid_config.json"
    );

    if (!configFile.exists()) {
      try {
        if (configFile.getParentFile() != null) {
          configFile.getParentFile().mkdirs();
        }
        String defaultJson = """
        {
        "point_color": "0xFF21B7D1",
        "point_hover_color_hex": "0xFFFFFF66",
        "line_color": "0xFFB233FF",
        "outline_color": "0xFF000000",
        "line_size": 0.0225,
        "outline_size": 2.0,
        "wobble_intensity": 0.015
        }
        """;
        java.nio.file.Files.writeString(configFile.toPath(), defaultJson, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      } catch (Exception writeException) {
        writeException.printStackTrace();
      }
      return;
    }

    try {
      String jsonContent = new String(java.nio.file.Files.readAllBytes(configFile.toPath()), java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

      pointColorRaw = parseHexOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "point_color"), 0xFF21B7D1);
      pointHoverColorRaw = parseHexOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "point_hover_color_hex"), 0xFFFFFF66);
      lineColorRaw = parseHexOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "line_color"), 0xFFB233FF);
      outlineColorRaw = parseHexOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "outline_color"), 0xFF000000);

      configLineSize = (float) parseDoubleOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "line_size"), 0.0225D);
      configOutlineSizeMultiplier = (float) parseDoubleOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "outline_size"), 2.0D);
      configWobbleIntensity = parseDoubleOrDefault(extractJsonKeyValue(jsonContent, "wobble_intensity"), 0.015D);
    } catch (Exception loaderCleanException) {
      loaderCleanException.printStackTrace();
    }
  }


  private static String extractJsonKeyValue(String json, String key) {
    String patternTarget = "\"" + key + "\""; if (!json.contains(patternTarget)) return "";
    int offset = json.indexOf(patternTarget) + patternTarget.length();
    int end = json.indexOf("\n", offset); if (end == -1) end = json.indexOf("}", offset);
    return json.substring(offset, end).replace(":", "").trim();
  }

  private static int parseHexOrDefault(String v, int f) {
    try { String c = v.replaceAll("[\"\\s]", ""); if (c.startsWith("0x") || c.startsWith("0X")) c = c.substring(2); return (int) Long.parseLong(c, 16); } catch (Exception e) { return f; }
  }
  private static double parseDoubleOrDefault(String v, double f) { try { return Double.parseDouble(v.replaceAll("[\"\\s]", "")); } catch (Exception e) { return f; } }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onMouseScroll(net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent.MouseScrollingEvent event) {
    if (!isGridActive) return;
    if (com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.isRiftMode()) {
      com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.handleRiftScroll((float) event.getScrollDeltaY());
    } else {
      handleStackScroll((float) event.getScrollDeltaY());
    }
    event.setCanceled(true);
  }


  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onScreenOpening(net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent.Opening event) {
    if (!isGridActive) return;

    net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen screen = event.getScreen();
    if (screen instanceof net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.PauseScreen
    || screen instanceof net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen<?>) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
      deactivateGrid();
    }
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRightClickStaff(net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickItem event) {
    if (isGridActive) {
      event.setCancellationResult(net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult.FAIL);
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    if (!event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;

    Player player = event.getEntity();
    net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation itemId = net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(event.getItemStack().getItem());
    boolean isStaff = player.isShiftKeyDown() && itemId != null && "hexcasting".equals(itemId.getNamespace()) && itemId.getPath().contains("staff");
    boolean isEverbook = event.getItemStack().getItem() instanceof com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.custom.EverbookItem;

    if (isStaff || isEverbook) {
      STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.clear();
      ravensmindString = null;
      stackScrollIndex = 0;

      try {
        Class<?> clientStackClass = Class.forName("at.petrak.hexcasting.client.ClientCastingStack");
        for (java.lang.reflect.Method stackMethod : clientStackClass.getDeclaredMethods()) {
          if (stackMethod.getName().contains("clear")) {
            stackMethod.setAccessible(true);
            Class<?>[] paramTypes = stackMethod.getParameterTypes();
            if (paramTypes.length == 0) {
              stackMethod.invoke(null);
            } else if (paramTypes.length == 1 && paramTypes[0] == java.util.UUID.class) {
              stackMethod.invoke(null, player.getUUID());
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

      net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor.sendToServer(
        new com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network.DialogueActionPayload(player.getId(), "hexacreatecore:clear_3d_stack", false)
      );

      activateCastingGrid(player);
      if (isEverbook) {
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.activateForPlayer(player, event.getItemStack(), event.getHand());
      }
      event.setCancellationResult(net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult.SUCCESS);
      event.setCanceled(true);
    }
  }


  public static void handleStackScroll(float delta) { if (delta > 0.0f) { if (stackScrollIndex > 0) stackScrollIndex--; } else if (delta < 0.0f) { int maxScroll = Math.max(0, STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS.size() - 10); if (maxScroll > stackScrollIndex) stackScrollIndex++; } }
  public static List<ArcaneNode3D> getActiveNodes() { return ACTIVE_GRID_NODES; }
  public static List<Integer> getActiveDrawingPath() { return ACTIVE_DRAWING_PATH; }
  public static List<List<Integer>> getPersistentLines() { return PERSISTENT_SPELL_LINES; }
  public static List<String> getStackStrings() { return STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS; }
  public static String getRavensmindString() { return ravensmindString; }
  public static int getScrollIndex() { return stackScrollIndex; }
  public static boolean isActive() { return isGridActive; }
  public static void handleMouseTurn(double mx, double my) { prevCamYaw = camYaw; prevCamPitch = camPitch; camYaw += (float) (mx * 0.15F); camPitch = net.minecraft.util.Mth.clamp(camPitch + (float) (my * 0.15F), -90.0F, 90.0F); }
  public static void setFreeCamRotation(float yaw, float pitch) { prevCamYaw = camYaw; prevCamPitch = camPitch; camYaw = yaw; camPitch = pitch; }
  public static Vec3 getLerpedCameraPos(float pt) { return new Vec3(net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(pt, prevCamX, camX), net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(pt, prevCamY, camY), net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(pt, prevCamZ, camZ)); }
  public static float getLerpedCameraYaw(float pt) { return net.minecraft.util.Mth.rotLerp(pt, prevCamYaw, camYaw); }
  public static float getLerpedCameraPitch(float pt) { return net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(pt, prevCamPitch, camPitch); }
  public static int getLineColorRaw() { return lineColorRaw; }
  public static int getOutlineColorRaw() { return outlineColorRaw; }
  public static float getConfigLineSize() { return configLineSize; }
  public static float getConfigOutlineSizeMultiplier() { return configOutlineSizeMultiplier; }
  public static Vec3 getCenterAnchorPoint() { return centerAnchorPoint; }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/HexCastingClientEvents.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.MovementInputUpdateEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "hexacreatecore", value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class HexCastingClientEvents {

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onMovementInput(MovementInputUpdateEvent event) {
    if (!HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;

    // Direct movement translation update pass onto our active spatial tracking frame
    HexagonalGridManager.handleFreeCamMovement(event.getInput());

    // Secure physical layer: wipe character movement impulses to lock player entity position in the world
    event.getInput().forwardImpulse = 0.0f;
    event.getInput().leftImpulse = 0.0f;
    event.getInput().up = false;
    event.getInput().down = false;
    event.getInput().left = false;
    event.getInput().right = false;
    event.getInput().jumping = false;
    event.getInput().shiftKeyDown = false;
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onMouseScroll(InputEvent.MouseScrollingEvent event) {
    if (!HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;
    HexagonalGridManager.handleStackScroll((float) event.getScrollDeltaY());
    event.setCanceled(true);
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/HexCastingGeometry.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.Tesselator;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferBuilder;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.DefaultVertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferUploader;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import java.util.List;

public final class HexCastingGeometry {
  private static Vec3 smoothedHudPos = null;
  private static float smoothedHudYaw = 0.0f;
  private static final TextEffectStyle ravensMindStyle = TextEffectStyle.outline(0xFFCD7F32);
  TextEffectStyle hexStackStyle = TextEffectStyle.aurora(0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFA855F7);    // Mostly White & HexCasting Purple Aurora

  public static void render3DCellShadedLines(PoseStack poseStack, List<List<Integer>> persistentLines, List<Integer> activePath, List<ArcaneNode3D> gridNodes, Vec3 cameraPos, int baseColorHex) {
    if (gridNodes.isEmpty()) return;
    boolean hasActiveLine = (activePath != null && activePath.size() >= 2);
    boolean hasPersistentLines = (persistentLines != null && !persistentLines.isEmpty());
    if (!hasActiveLine && !hasPersistentLines) return;

    RenderSystem.enableBlend();
    RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
    Tesselator tesselator = Tesselator.getInstance();
    float coreThickness = 0.0225f;

    GlStateManager._enableCull();
    GL11.glCullFace(GL11.GL_FRONT);
    BufferBuilder outlineBuffer = tesselator.begin(VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);
    renderScenePrisms(poseStack, outlineBuffer, persistentLines, activePath, gridNodes, cameraPos, coreThickness * 2.0f, 0xFF000000);
    BufferUploader.drawWithShader(outlineBuffer.buildOrThrow());

    GL11.glCullFace(GL11.GL_BACK);
    BufferBuilder coreBuffer = tesselator.begin(VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);
    renderScenePrisms(poseStack, coreBuffer, persistentLines, activePath, gridNodes, cameraPos, coreThickness, 0xFFB233FF);
    BufferUploader.drawWithShader(coreBuffer.buildOrThrow());
    RenderSystem.disableBlend();
  }

  public static void render3DStackHUD(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Vec3 center, Vec3 cameraPos, float camYaw, float camPitch, int activeShellIndex, List<String> stackStrings, String ravensmind, int scrollIndex) {
    if (com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSessionState.isActive()) {
      smoothedHudPos = null;
      return;
    }
    if (center == null || !HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) {
      smoothedHudPos = null;
      return;
    }

    List<String> allItems = HexagonalGridManager.getStackStrings();
    String activeRavensmind = HexagonalGridManager.getRavensmindString();
    boolean hasRavensmind = (activeRavensmind != null && !activeRavensmind.isEmpty());

    if (allItems.isEmpty() && !hasRavensmind) {
      smoothedHudPos = null;
      return;
    }

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    Font font = mc.font;

    int startIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(scrollIndex, Math.max(0, allItems.size() - 10)));
    int endIndex = Math.min(allItems.size(), startIndex + 10);

    double orbitRadius = 2.0D * (activeShellIndex + 2);
    double yawRad = Math.toRadians(camYaw);
    double lookX = -Math.sin(yawRad);
    double lookZ = Math.cos(yawRad);

    double relX = cameraPos.x - center.x;
    double relZ = cameraPos.z - center.z;
    double b = relX * lookX + relZ * lookZ;
    double c = (relX * relX + relZ * relZ) - (orbitRadius * orbitRadius);
    double discriminant = (b * b) - c;

    double goalX;
    double goalZ;
    if (discriminant >= 0.0D) {
      double t = -b + Math.sqrt(discriminant);
      goalX = cameraPos.x + (lookX * t);
      goalZ = cameraPos.z + (lookZ * t);
    } else {
      goalX = center.x + (lookX * orbitRadius);
      goalZ = center.z + (lookZ * orbitRadius);
    }

    double sineBob = Math.sin(System.currentTimeMillis() / 900.0D) * 0.06D;
    double goalY = center.y + 4.50D + sineBob;
    Vec3 goalPos = new Vec3(goalX, goalY, goalZ);

    float dt = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
    double linearAlpha = Math.min(1.0D, dt * 2.5D);
    double sineEase = Math.sin(linearAlpha * (Math.PI / 2.0D));

    if (smoothedHudPos == null || smoothedHudPos.distanceToSqr(goalPos) > 100.0D) {
      smoothedHudPos = goalPos;
      smoothedHudYaw = camYaw;
    } else {
      smoothedHudPos = smoothedHudPos.lerp(goalPos, sineEase);
      float yawDiff = net.minecraft.util.Mth.wrapDegrees(camYaw - smoothedHudYaw);
      smoothedHudYaw += yawDiff * (float) sineEase;
    }

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(smoothedHudPos.x - cameraPos.x, smoothedHudPos.y - cameraPos.y, smoothedHudPos.z - cameraPos.z);
    poseStack.mulPose(com.mojang.math.Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(-smoothedHudYaw - 180.0F));
    poseStack.scale(0.010F, -0.010F, 0.010F);

    float currentPanelLocalY = 0.0f;
    int fixedBoxWidth = 150;
    int maxTextWidth = fixedBoxWidth - 16;

    var ravensMindStyle = com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle.outline(0xFFFFD700, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    var hexAuroraStyle = com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.TextEffectStyle.aurora(0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFA855F7);

    // 1. Pinned Raven's Mind box at the very top of the floating list
    if (hasRavensmind) {
      String cleanRavenVal = net.minecraft.ChatFormatting.stripFormatting(activeRavensmind);
      if (cleanRavenVal == null) cleanRavenVal = activeRavensmind;
      String ravenPrefix = "[Raven's Mind] ";
      List<String> ravenLines = wrapPlainText(font, ravenPrefix + cleanRavenVal, maxTextWidth);
      float ravenHeight = (ravenLines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, currentPanelLocalY, 0.0f);

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
        fixedBoxWidth, ravenHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0x1A241808, 0x1AFFD700, List.of(), false
      );

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.25f);
      float textLeftX = -(fixedBoxWidth * 0.5f) + 8.0f;
      float textTopY = -(ravenLines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);

      for (int l = 0; ravenLines.size() > l; l++) {
        String line = ravenLines.get(l);
        float ly = textTopY + (l * font.lineHeight);
        if (l == 0 && line.startsWith(ravenPrefix)) {
          String rest = line.substring(ravenPrefix.length());
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
            poseStack, bufferSource, font, ravenPrefix, textLeftX, ly, ravensMindStyle
          );
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
            poseStack, bufferSource, font, rest, textLeftX + font.width(ravenPrefix), ly, hexAuroraStyle
          );
        } else {
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
            poseStack, bufferSource, font, line, textLeftX, ly, hexAuroraStyle
          );
        }
      }
      poseStack.popPose();

      poseStack.popPose();
      currentPanelLocalY += ravenHeight + 10.0f;
    }

    // 2. Scrollable 10-slot stack boxes below Raven's Mind
    for (int i = startIndex; endIndex > i; i++) {
      String cleanIota = net.minecraft.ChatFormatting.stripFormatting(allItems.get(i));
      if (cleanIota == null) cleanIota = allItems.get(i);
      String idxPrefix = "[" + i + "] ";
      List<String> lines = wrapPlainText(font, idxPrefix + cleanIota, maxTextWidth);
      float boxHeight = (lines.size() * font.lineHeight) + 12.0f;

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, currentPanelLocalY, 0.0f);

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
        fixedBoxWidth, boxHeight, 3.0f, 3.0f,
        0x1A110E1A, 0x1A5A2E85, List.of(), false
      );

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.25f);
      float textLeftX = -(fixedBoxWidth * 0.5f) + 8.0f;
      float textTopY = -(lines.size() * font.lineHeight * 0.5f);

      for (int l = 0; lines.size() > l; l++) {
        String line = lines.get(l);
        float ly = textTopY + (l * font.lineHeight);
        if (l == 0 && line.startsWith(idxPrefix)) {
          String rest = line.substring(idxPrefix.length());
          font.drawInBatch(
            idxPrefix, textLeftX, ly, 0xFFFFFF55, true,
            poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0,
            net.minecraft.client.renderer.LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT
          );
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
            poseStack, bufferSource, font, rest, textLeftX + font.width(idxPrefix), ly, hexAuroraStyle
          );
        } else {
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text.ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.draw3D(
            poseStack, bufferSource, font, line, textLeftX, ly, hexAuroraStyle
          );
        }
      }
      poseStack.popPose();

      poseStack.popPose();
      currentPanelLocalY += boxHeight + 8.0f;
    }

    poseStack.popPose();
  }

  private static List<String> wrapPlainText(Font font, String text, int maxWidth) {
    List<String> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    for (FormattedCharSequence seq : font.split(Component.literal(text), maxWidth)) {
      StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
      seq.accept((idx, style, codePoint) -> {
        sb.appendCodePoint(codePoint);
        return true;
      });
      out.add(sb.toString());
    }
    return out.isEmpty() ? List.of("") : out;
  }

  private static void renderScenePrisms(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer consumer, List<List<Integer>> persistentLines, List<Integer> activePath, List<ArcaneNode3D> gridNodes, Vec3 cameraPos, float thickness, int colorHex) {
    drawSingleLineStream(poseStack, consumer, activePath, gridNodes, cameraPos, thickness, colorHex);
    for (List<Integer> completeLine : persistentLines) {
      drawSingleLineStream(poseStack, consumer, completeLine, gridNodes, cameraPos, thickness, colorHex);
    }
  }

  private static void drawSingleLineStream(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer consumer, List<Integer> path, List<ArcaneNode3D> gridNodes, Vec3 cameraPos, float thickness, int colorHex) {
    if (path == null || path.size() < 2) return;
    for (int i = 0; path.size() - 1 > i; i++) {
      ArcaneNode3D startNode = gridNodes.get(path.get(i));
      ArcaneNode3D endNode = gridNodes.get(path.get(i + 1));
      if (startNode == null || endNode == null) continue;

      Vec3 start = startNode.worldPos(); Vec3 end = endNode.worldPos();
      Vec3 midpoint = new Vec3((start.x + end.x) / 2.0D, (start.y + end.y) / 2.0D, (start.z + end.z) / 2.0D);

      double dx = end.x - start.x; double dy = end.y - start.y; double dz = end.z - start.z;
      float lineLength = (float) Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
      if (lineLength < 0.001f) continue;

      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(midpoint.x - cameraPos.x, midpoint.y - cameraPos.y, midpoint.z - cameraPos.z);
      poseStack.mulPose(new Quaternionf().rotationTo(new Vector3f(0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f), new Vector3f((float)(dx / lineLength), (float)(dy / lineLength), (float)(dz / lineLength))));
      RawGeometry.drawPrism(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), thickness, thickness, lineLength, colorHex);
      poseStack.popPose();
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/hexcasting/HexCastingGridInterceptor.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = "hexacreatecore", value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class HexCastingGridInterceptor {

  /**
   * Intercepts Hexcasting flat screen creations and boots up the 3D hexagonal cage.
   * Never toggles off if already active so right-clicking with the staff can draw lines.
   */
  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onScreenOpening(ScreenEvent.Opening event) {
    String screenClassName = event.getScreen().getClass().getName();
    
    if (screenClassName.contains("at.petrak.hexcasting.client.gui") || screenClassName.contains("PatternCastingScreen")) {
      event.setCanceled(true);

      if (HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;

      Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
      if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;

      HexagonalGridManager.activateCastingGrid(mc.player);
      mc.level.playSeededSound(null, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(), 
          SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.0f, mc.level.getRandom().nextLong());
    }
  }

  /**
   * Exits 3D casting mode cleanly when pressing ESC or the Inventory key.
   */
  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onKeyInput(InputEvent.Key event) {
    if (!HexagonalGridManager.isActive()) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.screen != null || mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;

    boolean isExitKey = event.getKey() == InputConstants.KEY_ESCAPE
        || mc.options.keyInventory.matches(event.getKey(), event.getScanCode());

    if (isExitKey && event.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS) {
      HexagonalGridManager.deactivateGrid();
      mc.level.playSeededSound(null, mc.player.getX(), mc.player.getY(), mc.player.getZ(), 
          SoundEvents.ENDERMAN_TELEPORT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 0.6f, mc.level.getRandom().nextLong());
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/item/custom/BossRushTriggerItem.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.item.custom;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.BossRushManager;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class BossRushTriggerItem extends Item {
