==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/network/DialogueClientPackets.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.HexagonalGridManager;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public final class DialogueClientPackets {

  /**
   * Unloads the incoming server stack string data directly into your active 3D HUD arrays.
   */
  public static void handleStackSyncOnClient(final DialogueStackSyncPayload payload, final IPayloadContext context) {
    context.enqueueWork(() -> {
      try {
        // Find our active text collections via reflectively matching fields or clear public helpers
        java.lang.reflect.Field stringsField = HexagonalGridManager.class.getDeclaredField("STACK_DISPLAY_STRINGS");
        stringsField.setAccessible(true);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        java.util.List<String> activeList = (java.util.List<String>) stringsField.get(null);

        activeList.clear();
        activeList.addAll(payload.stackDescs());

        java.lang.reflect.Field mindField = HexagonalGridManager.class.getDeclaredField("ravensmindString");
        mindField.setAccessible(true);
        mindField.set(null, payload.ravensmind().isEmpty() ? null : payload.ravensmind());
      } catch (Exception e) {
        e.printStackTrace();
      }
    });
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/network/DialogueNetworking.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.WonderumAnchorPayload;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.DialogueAction;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.hologram.MultiblockHologramRenderer;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.HexagonalGridManager;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLCommonSetupEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforgespi.language.ModFileScanData;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.event.RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.registration.PayloadRegistrar;
import org.objectweb.asm.Type;
import software.bernie.geckolib.animatable.GeoEntity;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.lang.reflect.Modifier;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.BiConsumer;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
public final class DialogueNetworking {
  private record ActionEntry(BiConsumer<ServerPlayer, Entity> handler, boolean selfTargeted) {}
  private record PendingProjection(String structureId, int durationTicks, long queuedAtTick) {}

  private static final Map<String, ActionEntry> SERVER_ACTIONS = new HashMap<>();
  private static final Map<UUID, PendingProjection> PENDING_PLACEMENTS = new HashMap<>();

  public static void registerAction(String actionId, BiConsumer<ServerPlayer, Entity> handler) {
    registerAction(actionId, false, handler);
  }

  public static void registerAction(String actionId, boolean selfTargeted, BiConsumer<ServerPlayer, Entity> handler) {
    SERVER_ACTIONS.put(actionId, new ActionEntry(handler, selfTargeted));
  }

  public static void scanAnnotatedActions() {
    Type targetType = Type.getType(DialogueAction.class);
    int discoveredCount = 0;

    for (ModFileScanData scanData : ModList.get().getAllScanData()) {
      for (ModFileScanData.AnnotationData annotation : scanData.getAnnotations()) {
        if (!targetType.equals(annotation.annotationType())) continue;

        try {
          Class<?> ownerClass = Class.forName(annotation.clazz().getClassName());
          String rawMember = annotation.memberName();
          String methodName = rawMember.contains("(") ? rawMember.substring(0, rawMember.indexOf('(')) : rawMember;

          Method method = ownerClass.getDeclaredMethod(methodName, ServerPlayer.class, Entity.class);
          if (!Modifier.isStatic(method.getModifiers())) {
            HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("@DialogueAction method {}.{} must be static!", ownerClass.getName(), methodName);
            continue;
          }

          method.setAccessible(true);
          DialogueAction meta = method.getAnnotation(DialogueAction.class);

          registerAction(meta.value(), meta.selfTargeted(), (player, entity) -> {
            try {
              method.invoke(null, player, entity);
            } catch (Exception e) {
              HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("Failed executing @DialogueAction '{}'", meta.value(), e);
            }
          });
          discoveredCount++;
        } catch (Exception e) {
          HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.error("Failed registering @DialogueAction on {}", annotation.clazz().getClassName(), e);
        }
      }
    }

    HexaCreateCore.LOGGER.info("Auto-registered {} @DialogueAction handlers.", discoveredCount);
  }

  /**
   * Universally commands any GeoEntity's specified animation controller to force-switch
   * its active data-driven JSON sequence over the network pipeline.
   */
  public static void syncAnimationOverride(Entity npc, String controllerName, String sequenceName) {
    if (npc instanceof GeoEntity geoEntity) {
      geoEntity.triggerAnim(controllerName, "override:" + sequenceName);
    }
  }

  private static void handleOnServer(DialogueActionPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
    context.enqueueWork(() -> {
      if (!(context.player() instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer)) return;

      String rawActionId = payload.actionId();
      String lookupKey = rawActionId;

      if (rawActionId.contains(";")) {
        lookupKey = rawActionId.split(";")[0];
      }

      ActionEntry entry = SERVER_ACTIONS.get(lookupKey);
      if (entry != null) {
        if (entry.selfTargeted()) {
          serverPlayer.getPersistentData().putString("hexacreatecore_raw_action", rawActionId);
          serverPlayer.getPersistentData().putInt("hexacreatecore_raw_entity_id", payload.entityId());

          entry.handler().accept(serverPlayer, serverPlayer);
          return;
        }

        Entity targetEntity = serverPlayer.level().getEntity(payload.entityId());
        if (targetEntity == null) return;

        // Security check boundary: ensure player is within interaction reach (6 blocks)
        if (serverPlayer.distanceToSqr(targetEntity) > 36.0D) return;

        entry.handler().accept(serverPlayer, targetEntity);
      }
    });
  }

  public static void queueHologramPlacement(ServerPlayer player, String structureId, int durationTicks) {
    PENDING_PLACEMENTS.put(
      player.getUUID(),
      new PendingProjection(structureId, durationTicks, player.level().getGameTime())
    );
    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(
      player,
      new HologramProjectionPayload(structureId, BlockPos.ZERO, -1)
    );
  }

  public static void clearPendingPlayerPlacement(ServerPlayer player) {
    PENDING_PLACEMENTS.remove(player.getUUID());
  }

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRightClickBlock(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickBlock event) {
    if (event.getLevel().isClientSide) return;
    if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer)) return;

    PendingProjection pending = PENDING_PLACEMENTS.get(serverPlayer.getUUID());
    if (pending == null) return;

    // Ignore the residual right-click from the dialogue button itself (wait at least 3 ticks)
    if (3L > serverPlayer.level().getGameTime() - pending.queuedAtTick()) {
      event.setCanceled(true);
      return;
    }

    PENDING_PLACEMENTS.remove(serverPlayer.getUUID());
    BlockPos anchor = event.getPos().above();

    PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(
      serverPlayer,
      new HologramProjectionPayload(pending.structureId(), anchor, pending.durationTicks())
    );

    event.setCanceled(true);
  }

  @EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID)
  public static final class ModBusRegistry {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onCommonSetup(FMLCommonSetupEvent event) {
      event.enqueueWork(DialogueNetworking::scanAnnotatedActions);
    }

      @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRegisterPayloads(RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent event) {
      final PayloadRegistrar registrar = event.registrar("1.0");

      registrar.playToServer(
        DialogueActionPayload.TYPE,
        DialogueActionPayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        DialogueNetworking::handleOnServer
      );

      registrar.playToClient(
        HologramProjectionPayload.TYPE,
        HologramProjectionPayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        (payload, context) -> context.enqueueWork(() -> MultiblockHologramRenderer.setProjection(payload))
      );

      registrar.playToClient(
        LiveStackUpdatePayload.TYPE,
        LiveStackUpdatePayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        (payload, context) -> context.enqueueWork(() -> {
          HexagonalGridManager.updateLiveCastingStack(payload.stackDescs(), payload.ravensmind());
        })
      );

      registrar.playToClient(
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.network.BossRushSyncPayload.TYPE,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.network.BossRushSyncPayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        (payload, context) -> context.enqueueWork(() ->
          com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.bossrush.client.BossRushClientHud.handleSyncPacket(payload)
        )
      );

      registrar.playToServer(
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSyncPayload.TYPE,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSyncPayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookSyncPayload::handleServer
      );

      registrar.playBidirectional(
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookEvalPayload.TYPE,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookEvalPayload.STREAM_CODEC.cast(),
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.hexcasting.everbook.EverbookEvalPayload::handle
      );

      registrar.playToServer(
        WonderumAnchorPayload.TYPE,
        WonderumAnchorPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
        WonderumAnchorPayload::handleServer
      );

      registrar.playToClient(
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole.WormholeSyncPayload.TYPE,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole.WormholeSyncPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.wormhole.WormholeSyncPayload::handleClient
      );

      registrar.playToServer(
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.blueprint.WonderumDraftBlockPayload.TYPE,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.blueprint.WonderumDraftBlockPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
        com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.create.wonderum.blueprint.WonderumDraftBlockPayload::handleServer
      );
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/network/LiveStackUpdatePayload.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.network;

import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import java.util.List;

public record LiveStackUpdatePayload(List<String> stackDescs, String ravensmind) implements CustomPacketPayload {
  public static final Type<LiveStackUpdatePayload> TYPE = new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("hexacreatecore", "live_stack_update"));

  public LiveStackUpdatePayload(List<String> stackDescs) {
    this(stackDescs, "");
  }

  public static final StreamCodec<FriendlyByteBuf, LiveStackUpdatePayload> STREAM_CODEC = StreamCodec.of(
  (buf, payload) -> {
      buf.writeInt(payload.stackDescs().size());
      for (String str : payload.stackDescs()) {
        buf.writeUtf(str);
      }
      buf.writeUtf(payload.ravensmind() != null ? payload.ravensmind() : "");
    },
    buf -> {
      int size = buf.readInt();
      java.util.List<String> list = new java.util.ArrayList<>(size);
      for (int i = 0; size > i; i++) {
        list.add(buf.readUtf());
      }
      String raven = buf.readUtf();
      return new LiveStackUpdatePayload(list, raven);
    }
  );

  @Override
  public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
    return TYPE;
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/RawGeometry.java <==
/*
 * THIS PART OF THE CODE IS MEANT FOR DEVELOPERS
 * */
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.Overload;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.DefaultVertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexFormat;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderStateShard;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;

import java.util.List;

import org.joml.Matrix4f;

public class RawGeometry {
  private RawGeometry() {} // Prevent instantiation
  public static final RenderType DIALOGUE_SOLID = RenderType.create(
    "dialogue_solid",
    DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR,
    VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS,
    256,
    false,
    true,
    RenderType.CompositeState.builder()
    .setShaderState(RenderStateShard.POSITION_COLOR_SHADER)
    .setTransparencyState(RenderStateShard.TRANSLUCENT_TRANSPARENCY)
    .setCullState(RenderStateShard.NO_CULL)
    .createCompositeState(false)
  );


  public static void drawRectangle(VertexConsumer consumer, Matrix4f matrix, float width, float height, int hexColor) {
    float halfW = width / 2.0f;
    float halfH = height / 2.0f;

    int a = (hexColor >> 24) & 0xFF;
    int r = (hexColor >> 16) & 0xFF;
    int g = (hexColor >> 8) & 0xFF;
    int b = hexColor & 0xFF;

    // Counter-clockwise quad mapping
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  halfW,  halfH, 0.0f, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -halfW,  halfH, 0.0f, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -halfW, -halfH, 0.0f, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  halfW, -halfH, 0.0f, r, g, b, a);
  }

  public static void drawPrism(VertexConsumer consumer, Matrix4f matrix, float width, float height, float depth, int hexColor) {
    float hW = width / 2.0f;
    float hH = height / 2.0f;
    float hD = depth / 2.0f;

    int a = (hexColor >> 24) & 0xFF;
    int r = (hexColor >> 16) & 0xFF;
    int g = (hexColor >> 8) & 0xFF;
    int b = hexColor & 0xFF;

    // Front Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);

    // Back Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);

    // Top Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);

    // Bottom Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);

    // Right Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix,  hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);

    // Left Face
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW,  hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH, -hD, r, g, b, a);
    vertex(consumer, matrix, -hW, -hH,  hD, r, g, b, a);
  }

  public static void drawBorderedPanel(
      PoseStack poseStack,
      VertexConsumer consumer,
      float width,
      float height,
      float borderThickness,
      float borderDepth,
      int bgColor,
      int borderColor
  ) {
    drawRectangle(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), width, height, bgColor);

    float halfW = width * 0.5f;
    float halfH = height * 0.5f;
    float halfBorder = borderThickness * 0.5f;
    float horizSpan = width + (borderThickness * 2.0f);

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(0.0f, -(halfH + halfBorder), 0.0f);
    drawPrism(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), horizSpan, borderThickness, borderDepth, borderColor);
    poseStack.popPose();

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(0.0f, (halfH + halfBorder), 0.0f);
    drawPrism(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), horizSpan, borderThickness, borderDepth, borderColor);
    poseStack.popPose();

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(-(halfW + halfBorder), 0.0f, 0.0f);
    drawPrism(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), borderThickness, height, borderDepth, borderColor);
    poseStack.popPose();

    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate((halfW + halfBorder), 0.0f, 0.0f);
    drawPrism(consumer, poseStack.last().pose(), borderThickness, height, borderDepth, borderColor);
    poseStack.popPose();
  }

  public static void drawDialogueNode(
    PoseStack poseStack,
    MultiBufferSource bufferSource,
    Font font,
    float localX,
    float localY,
    float localZ,
    float width,
    float height,
    float borderThickness,
    float borderDepth,
    int bgColor,
    int borderColor,
    // Different here
    List<FormattedCharSequence> lines,
    boolean centerText
  ) {
    poseStack.pushPose();
    poseStack.translate(localX, localY, localZ);

    VertexConsumer consumer = bufferSource.getBuffer(DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    drawBorderedPanel(poseStack, consumer, width, height, borderThickness, borderDepth, bgColor, borderColor);

    if (lines != null && !lines.isEmpty()) {
      poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.5f);
      float textStartY = -(height * 0.5f) + 6.0f;
      Matrix4f textMatrix = poseStack.last().pose();

      for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
        FormattedCharSequence line = lines.get(i);
        float textStartX = centerText
          ? -(font.width(line) * 0.5f)
          : -(width * 0.5f) + 8.0f;

        font.drawInBatch(
          line,
          textStartX,
          textStartY + (i * font.lineHeight),
          0xFFFFFF,
          false,
          textMatrix,
          bufferSource,
          Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL,
          0,
          15728880
        );
      }
    }

    poseStack.popPose();
  }

  @Overload
  public static void drawDialogueNode(
    PoseStack poseStack,
    MultiBufferSource bufferSource,
    Font font,
    float localX,
    float localY,
    float localZ,
    float width,
    float height,
    float borderThickness,
    float borderDepth,
    int bgColor,
    int borderColor,
    // Different here
    DialoguePlaybackState playback,
    boolean typewriter,
    boolean centerText
  ) {
    List<FormattedCharSequence> linesToDraw = typewriter
    ? playback.getVisibleLines()
    : playback.getFullLines();

    drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack,
      bufferSource,
      font,
      localX,
      localY,
      localZ,
      width,
      height,
      borderThickness,
      borderDepth,
      bgColor,
      borderColor,
      linesToDraw,
      centerText
    );
  }

  public static void drawProgressBar(
    com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack poseStack,
    net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource bufferSource,
    float x, float y, float z,
    float width, float height,
    float borderWidth,
    int bgColor, int borderColor, int progressColor,
    float progressRatio
  ) {
    drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, bufferSource, net.minecraft.client.Minecraft.getInstance().font,
      x, y, z, width, height, borderWidth, 0.0f,
      bgColor, borderColor, java.util.Collections.emptyList(), false
    );
    //Compute inner boundary coordinates for progress tracking strip
    float padding = borderWidth + 2.0f;
    float innerW = width - (padding * 2.0f);
    float innerH = height - (padding * 2.0f);

    float activeBarW = innerW * Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(1.0f, progressRatio));
    if (activeBarW <= 0.0f) return;

    float startX = x - (innerW * 0.5f) + (activeBarW * 0.5f);

    drawDialogueNode(
      poseStack, bufferSource, net.minecraft.client.Minecraft.getInstance().font,
      startX, y, z + 0.5f, activeBarW, innerH, 0.0f, 0.0f,
      progressColor, 0, java.util.Collections.emptyList(), false
    );
  }

    public static void drawArcaneNodePrism(
      com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack poseStack,
      net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource bufferSource,
      float x, float y, float z,
      float size,
      int color
  ) {
    // Use a clean, non-textured GUI or flat color layout to bypass atlas sheets entirely
    VertexConsumer buffer = bufferSource.getBuffer(net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType.gui());
    org.joml.Matrix4f matrix = poseStack.last().pose();

    int a = (color >> 24) & 0xFF;
    int r = (color >> 16) & 0xFF;
    int g = (color >> 8) & 0xFF;
    int b = color & 0xFF;

    float rS = size * 0.5f;

    // Front Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);

    // Back Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);

    // Top Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);

    // Bottom Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);

    // Right Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x + rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);

    // Left Face
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y - rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z + rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
    buffer.addVertex(matrix, x - rS, y + rS, z - rS).setColor(r, g, b, a);
  }

  public static void vertex(VertexConsumer c, Matrix4f m, float x, float y, float z, int argb) {
    c.addVertex(m, x, y, z).setColor(
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb),
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb),
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb),
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(argb)
    );
  }

  public static void vertex(VertexConsumer c, Matrix4f m, float x, float y, float z, int r, int g, int b, int a) {
    c.addVertex(m, x, y, z).setColor(r, g, b, a);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/dictionaries/AuroraRimDictionary.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries;

import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

public final class AuroraRimDictionary implements ShaderDictionary {

  public static final AuroraRimDictionary COSMIC = new AuroraRimDictionary(0xCCC026D3, 0xCC34D399, 0.74f, 0.90f);
  public static final AuroraRimDictionary EMERALD = new AuroraRimDictionary(0xCC34D399, 0xCC6EE7B7, 0.74f, 0.90f);
  public static final AuroraRimDictionary CRIMSON = new AuroraRimDictionary(0xCCFF1E56, 0xCCFF6B8B, 0.74f, 0.90f);

  private final int primaryRim, secondaryRim;
  private final float innerStartT, peakT;

  public AuroraRimDictionary(int primaryRim, int secondaryRim, float innerStartT, float peakT) {
    this.primaryRim = primaryRim;
    this.secondaryRim = secondaryRim;
    this.innerStartT = innerStartT;
    this.peakT = peakT;
  }

  @Override
  public int sampleArgb(float localX, float localY, double angleRad, float radialT, float timeSeconds) {
    if (innerStartT >= radialT) return 0x00000000;
    float wave = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((angleRad * 3.0D) + (timeSeconds * 2.4f));
    int peakColor = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(primaryRim, secondaryRim, wave);

    float intensity = (peakT >= radialT)
    ? Mth.clamp((radialT - innerStartT) / (peakT - innerStartT), 0.0f, 1.0f)
    : Mth.clamp((1.0f - radialT) / (1.0f - peakT), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    float smoothAlpha = intensity * intensity;
    return (peakColor & 0x00FFFFFF) | ((int) (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(peakColor) * smoothAlpha) << 24);
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/dictionaries/BlackHoleDictionary.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries;

import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

public final class BlackHoleDictionary implements ShaderDictionary {

  public static final BlackHoleDictionary SILVER_SINGULARITY = new BlackHoleDictionary(
    0xFF020205, // Pitch-black event horizon core
    0xFFF8FAFC, // Pure silver-white photon ring peak
    0xFF94A3B8, // Metallic slate-gray mid accretion
    0xFF334155  // Deep steel outer lensing halo
  );

  private final int singularityBlack;
  private final int photonSilver;
  private final int accretionGray;
  private final int outerSteel;

  public BlackHoleDictionary(int singularityBlack, int photonSilver, int accretionGray, int outerSteel) {
    this.singularityBlack = singularityBlack;
    this.photonSilver = photonSilver;
    this.accretionGray = accretionGray;
    this.outerSteel = outerSteel;
  }

  @Override
  public int sampleArgb(float localX, float localY, double angleRad, float radialT, float timeSeconds) {
    float horizonRadius = 0.42f;
    float photonPeak = 0.50f;

    // 1. Inside Schwarzschild Event Horizon: absolute pitch-black core
    if (horizonRadius >= radialT) {
      return singularityBlack;
    }

    // 2. Relativistic Doppler beaming + gravitational lensing swirl around the mass
    double spinAngle = angleRad - (timeSeconds * 2.6D);
    double lensingWarp = (horizonRadius * horizonRadius) / Math.max(0.08D, radialT - (horizonRadius * 0.65D));
    double warpedPhase = spinAngle + (lensingWarp * 3.4D);

    float doppler = 0.65f + 0.35f * (float) Math.cos(angleRad - 0.6D);
    float filamentA = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((warpedPhase * 2.0D) + (radialT * 10.0f));
    float filamentB = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.cos((warpedPhase * 3.0D) - (timeSeconds * 1.9f));

    // 3. Sharp Silver Photon Sphere Ring just outside the black horizon
    if (photonPeak >= radialT) {
      float ringT = Mth.clamp((radialT - horizonRadius) / (photonPeak - horizonRadius), 0.0f, 1.0f);
      int ringColor = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(singularityBlack, photonSilver, ringT * ringT * doppler);
      return ringColor | 0xFF000000;
    }

    // 4. Outer Silver -> Gray Lensed Accretion Disk fading smoothly to the edge
    float diskT = Mth.clamp((radialT - photonPeak) / (1.0f - photonPeak), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    int diskBase = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(photonSilver, accretionGray, Math.min(1.0f, diskT * 1.45f));
    int diskSwirl = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(diskBase, outerSteel, (1.0f - filamentA * 0.65f) * diskT);
    diskSwirl = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(diskSwirl, photonSilver, filamentB * (1.0f - diskT) * 0.45f * doppler);

    float outerFade = (diskT > 0.55f) ? Mth.clamp((1.0f - diskT) / 0.45f, 0.0f, 1.0f) : 1.0f;
    return (diskSwirl & 0x00FFFFFF) | ((int) (255 * outerFade) << 24);
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/dictionaries/CompositeDictionary.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries;

import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;

import java.util.List;

public final class CompositeDictionary implements ShaderDictionary {

  public static final ShaderDictionary EVERBOOK_DEFAULT = new CompositeDictionary(CosmicOceanDictionary.DEFAULT, AuroraRimDictionary.COSMIC);
  public static final ShaderDictionary EVERBOOK_EMERALD = new CompositeDictionary(CosmicOceanDictionary.EMERALD_TINT, AuroraRimDictionary.EMERALD);
  public static final ShaderDictionary EVERBOOK_CRIMSON = new CompositeDictionary(CosmicOceanDictionary.CRIMSON_TINT, AuroraRimDictionary.CRIMSON);

  private final List<ShaderDictionary> layers;

  public CompositeDictionary(ShaderDictionary... layers) {
    this.layers = List.of(layers);
  }

  @Override
  public int sampleArgb(float localX, float localY, double angleRad, float radialT, float timeSeconds) {
    int result = 0x00000000;
    for (ShaderDictionary layer : layers) {
      int top = layer.sampleArgb(localX, localY, angleRad, radialT, timeSeconds);
      int topAlpha = FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(top);
      if (topAlpha == 0) continue;
      if (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(result) == 0) {
        result = top;
      } else {
        result = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(result, (top & 0x00FFFFFF) | (Math.max(FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(result), topAlpha) << 24), topAlpha / 255.0f);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/dictionaries/CosmicOceanDictionary.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries;

import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

public final class CosmicOceanDictionary implements ShaderDictionary {

  public static final CosmicOceanDictionary DEFAULT = new CosmicOceanDictionary(0xD8090414, 0xD84C1D95, 0xD886198F, 0xD80D9488, 0x00000000, 0.0f);
  public static final CosmicOceanDictionary EMERALD_TINT = new CosmicOceanDictionary(0xD8090414, 0xD84C1D95, 0xD886198F, 0xD80D9488, 0xCC10B981, 0.35f);
  public static final CosmicOceanDictionary CRIMSON_TINT = new CosmicOceanDictionary(0xD8090414, 0xD84C1D95, 0xD886198F, 0xD80D9488, 0xCCFF1E56, 0.45f);

  private final int voidBase, violetMid, magentaCurrent, tealCurrent, tintColor;
  private final float tintStrength;

  public CosmicOceanDictionary(int voidBase, int violetMid, int magentaCurrent, int tealCurrent, int tintColor, float tintStrength) {
    this.voidBase = voidBase;
    this.violetMid = violetMid;
    this.magentaCurrent = magentaCurrent;
    this.tealCurrent = tealCurrent;
    this.tintColor = tintColor;
    this.tintStrength = tintStrength;
  }

  @Override
  public int sampleArgb(float localX, float localY, double angleRad, float radialT, float timeSeconds) {
    float driftOne = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((localX * 2.6f + localY * 1.9f) + (timeSeconds * 1.45f));
    float driftTwo = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.cos((-localX * 2.2f + localY * 2.8f) - (timeSeconds * 1.15f));
    float driftThree = 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin((localX * 1.5f - localY * 3.3f) + (timeSeconds * 1.85f));

    int base = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(voidBase, violetMid, (driftOne * 0.42f) + (driftTwo * 0.35f) + (driftThree * 0.23f));
    int color = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(base, ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(magentaCurrent, tealCurrent, driftTwo), driftThree * 0.48f);
    if (tintStrength > 0.001f) color = ShaderDictionary.lerpArgb(color, tintColor, tintStrength);

    float edgeFade = (radialT > 0.86f) ? Mth.clamp((1.0f - radialT) / 0.14f, 0.0f, 1.0f) : 1.0f;
    return (color & 0x00FFFFFF) | ((int) (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(color) * edgeFade) << 24);
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/dictionaries/ShaderDictionary.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries;

import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

@FunctionalInterface
public interface ShaderDictionary {
  int sampleArgb(float localX, float localY, double angleRad, float radialT, float timeSeconds);

  default ShaderDictionary then(ShaderDictionary overlay) {
    return new CompositeDictionary(this, overlay);
  }

  static int lerpArgb(int c0, int c1, float t) {
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(
      Mth.lerpInt(t, FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c1)),
      Mth.lerpInt(t, FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.red(c1)),
      Mth.lerpInt(t, FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c1)),
      Mth.lerpInt(t, FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c1))
    );
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/hooks/PolyhedronFaceShaderHook.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.ShaderDictionary;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

import java.util.List;

public final class PolyhedronFaceShaderHook {

  private final ShaderDictionary dictionary;
  private float alphaMultiplier = 0.36f;
  private int subdivisions = 4;

  private PolyhedronFaceShaderHook(ShaderDictionary dictionary) {
    this.dictionary = dictionary;
  }

  public static PolyhedronFaceShaderHook bind(ShaderDictionary dictionary) {
    return new PolyhedronFaceShaderHook(dictionary);
  }

  public PolyhedronFaceShaderHook opacity(float alphaMultiplier) {
    this.alphaMultiplier = alphaMultiplier;
    return this;
  }

  public PolyhedronFaceShaderHook rings(int subdivisions) {
    this.subdivisions = Math.max(2, subdivisions);
    return this;
  }

  public void renderFaces(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, List<List<Vec3>> faces, float radius, float timeSeconds) {
    VertexConsumer vc = bufferSource.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    Matrix4f mat = poseStack.last().pose();

    for (List<Vec3> face : faces) {
      int sides = face.size();
      if (3 > sides) continue;

      Vec3 center = Vec3.ZERO;
      for (Vec3 v : face) center = center.add(v);
      center = center.scale(1.0D / sides);

      for (int s = 0; sides > s; s++) {
        Vec3 v0 = face.get(s);
        Vec3 v1 = face.get((s + 1) % sides);

        for (int r = 0; subdivisions > r; r++) {
          float t0 = (float) r / subdivisions;
          float t1 = (float) (r + 1) / subdivisions;

          Vec3 p00 = center.lerp(v0, t0).scale(radius);
          Vec3 p10 = center.lerp(v1, t0).scale(radius);
          Vec3 p11 = center.lerp(v1, t1).scale(radius);
          Vec3 p01 = center.lerp(v0, t1).scale(radius);

          RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, (float) p00.x, (float) p00.y, (float) p00.z, sampleFaceColor(p00, timeSeconds));
          RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, (float) p10.x, (float) p10.y, (float) p10.z, sampleFaceColor(p10, timeSeconds));
          RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, (float) p11.x, (float) p11.y, (float) p11.z, sampleFaceColor(p11, timeSeconds));
          RawGeometry.vertex(vc, mat, (float) p01.x, (float) p01.y, (float) p01.z, sampleFaceColor(p01, timeSeconds));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private int sampleFaceColor(Vec3 p, float timeSeconds) {
    float u = (float) (p.x * 0.55D - p.z * 0.45D);
    float v = (float) (p.y * 0.65D + p.z * 0.35D);
    double sphericalAngle = Math.atan2(p.z, p.x);
    int raw = dictionary.sampleArgb(u, v, sphericalAngle, 0.0f, timeSeconds);
    int scaledAlpha = (int) (FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(raw) * alphaMultiplier);
    return (raw & 0x00FFFFFF) | (scaledAlpha << 24);
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/hooks/PortalShaderHook.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.hooks;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.CompositeDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.ShaderDictionary;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.renderers.SuperellipsePortalRenderer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;

public final class PortalShaderHook {

  private final ShaderDictionary dictionary;
  private float centerX = 0.0f;
  private float centerY = 0.0f;
  private float halfWidth = 1.0f;
  private float halfHeight = 0.65f;
  private int angularSegments = 64;
  private int radialRings = 12;
  private boolean flushAfterRender = false;
  private float centerZ = 0.0f;
  private float halfDepth = 0.0f;

  public PortalShaderHook at(float centerX, float centerY, float centerZ) {
    this.centerX = centerX;
    this.centerY = centerY;
    this.centerZ = centerZ;
    return this;
  }

  public PortalShaderHook depth(float halfDepth) {
    this.halfDepth = halfDepth;
    return this;
  }

  private PortalShaderHook(ShaderDictionary dictionary) {
    this.dictionary = dictionary;
  }

  public static PortalShaderHook bind(ShaderDictionary... dictionaries) {
    ShaderDictionary active = (dictionaries.length == 1) ? dictionaries[0] : new CompositeDictionary(dictionaries);
    return new PortalShaderHook(active);
  }

  public PortalShaderHook at(float centerX, float centerY) {
    this.centerX = centerX;
    this.centerY = centerY;
    return this;
  }

  public PortalShaderHook size(float halfWidth, float halfHeight) {
    this.halfWidth = halfWidth;
    this.halfHeight = halfHeight;
    return this;
  }

  public PortalShaderHook resolution(int angularSegments, int radialRings) {
    this.angularSegments = angularSegments;
    this.radialRings = radialRings;
    return this;
  }

  public PortalShaderHook flushBatch() {
    this.flushAfterRender = true;
    return this;
  }

  public void render(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, float timeSeconds) {
    SuperellipsePortalRenderer.render(
      bufferSource.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID),
      poseStack.last().pose(),
      centerX, centerY, halfWidth, halfHeight,
      timeSeconds, angularSegments, radialRings,
      dictionary
    );
    if (flushAfterRender && bufferSource instanceof MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buffered) {
      buffered.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    }
  }

  public void render3D(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, float timeSeconds) {
    com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.renderers.VolumetricSingularityRenderer.render(
      poseStack,
      bufferSource.getBuffer(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID),
      centerX, centerY, centerZ,
      halfWidth, halfHeight,
      halfDepth > 0.0f ? halfDepth : Math.min(halfWidth, halfHeight) * 0.45f,
      timeSeconds, angularSegments, radialRings,
      dictionary
    );
    if (flushAfterRender && bufferSource instanceof MultiBufferSource.BufferSource buffered) {
      buffered.endBatch(RawGeometry.DIALOGUE_SOLID);
    }
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/renderers/SuperellipsePortalRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.renderers;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.ShaderDictionary;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

public final class SuperellipsePortalRenderer {

  private SuperellipsePortalRenderer() {}

  public static void render(
    VertexConsumer vertexConsumer, Matrix4f poseMatrix,
    float centerX, float centerY, float halfWidth, float halfHeight,
    float timeSeconds, int angularSegments, int radialRings,
    ShaderDictionary dictionary
  ) {
    int segs = Math.max(36, angularSegments);
    int rings = Math.max(6, radialRings);

    for (int segment = 0; segs > segment; segment++) {
      double angle0 = (Math.PI * 2.0D * segment) / segs;
      double angle1 = (Math.PI * 2.0D * (segment + 1)) / segs;

      float tear0 = 1.11f + 0.032f * (float) Math.sin((angle0 * 9.0D) - (timeSeconds * 3.8f));
      float tear1 = 1.11f + 0.032f * (float) Math.sin((angle1 * 9.0D) - (timeSeconds * 3.8f));

      for (int ring = 0; rings > ring; ring++) {
        float tInner = (float) ring / rings;
        float tOuter = (float) (ring + 1) / rings;

        float[] p00 = evalSuperellipse(centerX, centerY, halfWidth, halfHeight, angle0, tInner * tear0, timeSeconds);
        float[] p10 = evalSuperellipse(centerX, centerY, halfWidth, halfHeight, angle1, tInner * tear1, timeSeconds);
        float[] p11 = evalSuperellipse(centerX, centerY, halfWidth, halfHeight, angle1, tOuter * tear1, timeSeconds);
        float[] p01 = evalSuperellipse(centerX, centerY, halfWidth, halfHeight, angle0, tOuter * tear0, timeSeconds);

        RawGeometry.vertex(vertexConsumer, poseMatrix, p00[0], p00[1], 0.0f, dictionary.sampleArgb(p00[0], p00[1], angle0, tInner, timeSeconds));
        RawGeometry.vertex(vertexConsumer, poseMatrix, p10[0], p10[1], 0.0f, dictionary.sampleArgb(p10[0], p10[1], angle1, tInner, timeSeconds));
        RawGeometry.vertex(vertexConsumer, poseMatrix, p11[0], p11[1], 0.0f, dictionary.sampleArgb(p11[0], p11[1], angle1, tOuter, timeSeconds));
        RawGeometry.vertex(vertexConsumer, poseMatrix, p01[0], p01[1], 0.0f, dictionary.sampleArgb(p01[0], p01[1], angle0, tOuter, timeSeconds));
      }
    }
  }

  private static float[] evalSuperellipse(float cx, float cy, float halfW, float halfH, double angle, float scaleMult, float timeSec) {
    float wobble = 1.0f
    + (float) Math.sin((angle * 4.0D) + (timeSec * 1.9f)) * 0.038f
    + (float) Math.cos((angle * 7.0D) - (timeSec * 2.5f)) * 0.022f
    + (float) Math.sin((angle * 11.0D) + (timeSec * 3.2f)) * 0.012f;
    double cos = Math.cos(angle), sin = Math.sin(angle);
    double norm = Math.pow(Math.pow(Math.abs(cos), 4.5D) + Math.pow(Math.abs(sin), 4.5D), 1.0D / 4.5D);
    return new float[]{
      cx + (float) ((cos / norm) * halfW * wobble * scaleMult),
      cy + (float) ((sin / norm) * halfH * wobble * scaleMult)
    };
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/shaders/renderers/VolumetricSingularityRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.renderers;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.shaders.dictionaries.ShaderDictionary;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;

public final class VolumetricSingularityRenderer {

  private VolumetricSingularityRenderer() {}

  public static void render(
    PoseStack ps,
    VertexConsumer vc,
    float centerX,
    float centerY,
    float centerZ,
    float halfWidth,
    float halfHeight,
    float halfDepth,
    float timeSeconds,
    int angularSegments,
    int radialRings,
    ShaderDictionary dictionary
  ) {
    int segs = Math.max(16, angularSegments);
    int rings = Math.max(6, radialRings);

    // 1. Suspended 3D Dark Singularity Core Cube (Pixel Gun 3D inspired rotating dark-matter core)
    float coreSize = Math.min(halfWidth, Math.min(halfHeight, halfDepth)) * 0.68f;
    ps.pushPose();
    ps.translate(centerX, centerY, centerZ);
    ps.mulPose(Axis.YP.rotationDegrees((timeSeconds * 48.0f) % 360.0f));
    ps.mulPose(Axis.XP.rotationDegrees((timeSeconds * 31.0f) % 360.0f));
    ps.mulPose(Axis.ZP.rotationDegrees((timeSeconds * 19.0f) % 360.0f));
    RawGeometry.drawPrism(vc, ps.last().pose(), coreSize, coreSize, coreSize, 0xFF020205);
    ps.popPose();

    // 2. 3D Volumetric Event Horizon & Lensed Accretion Shell (evaluated across 3D superellipse volume)
    Matrix4f m = ps.last().pose();
    float exponent = 0.42f; // Rounded-square Minecraft superellipse profile

    for (int r = 0; rings > r; r++) {
      float t0 = (float) r / rings;
      float t1 = (float) (r + 1) / rings;

      // Dome profile: wraps around the 3D core at t=0 and tapers smoothly to z=centerZ at t=1
      float zDome0 = halfDepth * (float) Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0f, 1.0f - (t0 * t0)));
      float zDome1 = halfDepth * (float) Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0f, 1.0f - (t1 * t1)));

      for (int s = 0; segs > s; s++) {
        double a0 = (s * Math.PI * 2.0D) / segs;
        double a1 = ((s + 1) * Math.PI * 2.0D) / segs;

        float sx0 = superellipseAxis(a0, exponent);
        float sy0 = superellipseAxis(a0 - Math.PI * 0.5D, exponent);
        float sx1 = superellipseAxis(a1, exponent);
        float sy1 = superellipseAxis(a1 - Math.PI * 0.5D, exponent);

        float x00 = centerX + sx0 * halfWidth * t0;
        float y00 = centerY + sy0 * halfHeight * t0;
        float x10 = centerX + sx1 * halfWidth * t0;
        float y10 = centerY + sy1 * halfHeight * t0;

        float x01 = centerX + sx0 * halfWidth * t1;
        float y01 = centerY + sy0 * halfHeight * t1;
        float x11 = centerX + sx1 * halfWidth * t1;
        float y11 = centerY + sy1 * halfHeight * t1;

        int col00 = dictionary.sampleArgb(sx0 * t0, sy0 * t0, a0, t0, timeSeconds);
        int col10 = dictionary.sampleArgb(sx1 * t0, sy1 * t0, a1, t0, timeSeconds);
        int col01 = dictionary.sampleArgb(sx0 * t1, sy0 * t1, a0, t1, timeSeconds);
        int col11 = dictionary.sampleArgb(sx1 * t1, sy1 * t1, a1, t1, timeSeconds);

        // Front 3D Event Horizon Hemisphere (+Z)
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x00, y00, centerZ + zDome0, col00);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x10, y10, centerZ + zDome0, col10);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x11, y11, centerZ + zDome1, col11);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x01, y01, centerZ + zDome1, col01);

        // Back 3D Event Horizon Hemisphere (-Z)
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x10, y10, centerZ - zDome0, col10);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x00, y00, centerZ - zDome0, col00);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x01, y01, centerZ - zDome1, col01);
        RawGeometry.vertex(vc, m, x11, y11, centerZ - zDome1, col11);
      }
    }
  }

  private static float superellipseAxis(double angleRad, float exponent) {
    double c = Math.cos(angleRad);
    return (float) (Math.signum(c) * Math.pow(Math.abs(c), exponent));
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/SmoothPositionState.java <==
/*
 * THIS PART OF THE CODE IS MEANT FOR DEVELOPERS
 * */
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified;

import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;

public class SmoothPositionState {
  public double currentX, currentY, currentZ;
  public float currentYaw;
  private boolean initialized = false;

  public float revealedChars = 0.0f;
  private String lastText = "";
  private int lastTicks = 0;

  public void onTicksSynced(int newTicks) {
    if (newTicks > this.lastTicks) {
      this.revealedChars = 0.0f;
    }
    this.lastTicks = newTicks;
  }

  public void resetTypewriter() {
    this.revealedChars = 0.0f;
  }

  public void update(double targetX, double targetY, double targetZ, float targetYaw, float deltaSeconds, String currentText, float charsPerSecond, SoundEvent blipSound) {
    if (!currentText.equals(this.lastText)) {
      this.lastText = currentText;
      this.revealedChars = 0.0f;
    }

    if (this.revealedChars < currentText.length()) {
      //this.revealedChars = Math.min(currentText.length(), this.revealedChars + (charsPerSecond * deltaSeconds));
      float nextChars = Math.min(currentText.length(), this.revealedChars + (charsPerSecond * deltaSeconds));

      if ((int) nextChars > (int) this.revealedChars && blipSound != null) {
        char revealedChar = currentText.charAt((int) nextChars - 1);
        if (!Character.isWhitespace(revealedChar)) {
          float pitch = 0.95f + (float) (Math.random() * 0.1f);
          net.minecraft.client.Minecraft.getInstance().getSoundManager().play(
            net.minecraft.client.resources.sounds.SimpleSoundInstance.forUI(blipSound, pitch, 0.5f)
          );
        }
      }

      this.revealedChars = nextChars;
    }

    if (!initialized) {
      this.currentX = targetX;
      this.currentY = targetY;
      this.currentZ = targetZ;
      this.currentYaw = targetYaw;
      this.initialized = true;
      return;
    }

    float positionSmoothing = 12.0f;
    float posFactor = 1.0f - (float) Math.exp(-positionSmoothing * deltaSeconds);
    this.currentX += (targetX - this.currentX) * posFactor;
    this.currentY += (targetY - this.currentY) * posFactor;
    this.currentZ += (targetZ - this.currentZ) * posFactor;

    // :TODO: change smoothing through a setter function
    float rotationSmoothing = 5.0f; 
    float rotFactor = 1.0f - (float) Math.exp(-rotationSmoothing * deltaSeconds);

    float yawDelta = targetYaw - this.currentYaw;
    while (yawDelta < -180.0F) yawDelta += 360.0F;
    while (yawDelta >= 180.0F) yawDelta -= 360.0F;

    this.currentYaw += yawDelta * rotFactor;
  }
}


==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/text/TextEffectStyle.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text;

import net.minecraft.util.FastColor;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

import java.util.List;

public record TextEffectStyle(
  List<Integer> colors,
  List<Float> stops,
  float scrollSpeed,
  float waveAmplitude,
  float waveFrequency,
  BackdropMode backdropMode,
  int glowColor,
  int secondaryGlowColor,
  boolean sparkles,
  boolean rainbowStroke,
  boolean hasPrismaticOutline,
  float gradientAngleDeg
) {
  public TextEffectStyle(
    List<Integer> colors, List<Float> stops, float scrollSpeed,
    float waveAmplitude, float waveFrequency, BackdropMode backdropMode,
    int glowColor, int secondaryGlowColor, boolean sparkles
  ) {
    this(colors, stops, scrollSpeed, waveAmplitude, waveFrequency, backdropMode, glowColor, secondaryGlowColor, sparkles, false, false, 0.0f);
  }

  public TextEffectStyle(
    List<Integer> colors, List<Float> stops, float scrollSpeed,
    float waveAmplitude, float waveFrequency, BackdropMode backdropMode,
    int glowColor, int secondaryGlowColor, boolean sparkles, boolean rainbowStroke
  ) {
    this(colors, stops, scrollSpeed, waveAmplitude, waveFrequency, backdropMode, glowColor, secondaryGlowColor, sparkles, rainbowStroke, false, 0.0f);
  }

  public TextEffectStyle(
    List<Integer> colors, List<Float> stops, float scrollSpeed,
    float waveAmplitude, float waveFrequency, BackdropMode backdropMode,
    int glowColor, int secondaryGlowColor, boolean sparkles, boolean rainbowStroke, boolean hasPrismaticOutline
  ) {
    this(colors, stops, scrollSpeed, waveAmplitude, waveFrequency, backdropMode, glowColor, secondaryGlowColor, sparkles, rainbowStroke, hasPrismaticOutline, 0.0f);
  }

  public enum BackdropMode {
    NONE,
    GLOW_BLOOM,
    CHROMATIC_GLITCH,
    PRISMATIC_OUTLINE,
    SPECTRUM_STROKE
  }

  public static TextEffectStyle prismaticOutline() {
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(0xFF0E081C, 0xFF28124D, 0xFF0E081C),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f),
      2.2f, 0.35f, 0.25f,
      BackdropMode.PRISMATIC_OUTLINE, 0xFF00F5FF, 0xFFFF2A6D, true
    );
  }

  public static TextEffectStyle outline(int hexRgb) {
    int base = ensureOpaque(hexRgb);
    int darkCore = shade(base, 0.16f);
    int midCore = shade(base, 0.32f);
    int brightEdge = tintWhite(base, 0.65f);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(darkCore, midCore, darkCore),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f),
      2.0f, 0.35f, 0.25f,
      BackdropMode.PRISMATIC_OUTLINE, base, brightEdge, false
    );
  }
  public static TextEffectStyle outline(String hex) { return outline(parseHex(hex)); }


  /** Iridescent chromatic-aberration glitch shader (default rainbow palette) */
  public static TextEffectStyle glitch() {
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(0xFF38BDF8, 0xFFF472B6, 0xFFA3E635, 0xFFC084FC, 0xFF38BDF8),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.25f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f),
      2.4f, 1.1f, 0.45f,
      BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH, 0x88FF2A6D, 0x8800F5FF, false
    );
  }

  /** Chromatic-aberration glitch shader tinted around a single hexadecimal color (e.g. 0xFF21B7D1 or "#21B7D1") */
  public static TextEffectStyle glitch(int hexRgb) {
    int base = ensureOpaque(hexRgb);
    int dark = shade(base, 0.45f);
    int bright = tintWhite(base, 0.55f);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(dark, base, bright, base, dark),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.3f, 0.5f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
      2.4f, 1.1f, 0.45f,
      BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH, 0x88FF2A6D, 0x8800F5FF, false
    );
  }

  /** Soft breathing back-glow + smooth gradient derived from any single hexadecimal color */
  public static TextEffectStyle aurora(int hexRgb) {
    int base = ensureOpaque(hexRgb);
    int deep = shade(base, 0.55f);
    int highlight = tintWhite(base, 0.65f);
    int primaryGlow = withAlpha(base, 0x77);
    int outerGlow = withAlpha(highlight, 0x3A);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(deep, base, highlight, base, deep),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.3f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f),
      1.4f, 0.45f, 0.3f,
      BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM, primaryGlow, outerGlow, false
    );
  }

  /** Soft back-glow + gradient + occasional twinkling 4-point star glints from any hexadecimal color */
  public static TextEffectStyle starly(int hexRgb) {
    int base = ensureOpaque(hexRgb);
    int deep = shade(base, 0.50f);
    int highlight = tintWhite(base, 0.70f);
    int primaryGlow = withAlpha(base, 0x70);
    int outerGlow = withAlpha(highlight, 0x35);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(deep, base, highlight, base, deep),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.32f, 0.52f, 0.76f, 1.0f),
      1.6f, 0.4f, 0.25f,
      BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM, primaryGlow, outerGlow, true
    );
  }

  public static TextEffectStyle glitch(String hex) { return glitch(parseHex(hex)); }
  public static TextEffectStyle aurora(String hex) { return aurora(parseHex(hex)); }
  public static TextEffectStyle starly(String hex) { return starly(parseHex(hex)); }

  public static int parseHex(String hex) {
    if (hex == null || hex.isEmpty()) return 0xFFFFFFFF;
    String clean = hex.startsWith("#") ? hex.substring(1) : hex;
    if (clean.startsWith("0x") || clean.startsWith("0X")) clean = clean.substring(2);
    long val = Long.parseLong(clean, 16);
    if (clean.length() == 6) val |= 0xFF000000L;
    return (int) val;
  }

  private static int ensureOpaque(int rgb) {
    return ((rgb >>> 24) == 0) ? (0xFF000000 | (rgb & 0x00FFFFFF)) : rgb;
  }

  private static int withAlpha(int argb, int alpha) {
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(alpha & 0xFF, FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb), FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb));
  }

  private static int shade(int argb, float factor) {
    int r = Math.round(FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb) * factor);
    int g = Math.round(FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb) * factor);
    int b = Math.round(FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb) * factor);
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(255, r, g, b);
  }

  private static int tintWhite(int argb, float amount) {
    int r = Math.round(Mth.lerp(amount, FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb), 255.0f));
    int g = Math.round(Mth.lerp(amount, FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb), 255.0f));
    int b = Math.round(Mth.lerp(amount, FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb), 255.0f));
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(255, r, g, b);
  }

  public int sampleColor(float u, float timeSec) {
    return sampleColor(u, 0.5f, timeSec);
  }

  public int sampleColor(float u, float v, float timeSec) {
    if (colors == null || colors.isEmpty()) return 0xFFFFFFFF;
    if (colors.size() == 1) return colors.get(0);

    float coord = u;
    if (Math.abs(gradientAngleDeg) > 0.01f) {
      double rad = Math.toRadians(gradientAngleDeg);
      coord = (float) ((u * Math.cos(rad)) + (v * Math.sin(rad)));
    }

    float shifted = (coord - (timeSec * scrollSpeed * 0.25f)) % 1.0f;
    if (0.0f > shifted) shifted += 1.0f;

    int count = colors.size();
    for (int i = 0; (count - 1) > i; i++) {
      float s0 = (stops != null && stops.size() > i) ? stops.get(i) : (i / (float) (count - 1));
      float s1 = (stops != null && stops.size() > (i + 1)) ? stops.get(i + 1) : ((i + 1) / (float) (count - 1));
      if (shifted >= s0 && s1 >= shifted) {
        float localT = (s1 > s0) ? ((shifted - s0) / (s1 - s0)) : 0.0f;
        return lerpArgb(colors.get(i), colors.get(i + 1), localT);
      }
    }
    return colors.get(count - 1);
  }


  public static int lerpArgb(int c0, int c1, float t) {
    t = Mth.clamp(t, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    int a = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.alpha(c1)));
    int r = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, FastColor.ARGB32.red(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.red(c1)));
    int g = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, FastColor.ARGB32.green(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.green(c1)));
    int b = Math.round(Mth.lerp(t, FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c0), FastColor.ARGB32.blue(c1)));
    return FastColor.ARGB32.color(a, r, g, b);
  }

  /** Two-color Aurora gradient + soft glow (e.g. White + HexCasting Purple) */
  public static TextEffectStyle aurora(int primaryRgb, int secondaryRgb) {
    int c1 = ensureOpaque(primaryRgb);
    int c2 = ensureOpaque(secondaryRgb);
    int primaryGlow = withAlpha(c2, 0x77);
    int outerGlow = withAlpha(c1, 0x3A);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(c1, c2, tintWhite(c2, 0.55f), c2, c1),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.3f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f),
      1.4f, 0.35f, 0.28f,
      BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM, primaryGlow, outerGlow, false
    );
  }

  /** Custom core + animated edge outline (e.g. Gold + White for Raven's Mind) */
  public static TextEffectStyle outline(int coreRgb, int edgeRgb) {
    int core = ensureOpaque(coreRgb);
    int edge = ensureOpaque(edgeRgb);
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(shade(core, 0.75f), core, tintWhite(core, 0.35f), core, shade(core, 0.75f)),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.3f, 0.5f, 0.7f, 1.0f),
      2.0f, 0.3f, 0.25f,
      BackdropMode.PRISMATIC_OUTLINE, core, edge, false
    );
  }

  /** Radiant pearlescent white core surrounded by a fast-cycling full-spectrum rainbow outline */
  public static TextEffectStyle spectrum() {
    return new TextEffectStyle(
      List.of(0xFFFFFFFF, 0xFFFEF9C3, 0xFFE0F2FE, 0xFFFCE7F3, 0xFFFFFFFF),
      List.of(0.0f, 0.25f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f),
      2.6f, 0.45f, 0.30f,
      BackdropMode.SPECTRUM_STROKE, 0xFFFF2A6D, 0xFF00F5FF, true
    );
  }

  public static int sampleSpectrum(float phase) {
    float p = phase % 1.0f;
    if (0.0f > p) p += 1.0f;
    int[] stops = { 0xFFFF2A54, 0xFFFFA500, 0xFFFACC15, 0xFF4ADE80, 0xFF00F5FF, 0xFFC084FC, 0xFFFF2A54 };
    float scaled = p * (stops.length - 1);
    int idx = Math.min(stops.length - 2, (int) scaled);
    return lerpArgb(stops[idx], stops[idx + 1], scaled - idx);
  }

}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/text/ThreeDifiedTextRenderer.java <==
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.text;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.RawGeometry;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Font;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.LightTexture;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Style;

public final class ThreeDifiedTextRenderer {

  private ThreeDifiedTextRenderer() {}

  public static void draw2D(GuiGraphics gui, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style) {
    draw2DInternal(gui, font, text, x, y, style, false);
  }

  public static void draw2DBold(GuiGraphics gui, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style) {
    draw2DInternal(gui, font, text, x, y, style, true);
  }

  public static void draw3D(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style) {
    draw3DInternal(poseStack, bufferSource, font, text, x, y, style, false);
  }

  public static void draw3DBold(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style) {
    draw3DInternal(poseStack, bufferSource, font, text, x, y, style, true);
  }

  private static void draw2DInternal(GuiGraphics gui, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style, boolean bold) {
    if (text == null || text.isEmpty()) return;
    float timeSec = (System.currentTimeMillis() % 600000L) / 1000.0f;
    int totalW = Math.max(1, font.width(bold ? Component.literal(text).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true)) : Component.literal(text)));
    int lineH = font.lineHeight;

    boolean isPrismatic = style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.PRISMATIC_OUTLINE;
    boolean isSpectrum = style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.SPECTRUM_STROKE || style.rainbowStroke();
    boolean hasStroke = isPrismatic || isSpectrum;

    if (style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM) {
      float pulse = 0.85f + 0.15f * (float) Math.sin(timeSec * 4.5f);
      int outerGlow = scaleAlpha(style.secondaryGlowColor(), pulse);
      int innerGlow = scaleAlpha(style.glowColor(), pulse);
      int x0 = Math.round(x);
      int y0 = Math.round(y);
      gui.fill(x0 - 4, y0 - 2, x0 + totalW + 4, y0 + lineH + 2, outerGlow);
      gui.fill(x0 - 2, y0 - 1, x0 + totalW + 2, y0 + lineH + 1, innerGlow);
    }

    float cursorX = x;

    for (int i = 0; text.length() > i; i++) {
      String ch = String.valueOf(text.charAt(i));
      var styledCh = bold ? Component.literal(ch).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true)) : Component.literal(ch);
      int cw = font.width(styledCh);
      float u = ((cursorX - x) + (cw * 0.5f)) / (float) totalW;
      float waveY = (style.waveAmplitude() > 0.0f)
        ? (float) Math.sin((timeSec * 5.0f) + (i * style.waveFrequency())) * style.waveAmplitude()
        : 0.0f;

      int cx = Math.round(cursorX);
      int cy = Math.round(y + waveY);

      if (style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH) {
        float jitterX = (float) Math.sin((timeSec * 23.0f) + (i * 1.7f)) * 1.3f;
        float jitterY = (float) Math.cos((timeSec * 19.0f) + (i * 2.3f)) * 0.9f;
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, Math.round(cursorX + jitterX), Math.round(y + waveY + jitterY), style.glowColor(), false);
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, Math.round(cursorX - jitterX), Math.round(y + waveY - jitterY), style.secondaryGlowColor(), false);
      }

      if (hasStroke) {
        float shift = ((u * 1.4f) - (timeSec * style.scrollSpeed() * 0.35f)) % 1.0f;
        if (0.0f > shift) shift += 1.0f;
        int edgeCol = isSpectrum
          ? TextEffectStyle.sampleSpectrum(shift)
          : TextEffectStyle.lerpArgb(style.glowColor(), style.secondaryGlowColor(), 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(shift * Math.PI * 2.0D));
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, cx - 1, cy, edgeCol, false);
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, cx + 1, cy, edgeCol, false);
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, cx, cy - 1, edgeCol, false);
        gui.drawString(font, styledCh, cx, cy + 1, edgeCol, false);
      }


      int charColor = style.sampleColor(u, timeSec);
      gui.drawString(font, styledCh, cx, cy, charColor, !hasStroke);
      cursorX += cw;
    }

    if (style.sparkles()) {
      for (int s = 0; 4 > s; s++) {
        float phase = ((timeSec * 1.3f) + (s * 0.27f)) % 1.0f;
        float twinkle = (float) Math.sin(phase * Math.PI);
        if (0.15f > twinkle) continue;
        float sx = x + (((s * 37) % 100) / 100.0f) * totalW;
        float sy = y + (((s * 53 + (int) (timeSec * 3.0f)) % 100) / 100.0f) * lineH;
        int sparkCol = scaleAlpha(0xFFFFFFFF, twinkle);
        int ix = Math.round(sx);
        int iy = Math.round(sy);
        gui.fill(ix - 2, iy, ix + 3, iy + 1, sparkCol);
        gui.fill(ix, iy - 2, ix + 1, iy + 3, sparkCol);
      }
    }
  }

  private static void draw3DInternal(PoseStack poseStack, MultiBufferSource bufferSource, Font font, String text, float x, float y, TextEffectStyle style, boolean bold) {
    if (text == null || text.isEmpty()) return;
    float timeSec = (System.currentTimeMillis() % 600000L) / 1000.0f;
    int totalW = Math.max(1, font.width(bold ? Component.literal(text).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true)) : Component.literal(text)));
    int lineH = font.lineHeight;

    // 1. 3D Back-layer Aura / Glow Bloom
    if (style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.GLOW_BLOOM) {
      float pulse = 0.85f + 0.15f * (float) Math.sin(timeSec * 4.5f);
      int outerGlow = scaleAlpha(style.secondaryGlowColor(), pulse);
      int innerGlow = scaleAlpha(style.glowColor(), pulse);
      float cx = x + (totalW * 0.5f);
      float cy = y + (lineH * 0.5f);
      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        cx, cy, -0.04f, totalW + 8.0f, lineH + 4.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
        outerGlow, 0x00000000, java.util.List.of(), false
      );
      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack, bufferSource, font,
        cx, cy, -0.06f, totalW + 4.0f, lineH + 2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
        innerGlow, 0x00000000, java.util.List.of(), false
      );
    }

    float cursorX = x;
    boolean isPrismatic = style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.PRISMATIC_OUTLINE;
    boolean isSpectrum = style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.SPECTRUM_STROKE || style.rainbowStroke();
    boolean hasStroke = isPrismatic || isSpectrum;

    for (int i = 0; text.length() > i; i++) {
      String ch = String.valueOf(text.charAt(i));
      var styledCh = bold ? Component.literal(ch).withStyle(Style.EMPTY.withBold(true)) : Component.literal(ch);
      int cw = font.width(styledCh);
      float u = ((cursorX - x) + (cw * 0.5f)) / (float) totalW;
      float waveY = (style.waveAmplitude() > 0.0f)
        ? (float) Math.sin((timeSec * 5.0f) + (i * style.waveFrequency())) * style.waveAmplitude()
        : 0.0f;

      if (style.backdropMode() == TextEffectStyle.BackdropMode.CHROMATIC_GLITCH) {
        float jx = (float) Math.sin((timeSec * 23.0f) + (i * 1.7f)) * 1.2f;
        float jy = (float) Math.cos((timeSec * 19.0f) + (i * 2.3f)) * 0.8f;
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX + jx, y + waveY + jy, style.glowColor(), false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX - jx, y + waveY - jy, style.secondaryGlowColor(), false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
      }

      if (hasStroke) {
        float shift = ((u * 1.4f) - (timeSec * style.scrollSpeed() * 0.35f)) % 1.0f;
        if (0.0f > shift) shift += 1.0f;
        int edgeCol = isSpectrum
          ? TextEffectStyle.sampleSpectrum(shift)
          : TextEffectStyle.lerpArgb(style.glowColor(), style.secondaryGlowColor(), 0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(shift * Math.PI * 2.0D));
        float off = bold ? 0.9f : 0.75f;
        poseStack.pushPose();
        poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, -0.08f);
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX - off, y + waveY, edgeCol, false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX + off, y + waveY, edgeCol, false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX, y + waveY - off, edgeCol, false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
        font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX, y + waveY + off, edgeCol, false, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
        poseStack.popPose();
      }

      float v = 0.5f + ((i % 2 == 0) ? -0.25f : 0.25f);
      int charColor = style.sampleColor(u, v, timeSec);
      poseStack.pushPose();
      if (hasStroke) poseStack.translate(0.0f, 0.0f, -0.18f);
      font.drawInBatch(styledCh, cursorX, y + waveY, charColor, !hasStroke, poseStack.last().pose(), bufferSource, Font.DisplayMode.NORMAL, 0, LightTexture.FULL_BRIGHT);
      poseStack.popPose();
      cursorX += cw;
    }
  }

  private static int scaleAlpha(int argb, float factor) {
    int a = Math.round(((argb >>> 24) & 0xFF) * factor);
    return net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.color(
      a,
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.red(argb),
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.green(argb),
      net.minecraft.util.FastColor.ARGB32.blue(argb)
    );
  }
}

/*
 * THIS PART OF THE CODE IS MEANT FOR DEVELOPERS
 * */
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.HexaCreateCore;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource;
import net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;

import java.util.List;

// DEFAULT RENDERER
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = HexaCreateCore.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ThreeDifiedRenderer {
  private static List<FormattedCharSequence> lines = null;

  @SubscribeEvent
  public static void onRenderLevelStage(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
    if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT_BLOCKS) return;

    Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
    if (mc.level == null) return;

    PoseStack poseStack = event.getPoseStack();
    MultiBufferSource.BufferSource bufferSource = mc.renderBuffers().bufferSource();

    var camera = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().camera;
    float realTick = mc.getTimer().getGameTimeDeltaTicks();
    float partialTick = mc.getTimer().getRealtimeDeltaTicks() / 20.0f;
        net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 camPos = camera.getPosition();
    net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 eyePos = mc.player.getEyePosition(realTick);
    float rayOriginX = (float) (eyePos.x - camPos.x);
    float rayOriginY = (float) (eyePos.y - camPos.y);
    float rayOriginZ = (float) (eyePos.z - camPos.z);

    net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 playerView = mc.player.getViewVector(realTick);
    org.joml.Vector3f lookDir = new org.joml.Vector3f(
        (float) playerView.x,
        (float) playerView.y,
        (float) playerView.z
    );

    //org.joml.Vector3f lookDir = camera.getLookVector();

    for (net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity entity : mc.level.entitiesForRendering()) {
      if (!(entity instanceof ThreeDified dialogueEntity)) continue;
      if (dialogueEntity.hasCustomRenderer()) continue;

      DialoguePlaybackState playback = dialogueEntity.getDialoguePlayback();
      boolean isShowing = dialogueEntity.shouldShowDialogue();
      playback.setShowing(isShowing);
      if (!isShowing || playback.isForceClosed()) continue;

      DialogueTransformState transform = dialogueEntity.getDialogueTransform();
      transform.update(
        entity,
        realTick,
        1.0D,
        mc.player.getEyePosition(realTick),
        camera.getPosition(),
        camera.getYRot(),
        partialTick
      );
      playback.updateFromEntity(mc.font, entity, partialTick);


      poseStack.pushPose();
      poseStack.translate(transform.renderX, transform.renderY, transform.renderZ);

      poseStack.mulPose(com.mojang.math.Axis.YP.rotationDegrees(-transform.currentYaw - 180.0F));
      poseStack.mulPose(com.mojang.math.Axis.XP.rotationDegrees(-camera.getXRot()));

      poseStack.scale(0.015F, -0.015F, 0.015F);

      RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
        poseStack,
        bufferSource,
        mc.font,
        0.0f,
        0.0f,
        0.0f,
        playback.boxWidth,
        playback.boxHeight,
        4.0f,
        4.0f,
        playback.currentBgColor,
        playback.currentBorderColor,
        playback,
        true,
        false
      );

      int newHoveredIndex = -1;
      float currentY = (playback.boxHeight * 0.5f) + 8.0f;
      for (int i = 0; playback.options.size() > i; i++) {
        DialogueNode button = playback.options.get(i);
        float buttonCenterY = currentY + (button.height * 0.5f);

        // EXAMPLE IF YOU WANT TO USE .isLookingAtNode() instead
        //boolean isHovered = DialogueRaycaster.isLookingAtNode(
        //  poseStack.last().pose(),
        //  lookDir,
        //  0.0f, buttonCenterY, 2.0f,
        //  button.baseWidth, button.baseHeight,
        //  5.0D
        //);

        boolean isHovered = DialogueRaycaster.isLookingAtNode(
          poseStack.last().pose(),
          rayOriginX, rayOriginY, rayOriginZ,
          lookDir,
          0.0f, buttonCenterY, 2.0f,
          button.baseWidth, button.baseHeight,
          5.0D
        );

        if (isHovered) {
          newHoveredIndex = i;
        }

        boolean isHeld = isHovered && (mc.options.keyAttack.isDown() || mc.options.keyUse.isDown());
        button.updatePressScale(isHeld, 0.85f, partialTick);

        float buttonZ = isHeld ? 1.0f : (isHovered ? 5.0f : 2.0f);
        int borderColor = isHovered ? button.hoverBorderColor : button.borderColor;

        RawGeometry.drawDialogueNode(
          poseStack, bufferSource, mc.font,
          0.0f, buttonCenterY, buttonZ,
          button.width, button.height,
          3.0f, 4.0f,
          button.bgColor, borderColor,
          button.lines, true
        );

        playback.hoveredOptionIndex = newHoveredIndex;
        currentY += button.height + 10.0f;
      }

      poseStack.popPose();
    }
  }
}

==> src/main/java/com/genesiz/hexacreatecore/threedified/ThreeDifiedVec3.java <==
/*
 * THIS PART OF THE CODE IS MEANT FOR DEVELOPERS
 * */
package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified;

import com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.decorators.annotations.Overload;

import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;

public record ThreeDifiedVec3<T extends Entity &ThreeDified>(float partialTick, T entity, double orbit , net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 facing, net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 camera, SmoothPositionState state) {

  @Overload
  public ThreeDifiedVec3(float partialTick, T entity, net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 facing, net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 camera, SmoothPositionState state) {
    this(partialTick, entity, 0.0, facing, camera, state);
  }

  @Overload
  public ThreeDifiedVec3(float partialTick, T entity, net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 facing, net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 camera) {
    this(partialTick, entity, 0.0, facing, camera, null);
  }

  public double entPosX(){
    return net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(partialTick, entity.xo, entity.getX());
  }
  public double entPosY(){
    return net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(partialTick, entity.yo, entity.getY());
  }
  public double entPosZ(){
    return net.minecraft.util.Mth.lerp(partialTick, entity.zo, entity.getZ());
  }

  private double horDist() {
    double dx = facing.x - entPosX();
    double dz = facing.z - entPosZ();
    return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
  }

  public double targetX() {
    if (horDist() > 0.001D) return entPosX() + ((facing.x - entPosX()) / horDist()) * orbit;
    return entPosX();}

  public double targetZ() {
    if (horDist() > 0.001D) return entPosZ() + ((facing.z - entPosZ()) / horDist()) * orbit;
    return entPosZ();
  }

  public double targetY() {
    return entPosY() + entity.getBbHeight() + 0.3D;
  }

  public double renderX() {
    return (state != null ? state.currentX : targetX()) - camera.x;
  }

  public double renderY() {
    return (state != null ? state.currentY : targetY()) - camera.y;
  }

  public double renderZ() {
    return (state != null ? state.currentZ : targetZ()) - camera.z;
  }
}

package com.genesiz.hexacreatecore.threedified.threedishapes_factory;

public enum ShapeAnchor {
  CENTER(0.0f, 0.0f),
  TOP_CENTER(0.0f, -0.5f),
  BOTTOM_CENTER(0.0f, 0.5f),
  LEFT_CENTER(-0.5f, 0.0f),
  RIGHT_CENTER(0.5f, 0.0f),
  TOP_LEFT(-0.5f, -0.5f),
  TOP_RIGHT(0.5f, -0.5f),
  BOTTOM_LEFT(-0.5f, 0.5f),
  BOTTOM_RIGHT(0.5f, 0.5f),
  ALL_CORNERS(0.0f, 0.0f),
  LEFT_AND_RIGHT(0.0f, 0.0f);

  public final float normX;
  public final float normY;

  ShapeAnchor(float normX, float normY) {
    this.normX = normX;
    this.normY = normY;
  }
}
